patippuri
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolinōkkup pārviyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum cērttu avaruṭiya maṟivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭurikaḷi ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭurip putiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷi naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamiyuṭivar eṉṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñari iṉṉum peṟṟut taravilli eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturiyarum, kuṟimatiyar tēkkivitta kariyiruḷi nīkka vanta maṟimaliyār vaḻivanta niṟimaliyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravittalivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷi mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam urippaṉa. tamiḻi āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyi mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟi ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamiyākum.
pāvāṇarit tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟiyum, citikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putiyalkaḷi tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyi mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷi ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tanti periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷki
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.
vāṉmaḻi vaḷaccurappu
āḻattuḷ āḻamāy akaḻntu taṅkam ēṉ eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu? āḻkaṭaluḷ ceṉṟu, uyirip paṇayam vittu muttuk kuḷikkap paṭuvatu ēṉ? pavaḻap pāḷaṅkaḷi eṭuppatu ēṉ? vayiram mutalām maṇikkulaṅkaḷi iṭiyaṟāmal tēṭit tēṭi eṭuppatu ēṉ?
aṇikalap poruḷkaḷ eṉpataṟku maṭṭumā? vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭṭukkum īṭṭutaṟku ariya vippu vaḷamāka iruntu, nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal mēmpāṭṭukku aṭimaṇi
yākavum ivi iruppatāl tāṉē!
ivaṟṟiṉum mēlām vippu vaḷamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu! uṇṭu! avi cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ!
kālam kālamāka aḷḷik koṇṭālum vaṟṟā vaḷamāyt tikaḻum aṟivuk karuvūlamām nūlkaḷ!
oṉṟik kaṟṟāl oru nūṟāy ōrāyiramāyp palkip perukat takka peṟaṟkariya tāy nūlkaḷām pēṟaminta nūlkaḷ! cēynūlkaḷi īṉavalla ceḻu nūlkaḷ!
nuṇ māṇ nuḻipulat tāy nūlkaḷāy iruppiṉum! avaṟṟit tāṅkiya tāḷkaḷ, ettakiya pēṇutal uṭiyavi eṉiṉum kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyak kūṭiyavi tāmē!
kalleḻuttē, katir veppālum kaṭaluppālum kāttup pēṇum kaṭappāṭillār kippaṭalālum aḻintu paṭṭami kaṇkūṭu tāṉē!
ēṭṭu varivukaḷ, nīrē neruppē nīṭitta kālamē puṟṟē pōṟṟā maṭamiyē eṉpavaṟṟāl aḻintu paṭṭamikku aḷavu tāṉum uṇṭō? makkaḷukku māṇap periya payaṉām nūlkaḷ, kaṟkum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum, piṟantu perukivarum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum takka vakiyil avarkaḷukkup pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyar vitta paḻantēṭṭeṉṉa annūlkaḷ, mīḷamīḷat taṭṭillāmal kiṭikkac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu nūṟpatippar tavirāk kaṭamiyallavō!
akkaṭamiyi avarkaḷ kālantōṟum kaṭappāṭākak koṇṭu ceyya, āḷum aracum vāḻum aṟivarum arunnuṇiyātal tāṉē kaṭṭāyat tēvi! ivvāṟu patipparum aṟivarum āḷnarum tattam mūccuk kaṭaṉāka, nūṟkoṭi puritalukku mēṟkoṭi illiyām!
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ oṉṟā iraṇṭā? namakku eṭṭātavi pōka eṭṭiya nūlkaḷ 32. kaṭṭuri poḻivu mutalām vakiyāl kiṭṭiya tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ 12. iṉṉum eṭṭāta tokuppum aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟum vara vāyppuṇṭu!
kaṭita varivu pā varivu eṉa mēlum perukavum vāyppuṇṭu!
ivaṟṟi ellām āyiram acciṭṭu avvaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭāl, tēṭuvārkkut tēṭum poḻutil vāykkum tiruvākat tikaḻak kūṭumō?
ātalāl, cēy nūlkaḷukku mūlamām tāynūlkaḷ, kālam tōṟum vāṉmaḻi vaḷac curappāka veḷippaṭa vēṇṭum kaṭṭāyam uṇarntu kaṭami puriyum vīṟuṭiyār vēṇṭum! mika vēṇṭum!
ittakiya viḻumiya vīṟuṭiyar - iṉamāṉac cemmal - tamiḻp pōrāḷi - tiruttaku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār, tām muntu muḻutuṟak koṇarnta pāvāṇar paṭippukaḷi mīḷamīḷat tamiḻulāk koḷḷa vikkum muṉ muyaṟciyāy, im mīḷpatippi vaḻaṅkukiṟār! tamiḻulakam payaṉ koḷvatāka! payaṉ ceyvatāka!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.

centamiḻc ciṟappu
1
matippaṭic coṟkaḷ
makkaḷ peyarukkumuṉ matippukkuṟittuc  cērkkum aṭic coṟkaḷ kīḻvarumāṟu:
peyar	aṭiccol
maṇamākāta iḷiñaṉ peyarukkumuṉ...	kumaraṉ (Master)
maṇamākāta iḷiñi peyarukku muṉ...	kumari (Miss)
iḷanti (Youth) kaṭanta āṭavaṉpeyarukkumuṉ...	tiruvāḷaṉ (Mr.)
iḷanti kaṭanta peṇṭiṉ peyarukku muṉ...	tiruvāṭṭi (Mrs.)
kaṇṇiyam vāynta āṭavaṉpeyarukkumuṉ...bgUkh‹
kaṇṇiyam vāynta peṇṭiṉ peyarukku muṉ... 	perumāṭṭi  
ṉakarameyyum ḷakarameyyum ikaravuyirum iṟutiyiṟ koṇṭa orumi yīṟukaḷ ulaka vaḻakkil uyarvu kuṟiyāmiyiṉ, kalvi celvam patavi aṟivu, mūppu mutaliyavaṟṟāl uyarvu peṟṟavar peyariyum, avar peyarukku muṉvarum aṭiccolliyum, uyarvuppaṉmi vaṭivilēyē kuṟittal vēṇṭum.
orumi	uyarvup paṉmi	paṉmi
aḻakaṉ	aḻakaṉār	aḻakar, aḻakaṉmār
tanti	tantiyār	tantiyar, (tantimār)
appaṉ	appaṉār	appaṉmār
takappaṉ	takappaṉār	takappaṉmār
ammi	ammiyār	ammiyar, ammimār
tāy	tāyār	tāyar,tāymār
iḷiñaṉ	iḷiñaṉār	iḷiñar
kumaraṉ	kumaraṉār	kumarar, kumaraṉmār
iḷiñi	iḷiñiyār	iḷiñiyar
kumari	kumariyār	kumariyar, kumarimār
āṭavaṉ	āṭavaṉār	āṭavar, āṭavaṉmār
tiruvāḷaṉ	tiruvāḷar	tiruvāḷar, tiruvāḷaṉmār
	tiruvāḷaṉār}	
tiruvāṭṭi	tiruvāṭṭiyār	tiruvāṭṭimār
peṇṭu	peṇṭār	peṇṭir(uyarvu)
		peṇṭukaḷ(uyarviṉmi)
perumāṉ	perumāṉār	perumāṉar, perumāṉmār
perumāṭṭi	perumāṭṭiyār	perumāṭṭiyar,
			perumāṭṭimār
tuṟavi	tuṟaviyār	tuṟaviyar
aṭikaḷ	aṭikaḷ,	aṭikaṇmār
	aṭikaḷār
iḷantiyar eṉpatu irupāṟ potuppaṉmip peyar (Young men or women or both) iḷanti = iḷami
makarameyyīṟṟu iyaṟpeyari (Proper name) uyarvup paṉmiyiṟ kuṟittal  vēṇṭiṉ, ṉakara meyyīṟṟup peyarāka māṟṟikkoḷḷalām.
e-ṭu:
peyar	uyarvup paṉmi	paṉmi
celvam - celvaṉ	celvaṉār	celvar, celvaṉmār
mēṟkāṭṭiya eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu potuvāka ar īṟu paṉmiyiyum ār īṟu uyarvup paṉmiyiyum uṇarttum eṉa aṟintu koḷka.
kumaraṉ eṉṉum teṉcol kum eṉṉum aṭippiṟantu, kūṭṭattiṟkut takuntavaṉ allatu tiraṇṭavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil iḷiñaṉiyē kuṟittatu. itaṉ peṇpāl vaṭivāṉa kumari eṉṉum collum itē poruḷil iḷiñiyik kuṟittatu.
kum - kummutal = kūṭutal, tiraḷutal. kum - kummal = kuviyal.
kum - kumi - kuvi - kuvi, kuvāl, kuvivu, kuvavu.
kumi - kumiyal - kuviyal, kummiruṭṭu = tiṇinta kāriruḷ.
kuvavuttōḷ = tiraṇṭatōḷ.
kum - kumpu - kumpal = kūṭṭam, kumputal = kūṭutal.
kumpu = kūṭṭam.
kum - kumar = tiraṇṭa iḷami, iḷami kaṉṉimi aḻiyāttaṉmi,
kumar -kumaraṉ, kumari.
kuṟiñcinilat teyvamāṉa murukaṉum pālinilat teyvamāṉa kāḷiyum, eṉṟum iḷamiyar eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṟṟiyē muṟiyē kumaraṉ, kumari eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kāḷiyiṉ peyarālēyē, mūḻkip pōṉa paḻam pāṇṭināṭṭut teṉkōṭi maliyum vaṭakōṭi yāṟum kumariyeṉap peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. kumarimaliyiṉ peyarālēyē, mūḻkip pōṉa teṉ kaṇṭattik kumarikkaṇṭam eṉkiṉṟōm. kumarimali yirunta kālam, āriyar eṉṟa iṉamē tōṉṟāta toṉmutu paṇṭik kālamākum.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tamiḻaṉ toṉmiyi muṟṟum maṟittut tamiḻi vaṭamoḻiyiṉ vaḻiyatākak kāṭṭal vēṇṭi, piṉvarumāṟu kumaraṉ, kumari yeṉṉum iru coṟkaḷiṉ vaṭiviyum poruḷiyum tirittum mikuttum uḷḷaṉar.
kumaraṉ - kumāra = kuḻanti, piyaṉ, iḷiñaṉ, makaṉ (irukku vētam)
kumari - kumārī = ciṟumi, pattiliruntu paṉṉīrakavip paṭṭavaḷ, iḷiñi, makaḷ (atarvavētam)
iv viru coṟkaḷiyum āriyac collākak kāṭṭumāṟu mūlattiyum kīḻvarumāṟu tirittuḷḷaṉar.
ku + māra = eḷitāka iṟappatu.
ip pakuppum coṟporuṭ karaṇiyamum iyaṟkikku māṟākavum uttikkup poruntāmalum iruppatiyum, makaṉ, makaḷ eṉṉum poruḷ tamiḻiliṉmiyiyum nōkkuka, iṉṟum. ` intac cumiyit tūkka muṭiyāta nī oru kumaraṉā?’ eṉṟu ōr iḷiñaṉi nōkki makkaḷ viṉavuvati yum,kōṭic cēlikku oru veḷḷi, kumarippeṇṇukku oru piḷḷi eṉṉum paḻamoḻi vaḻakkiyum, ūṉṟi nōkki uṇmiyi aṟika.
kumaraṉ kumari eṉṉum coṟkaḷi vaṭacolleṉṟu nīkkiviṭiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kūṟṟi ottukkoṇṭatākavum, kumarimali mūḻkiyatu  āriya varukikkup piṟpaṭṭatākavumē muṭital kāṇka.
tiru eṉṉum colliṉ pala poruḷkaḷuḷ, teyvattaṉmi eṉpatum oṉṟu.
e-ṭu:	tirukkaṇṇappar, tirukkuṟaḷ, tiruvaraṅkam, tiruviḻā,  tirunīṟu, tirumaṇam mutaliya coṟkaḷil tiru eṉpatu teyvattaṉmik karuttōṭu tūymik karuttiyum uṇarttum.
matippāṉa makkaṭṭaṉmiyik kuṟikkum tiruvāḷaṉ eṉṉum aṭiccol, tiru. eṉṟu kuṟuki niṟkumpōtu muṟṟuppuḷḷi peṟa vēṇṭum. allākkāl, teyvattaṉmi yuṇarttum tiru eṉṉum collōṭoppak koṇṭu mayaṅka nērum.
e-ṭu:	tirunāvukkaracu (iṟiyaṭiyār peyar).
	tiru. nāvukkaracu (potumakaṉ peyar).
iṟiyaṭiyār peyarē. potumakaṉ peyarāyiṉ appaṭiyē yirukkalām. puḷḷi vēṇṭiyatilli, tuṟaviyār peyarukku muṉ tavattiru eṉpatiyum, tamiḻttoṇṭar peyarukku muṉ tamiḻttiru eṉpatiyum, maṟiyoḻukkattiṉar peyarukkumuṉ maṟittiru eṉpati yum aṭiccollāka āḷalām.
e-ṭu :	tavattirukkuṉṟakkuṭiyaṭikaḷ,
	tamiḻttiru maṟimali yaṭikaḷ,
	maṟittiru maṇiyam avarkaḷ.
srī la srī eṉṉum civamaṭa vaḻakkit tiruttavattiru allatu peruntavattiru eṉṟu kuṟikkalām.
tirumati eṉṉum aṭiccol, tiru. eṉṉum teṉcolliyum mati eṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyīṟṟut tiripiyuṅ koṇṭa irupiṟappi (Hybrid) ātalāl, ati aṟavē vilakkal vēṇṭum.
tirumakaṉ-tirumāḷ- srīmat (va.) - srīmatī (peṇpāl)
tirumati-  srīmatī, tiruvāṭṭi eṉṉum tūya teṉcolli nīkkiviṭṭut tirumati eṉṉum irupiṟappiyi āḷvatu,
pētimi yeṉpatoṉ ṟiyāteṉiṉ ētaṅkoṇ
ṭūtiyam pōka viṭal	(kuṟaḷ. 831 )
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaṭkē eṭuttukkāṭṭām. orukāl, tirumati eṉpataṉ īṟṟi aṟivup pulaṉik kuṟikkum teṉcollākak karutikkoṇṭaṉar pōlum! aṟivut tiṟaṉik kuṟikkum mati eṉṉum teṉcol vēṟu; peṇpāluṇarttum matī eṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyīṟu vēṟu.
iṉi matippaṭic coṟkaḷ, (1) muṉṉaṭic coṟkaḷ, (2) piṉ ṉaṭic coṟkaḷ eṉa iru vakiya.
kaṇṇiyam vāyntavar peyarukkup piṉ, avarkaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippatu piṉṉaṭic collākum. atu, uyarvuppaṉmi. ataṟku orumiyum paṉmiyum illi.
e-ṭu: tiru. māṇikkavēl ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷ.
āṟṟalum tērvuppaṭṭamum
pulamiyum toḻilum kuṟittu varum coṟkaḷ muṉṉaṭiyāka variṉ vēṟaṭi tēviyilli.
e-ṭu:	paṇṭitamaṇi katirēcaṉ ceṭṭiyār,
	pulavar ciṉṉāṇṭār,
	pērāciriyar cokkappaṉār,
	maruttuvar kaṇṇappar.
	nāvalar vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭār,
	putuppuṉivar kō. turiccāmi nāyakkar (G.K. nāyuṭu),
	paṇṭārakar (Dr) cāli iḷan tiriyaṉār.
pērāciriyar eṉpatip pērā. eṉṟu kuṟukkalām. cila coṟkaḷ muṉṉaṭiyākavum piṉṉaṭiyākavum āḷap peṟum.
e-ṭu : 	pulavar pukaḻēntiyār,	pukaḻēntip pulavar,
	periyār ī.vē.irā.	ī.vē.irāp. periyār.
‘avarkaḷ’ eṉṉum piṉṉaṭiyi evar peyarukkum piṉ kuṟikka lām. atu eḻutuvāriṉ nilimiyiyum viruppattiyum poṟuttatu.
araciṉar vaḻaṅkum paṭṭaṅkaḷāṉa aṭiccoṟkaḷip piṉ varumāṟu moḻipeyarkkalām.
e-ṭu:	patmasrī: tāmarittiru va. cuppiyāp piḷḷi avarkaḷ.
	cer (Sir) : vayavar tiyākarācac ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷ
	rājā cer (Rajah Sir): aracavayavar muttiyāc ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷ.
	rāy pahatūr: ariya āṇṭaki.
	rāv pahatūr: arāva āṇṭaki pavāṉantam piḷḷi avarkaḷ.
	tivāṉ pahatūr: amicca āṇṭaki nārāyaṇacāmip piḷḷi avarkaḷ.
	rāv cāhip: arāva aṇṇal kōtaṇṭapāṇip piḷḷi avarkaḷ.
araciyaṟ patavikaḷ paṟṟiya muṉṉaṭikaḷ

- ``mutaṉmoḻi'' 2.9.1971
2
tamiḻiṉ taṉipperun taṉmikaḷ
mūvāyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa ulaka moḻikaḷuḷ, iṉṟum palvēṟu vakiyil mutaṉmiyākavuḷḷatu, poṉṉiṉum maṇiyiṉuñ ciṟantatāka vum, uṇaviṉum maruntiṉum iṉṟiyamiyātatākavum, teyvamum tirumaṟiyumeṉak kaṇṇiyamākavum nam muṉṉōr pōṟṟi vaḷartta centamiḻē yeṉpatu, mukaṭēṟi niṉṟu muracaṟintu viḷampattakka muḻu vuṇmiyākum. ataṟkētuvāṉa tamiḻiṉ taṉipperun taṉmikaḷ varumāṟu:
1. muṉmi
iñ ñālattil mikap paḻimiyāṉa nilappakuti yeṉṟu tamiḻ varalāṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭṭatum, nilanūl, kaṭalnūl, uyirnūl ārāycci yāḷarāl uyttuṇarappaṭṭatum, māntaṉ piṟantakameṉṟu māntaṉūlārāṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatum, teṟkē intu māvāriyil mūḻkip pōṉa kumarikkaṇṭam (Lemuria) ākum. 
``paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa'' 	(cilap. 11 : 19-20)
eṉṟu āyiratteṇṇūṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉpē, muṟṟat tuṟanta muḻu muṉivarum naṭunilikkut taliciṟantavarum palkalip perumpulavaru mākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟiyiruppatu, kaṭukaḷavuṅ kaṭṭuc ceytiyaṉṟu. at toṉṉilattil ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyākat tōṉṟiyataṉālēyē.
``ōṅka liṭivan tuyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷakaṟṟum - āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatiroṉ ṟēṉiyatu
taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ''	(taṇṭiyalaṅkāra vurimēṟkoḷ)
eṉṟu paṇṭic cāṉṟōrāl tamiḻ ciṟappittup pāṭappaṭṭatu meṉka.
iṉi, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, pūraṇaliṅkam piḷḷi, kā. cuppiramaṇiyap piḷḷi, tuṭicikiḻār mutaliya pēraṟiñar kumarināṭṭut tamiḻt tōṟṟattippaṟṟit teḷivākak kūṟiyiruppavum, eṉ ari nūṟṟāṇṭu moḻiyārāycciyāl atu uṟutippaṭuttappaṭṭirukkavum, atu kaṭṭuk katiyeṉṟu kūṟivarum viyāpurikaḷum ārāycciyilikaḷum,
``ulakattār uṇṭeṉpa tilleṉpāṉ viyat
talakiyā vikkap paṭum''	(kuṟaḷ. 850)
eṉṉum kuṟaṭkē ciṟanta ilakkiyamāvar. mēlum, palkalit teḷivum naṭunili piṟaḻā vāymiyuṅ koṇṭa taliciṟanta uriyāciriyaruḷ oruvarāṉa aṭiyārkkunallār, tam cilappatikāra vēṉiṟ kāti yurit toṭakkattil, ``akkālattu avar nāṭṭut teṉpāli mukattiṟku vaṭavelliyākiya paḵṟuḷi yeṉṉu māṟṟiṟkum kumari yeṉṉu māṟṟiṟku miṭiyē eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatavāṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ nīrmali vāṉeṉa malinta ēḻteṅka nāṭum, ēḻ maturi nāṭum, ēḻmuṉpāli nāṭum, ēḻpiṉ pāli nāṭum, ēḻkuṉṟa nāṭum, ēḻkuṇakāri nāṭum, ēḻkuṟumpaṉi nāṭum eṉṉum inta nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭum, kumari kollam mutaliya paṉmali nāṭum kāṭum natiyum patiyum taṭanīrk kumari vaṭaperuṅ kōṭṭiṉkāṟum kaṭal koṇṭoḻitalāl, kumariyākiya puva meṉṟār eṉṟuṇarka.'' eṉṟu teṟkē īrāyiraṅ kal tolivu nīṇṭirunta paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭṭip palvēṟu ciṟu nāṭukaḷākap pakuttuk kūṟiyiruppatu, paḻiya ēṭṭiṉiṉṟu peyartteḻutiya marapuvaḻi varalāṟṟuc ceytiyākavaṉṟi, putuvatu puṉintatākavō cēranāṭṭup paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟip piṟaḻavuṇarnta tākavō irukka muṭiyāteṉṟu kūṟiviṭukka.
2. meṉmi
tamiḻar māntaṉ varalāṟṟiṟ kuḻantipōl muntit tōṉṟiya iṉattavarātalāl, avar vāyil kuḻantikaḷum mutiyavarum kaḷitta varum nōyāḷikaḷumkūṭa eḷitāy olikkat takkaṉavum, perumpālum ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉavumāṉa (uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭumāka) muppatu eḻuttolikaḷē piṟantiruntaṉa.  atōṭu, taṉi meyyoliyil toṭaṅkum coṟkaḷum, valliṉa meyyoliyil iṟuñ coṟkaḷum, cila meyyolikaṭkup piṉ cila meyyolikaḷ tamittō uyirmeyyākavō  iṭiyil varuñ coṟkaḷum, avar vāyil vantatilli. ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇa miyampal eṉṉum muṟiyil, akkālattu variyaṟukkappaṭṭa mutaliṭi kaṭi yeḻuttu varampukaḷē, ikkālat tilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum ēṟṟuk kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻiṉiṉṟē tirinta tiraviṭam uṭpaṭap piṟamoḻikaḷil ittakiya varampīṭilli ataṉāl, 
``eṇpeyar muṟipiṟap puruvam māttiri
mutalī ṟiṭinili pōli yeṉṟā
patampuṇarp peṉappaṉ ṉirupāṟ ṟatuvē''	(57)
eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāviṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa eḻuttilakkaṇam paṉṉiraṇṭaṉuḷ, `mutalī ṟiṭinili'' eṉṉum mūṉṟum tamiḻukkē ciṟappāka  vuriyaṉavām. piṟa moḻikaḷil enta eḻuttum enta iṭattilum varalām . tamiḻil aṅṅaṉam varamuṭiyātu.
iṉi, vaṭamoḻi valliṉa meykaḷiṉ nālvakiyuḷ, urappiyum eṭuttum kaṉittum olikkum piṉ mūṉṟu vakikaḷēyaṉṟi, iyalpāṉa tākak karutappaṭum mutal vakiyum, tamiḻ valliṉattōṭu ovvātu irumaṭi valittolikkum kaṭuvalliṉamām. kākattik kuṟikkum `kaka' eṉum vaṭacol vaṭivam. tamiḻil `kākka' eṉṟu kakaram iraṭṭittu eḻutap paṭṭāltāṉ, orumaruṅku vaṭamoḻiyi ottolikka muṭiyum. `mēkkar' (maker) eṉṉum āṅkilac collum ittakiyatē. iv vēṟupāṭṭi, eḻuttolikaḷi nuṇṇitākak kaṇṭavarpōl tammik kāṭṭik koḷḷum mēli vaṇṇaṉimoḻi nūlārum iṉṉum aṟiyavilli.
meliyum valiyumāka aṭuttuvarum ṅka, ñca, ṇṭa, nta, mpa, ṉṟa eṉṉum iṇimeykaḷ, āriyattilum tiraviṭattilum pōla `ṅkka', `ñcca',` ṇṭṭa',` ntta', `mppa', `ṉṟṟa' eṉṟu eṉṟēṉum tamiḻil valittolippatilli.
mūccoli (Aspirate) tamiḻil illavēyilli. eṭuppoli (Voice Sound) meliyaṭutta valikkē yuṇṭu.
colliṭiyil nikaḻum uṭaṉili meymmayakkam, vēṟṟunili meymmayakkam eṉṉum iruvaki meyc cērkkiyuḷ, muṉṉatil iraṇṭāvatu mey `cukku'. `paṭṭu' eṉpavaṟṟiṟpōl uyirōṭu kūṭiyatākavē yirukkum; piṉṉatil iraṇṭāvatu mey kāyppu. vāḻkki eṉpavaṟṟiṟ pōl  taṉimeyyākavō, `veṭkam, `payiṟci'  eṉpavaṟṟiṟ pōl uyirōṭu kūṭiya meyyākavō irukkum. āyiṉ, iru taṉi meykaḷē yaṉṟi, `sāntv, `camaṟkruta' eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷilum, camps, tempts eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟkaḷilum pōl, muttaṉi meykaḷum nāṟṟaṉi meykaḷum tamiḻil varavē varā.
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻ perumpālum mellōci moḻiyāyiruppata ṉālēyē, atu ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyāyt tōṉṟiyum iṟantu paṭāmal iṉṉum iḷami niliyil iruntu varukiṉṟatu. atit tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭavarum muyaṟci varuttamiṉṟi eḷitākap pēci varukiṉṟaṉar. kālañceṉṟa pā.vē. māṇikka nāyakar, oru muṟi ulakap perumoḻi kaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟilum av vāyiram coṟkaḷi yeṭuttu, avaṟṟiṟkuc celaviṭum mūcci mūccumāṉi koṇṭu aḷantu pārttatil, camaṟkirutat tiṟkē mikuntum tamiḻukku mikak kuṟintum iruppatākak kaṇṭār. iv vuṇmiyiyē, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, krutam, truṣṭi, tvaritam, cruṣṭi, hrutaya eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷōṭu, iḻutu, pārvi, virivu, paṭippu, neñcam eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷi oppunōkki eḷitāka viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉār.
oru moḻikku vēṇṭiyatu col vaḷamē yaṉṟi olivaḷamaṉṟu. āriyam, cēmiyam mutaliya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ tōṉṟāta at tol paṇṭik kālattilum, ekkālattum māntaṉ uḷḷattil eḻakkūṭiya karuttukaḷi yellān terivikkat takka coṟkaḷiyum  colluṟuppu kaḷiyum, nam kumarināṭṭu muṉṉōr paṭittu vittuḷḷaṉar. ātalāl. olimeṉmiyāl tamiḻukku uyarvēyaṉṟi iḻivilli eṉṟum, atip pēcuvārkku mūccu varuttamum pēccu varuttamu miṉmiyāl  vāḻnāḷ nīṭikkumeṉṟum, aṟintukoḷka.
3. tāymi
peṟṟōrik kuṟikkum ammi appaṉ eṉṉum kumarināṭṭut tamiḻccoṟkaḷ, āriyam uṭpaṭa ulakap perumoḻikaḷ palavaṟṟil vaṭivu tirintu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
nūṉ, nūm eṉṉum paṇṭit tamiḻ muṉṉilip peyarkaḷ, vaṭanāṭṭu intiyil tu, tum eṉṟum, ilattīṉil tu, vē  eṉṟum, kirēkkattil tū (cū), hmēyi eṉṟum paḻiya āṅkilattil  tū, yi(yū) eṉṟum, camaṟkirutattil tvam, yūyam eṉṟum muṟiyē tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. allatu vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
mēli moḻikaḷiluḷḷa teṉcollotta coṟkaṭ kellām mūlam tamiḻilēyē irukkiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu :
ayaṟcol	tamiḻccol	mūlam
ilattīṉ-maṉsu	maṉi	maṉ
kirēkkam-mātikō (mastigos)	mattiki	mottu
āṅkilam-turū	turuva	tuḷ
turukki-kāṉ	kōṉ	kōvaṉ
koro	karu	kaḷ
cīṉa moḻiyil mā eṉpatu kutiriyiyum tā eṉpatu valimi yiyum kuṟippatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tamiḻiṉ colvaḷattik kuṟikka vanta kālṭuvelār. am moḻi taṉakkē ciṟappāka vuriya vīṭu eṉṉum colli maṭṭumaṉṟi, il eṉṉum teluṅkac colliyum, maṉi eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭac colliyum, camaṟkirutattiṟkum piṉṉiya (Finnish) moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa kuṭi eṉṉum colliyum, taṉṉakattuk koṇṭuḷḷateṉṟu, atu tirāviṭat tiṟkut tāyum āriyattiṟku mūlamumākum eṉṉum uṇmiyik kuṟippākak kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. mēlum, tamiḻ ulaka mutaṉmoḻikku mika neruṅkiyateṉṟum, āriyattiṟku muntiyateṉṟum, palvēṟiṭaṅkaḷil veḷippaṭiyākavum kūṟiyuḷḷār. camaṟkirutattil ainti lirupaṅku tamiḻ eṉpatu iṉṟu ārāycciyāl teriyavarukiṉṟatu.
ā, ī. ū eṉṉum muttamiḻc cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷiṉiṉṟē āriyac cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷum paṭarkkip pakarap peyarkaḷum (Demonstratives and Pronouns of the third Person) tōṉṟip paṟpalavāṟu tirintu vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. pala āriya aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷum tamiḻāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. 
e-ṭu:	ilattīṉ amo (amar),  ser (cēr), do (tā). amartal - aṉpu kūrtal.
curuṅkac colliṉ, tamiḻccollē yillāta ulakap perumoḻi oṉṟumē yilli yeṉpatu maṟukka muṭiyāta uṇmiyākum.
4. tūymi
tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tāṉē tōṉṟi vaḷarnta taṉimoḻiyātalālum, ati nuṇṇaṟivu mikka potumakkaḷum pulamakkaḷum paṇpaṭutti vaḷarttamiyālum, nīrāviyiyum miṉṉiyattiyum tuṇik koḷḷāta ellāk kalikaḷiyum aṟiviyalkaḷiyum paṇṭit tamiḻarē kaṇṭaṟintuviṭṭamiyālum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum nūl vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭa ikkālattum, epporuḷ paṟṟiyum, paḻañcoṟ koṇṭum putuccoṟ puṉintum, muḻut tūymiyākap pēcat tamiḻ oṉṟiltāṉ iyalum. iv vuṇmiyi aṟintē, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟit taṉittu vaḻaṅkuvatu maṭṭumaṉṟit taḻittōṅkavuñ ceyyum eṉṟu kūṟiṉār kālṭuvelār.
centamiḻc colvaḷattik kāṇa, oru parukkip patamāka nilittiṇic coṟkaḷi nōkkiṉum pōtum. kumarināṭṭut tamiḻp potumakkaḷ, ilikaḷi nālvakiyāka vakuttu, vēmpum vāḻiyum pōla mellitāyiruppati iliyeṉṟum. nellum pullum pōlat tāḷi (taṇṭi)yoṭṭi nīṇṭu curacurappāka viruppatit tāḷ eṉṟum, cōḷamuṅ karumpum pōlap peruntāḷāka nīṇṭu toṅkuvatit       tōki yeṉṟum, teṉṉiyum paṉiyum pōlat tiṇṇamā        yiruppati ōliyeṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉar. pū nilikaḷi aivakiyākak        kaṇṭu tōṉṟiya niliyil arumpu eṉṟum, malarat toṭaṅkiya niliyiṟ pōtu eṉṟum, malarnta niliyil malar eṉṟum, utirntu viḻunta niliyil vī yeṉṟum, vāṭic civanta niliyiṟ cemmal eṉṟum, colliṉar; kāynilikaḷi nālvakiyākak kaṇṭu tōṉṟiya  niliyiṟ piñcu eṉṟum, caṟṟup  parutta niliyiṟ pirukku eṉṟum, muṟṟum parutta niliyiṟ kāy eṉṟum, paḻutta niliyiṟ paḻam (mañcaḷ niṟamāṉatu) allatu kaṉi (kaṉivāyiruppatu) eṉṟum, peyariṭṭaṉar. iṉi, piñcu niliyilum vāḻikkuk kaccal eṉṟum, māviṟku vaṭu eṉṟum, palā viṟku mūcu eṉṟum, teṉṉi paṉikkuk kurumpi eṉṟum, piṟavaṟṟiṟkup piṟavāṟum, ciṟappup peyar kuṟittaṉar.
āṅkilam ilakkakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷik koṇṭiruntum, ēṟattāḻa ellā moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟuṅ kaṭaṉ koṇṭum, kāyip paḻukkāta paḻam eṉṟu kuṟippatum, veyiliyum nilaviyuṅ kuṟikkat taṉic col illātiruppatum, iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
tamiḻiṉ iyalpi yaṟiyāta cilar, āṅkilam pōl tamiḻiyuṅ karutikkoṇṭu, oru moḻi piṟa moḻiyiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭāltāṉ vaḷara muṭiyumeṉṟum, moḻit tūymi yeṉpatu poruḷaṟṟa moḻiveṟi yeṉṟum, kūṟuvar. kālatar, cāḷaram, palakaṇi eṉa muccol lirukkavum, caṉṉal eṉṉum pōrttukkīciyac colli vēṇṭātu vaḻaṅkiyatāl, am muccollum vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyt tamiḻ vaṟumi yaṭintuḷḷami kāṇka. ``tamiḻarkkuḷḷa perumiyellām avar toṉṟutoṭṭut tūytāka vaḻaṅki varun tamiḻmoḻiyiṉiyē cārntirukkiṉṟatu'' eṉṟu, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār kūṟiyiruppati iṉimēlāyiṉuṅ karuttāyk kavaṉikka.
5. cemmi
coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum vaṭivilum poruḷilum ilakkaṇa muṭipilum vaḻānili, vaḻunili, vaḻuvamitinili eṉa munnilip paṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, vaḻuniliyillatu centamiḻ eṉṟum, aḵtuḷḷatu koṭun tamiḻ eṉṟum, tamiḻi iruvakiyāka vakuttaṉar ilakkaṇa nūlār. makkaṭku oḻukka varampu ettuṇi iṉṟiyamiyātatō, attuṇi iṉṟiyamiyātatē moḻikku ilakkaṇa varampum. coṟkaḷiṉ tiruntiya vaṭiviyum ōriya loḻuṅkiyum tamiḻiṟpōl vēṟemmoḻiyiluṅ kāṇa muṭiyātu.
e-ṭu:
	tamiḻ	teluṅku
taṉmip peyar	yāṉ, yām	ēṉu, mēmu
	nāṉ, nām	nēṉu, maṉamu
muṉṉilip peyar 	nīṉ(nī). nīm	nīvu, mīru
paṭarkkip peyar	tāṉ, tām	tāṉu, tāmu
a,i,u eṉṉum muccuṭṭeḻuttukaḷiyum e,ē, yā eṉṉum mūviṉāveḻuttukaḷiyum aṭiyākak koṇṭa tamiḻ aimpāṟ peyarkaḷiṉ ōriyaloḻuṅku, moḻinūlaṟiñariṉ uḷḷatti mikak kavarat takkatu. itu pōṉṟa aḻakiya meypporuḷiya loḻuṅki vēṟem moḻiyilēṉum moḻik kuṭumpattilēṉum kāṇa muṭiyāteṉṟu. kālṭuvelār viyantu kūṟiyiruttalik kāṇka.
putupperukku nīrikkuṟikkum veḷḷam eṉṉum centamiḻc col maliyāḷattil nīrp potuvik kuṟippatum, viṭi collutalik kuṟikkum cepputal eṉṉum centamiḻc col teluṅkiṟ potuvākac collutalik kuṟittalum, oṉṟic ceyya āṟṟutali (tiṟamiyō ṭiruttali)k  kuṟikkum māṭṭutal eṉṉum centamiḻccol kaṉṉaṭat til (māṭutal eṉṉum vaṭivil), potuvākac ceytalik kuṟikkiṉṟami yum koṭuntamiḻ niliyām. kūrntu pārttalik kuṟikkum nōkkutal eṉṉum col, maliyāḷattiṟ potuvākap pārttalik kuṟittalum aḵtē.
centamiḻeḻuttukaḷ koṭuntamiḻiṟ palavāṟu tiriyumēṉum, vali melittal maliyāḷattiṟkum rakaram tokal teluṅkiṟkum pakaram mūccoliyātal kaṉṉaṭattiṟkum, ciṟappākum.
e-ṭu:
maliyāḷam	:	kañci-kaññi, nīṅkaḷ- niṅṅaḷ, vantu- vannu.
teluṅku	:	curuṭṭu - tcuṭṭa, paruppu - pappu, maruntu - mantu.
kaṉṉaṭam	:	paḷḷi - haḷḷi, pāḷi - hāḷe, poṉ - 				hoṉṉu.
vaṭacoṟkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu palavakiyil tiriyum: āyiram - sakara, kaluḻaṉ - karuṭa (g), kōṭṭam - kōṣṭa, kōṭṭi - kōṭṭa, cāyi - cāyā (c), tūṇam - tūṇā, nakaram - nakara (g), paḷḷi - palli, mayil - mayūra, mukam - muka (kh), vaṭṭam - vrutta.
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻukku etu vaḻuniliyō, atu piṟa moḻikaṭku vaḻāniliyām. tamiḻukkuc cemmi iṉṟiyamiyāta paṇpāyiruttali ṉālēyē, atu centamiḻ eṉappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉ potumakkaḷōṭu ettuṇik kocciyāyp pēciṉum, ēṭeṭutteḻutum pōtum mēṭi yēṟip pēcum pōtum ilakkaṇa naṭiyik kiyāḷavēṇṭu meṉpatē tamiḻ marapu. am marapē, mūvāyiram āṇṭākat tamiḻik keṭukkap pakivar cūḻcci ceytuvariṉum, atu keṭāvāṟu ataṉik kāttu vantirukkiṉṟatu. ulaka vaḻakku vēṟu; kocci vaḻakku vēṟu. ulakam eṉpatu uyarntōr mēṟṟē. ātalāl, piṟa moḻikaḷpōl tamiḻiyuṅ karutikkoṇṭu, potumakkaḷ kocci naṭiyē ulaka vaḻakkeṉpatu tamiḻukku eṭṭuṇiyum poruntāteṉka.
6. mummi
ki.mu. pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉ tōṉṟiya talikkaḻakak kālattiliruntē, iciyum nāṭakamum iyaṟṟamiḻōṭu cērkkappaṭṭu, ilakkiyattamiḻ muttamiḻeṉa vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟatu. ataṉāl , mutaliru kaḻakattilum  vaḻaṅkiya ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ muttamiḻum paṟṟiyaṉa vākavē yiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam vēṟem moḻiyilumillāta vaḻakkiṟku, iciyum naṭippum pēccoṭu iyalpākak kalantiruppatoṭu, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ ici nāṭakak kalit tērcciyum paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyam muṟṟum ceyyuḷ vaṭiviliruntamiyum karaṇiyamākum.
7. iyaṟki vaḷarcci
tamiḻil iṭukuṟic collē yilli. ``ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē'' eṉpatu tolkāppiya nūṟpā (640). orumi paṉmi yeṇṇum poruṭpālumaṉṟi, āriyattiṟpōl irumi yeṇṇum coṟpālum tamiḻamippil amiyavilli.
paṉuvalkaḷil oru nāṭṭi varaṇikkumpōtu uḷporuḷiyum uḷnikaḻcciyiyumē yaṉṟi, ilporuḷiyum ilnikaḻcciyiyum āriya varukikku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭit tamiḻp pulavar kūṟuvatilli.
ceyyuḷaṇika ḷellām iyaṟkiyāka amintaṉa vaṉṟic ceyaṟkiyāka amikkappaṭṭaṉavalla.
8. ilakkaṇa niṟivu
maṟṟellā moḻiyilakkaṇaṅkaḷum eḻuttum, collum, coṟṟoṭarum, yāppum, aṇiyum ākiya aintiyē kūṟa, tamiḻilakkaṇam maṭṭum avaṟṟoṭu ceyyuṭkum nūṟkum uḷḷa poruḷiyuñ cērttu aṟukūṟukaḷik kūṟukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ coṟṟoṭarum yāppum aṇiyum poruḷil aṭakkappaṭṭu, eḻuttum collum poruḷum eṉa muppālavākavē paṇṭi yiyaṟṟamiḻ ilakkaṇaṅka ḷellām vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
akam puṟam eṉṉum iruvakip pākupāṭṭuḷ ellāp poruḷ kaḷum aṭakkappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa. puṟapporuḷ paṟṟiya eḻutiṇikaḷuḷ, mutalintum aracaṉukkuc ciṟappuk koṭuttaṟporuṭṭu avaṉukkuriya pōrt toḻiliyē paṟṟik kūṟiṉum, vāki pāṭāṇ eṉṉum iṟutiyiraṇṭum, maṟṟellā makkaḷ toḻilkaḷiyum cērttut taḻuvuvaṉa vākum. iti yaṟiyātār tamiḻp poruḷilakkaṇam kuṟipāṭuḷḷateṉak kuṟi kūṟuvar.
``kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻ ēṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō''  (tiruviḷi. nāṭṭup.)
eṉṉum parañcōti muṉivar pāṭal iṅkuk karutat takkatu.
``viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ
muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum'' 	(tol.1594)
eṉṟavāṟu, muṟṟat tuṟanta muḻumuṉivarē tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūl iyaṟṟiyavarātaliṉ, taṉittolippatum, taṉittoliyātatum, taṉit tolippatōṭu kūṭiyolippatum  ākiya mūvaki yolikaḷum, taṉi yuyiriyum taṉimeyyiyum (uṭampiyum allatu uyirillāp poruḷiyum) uyirōṭu kūṭiya meyyiyum muṟiyē ottiruppatāl, avaṟṟiṟku uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṟa peyarkaḷiyē uvamiyāku peyarāka iṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkēṟpa, uyirukkup puḷḷiyillā vaṭivum meykkup puḷḷiyuḷḷa vaṭivum uyirmeykkut taṉikkūṭṭu vaṭivum amittirukkiṉṟaṉar. iv varivaṭivamippum muṟiyeṉṉum variciyamippum mutaṉmutaṟ koṇṭeḻuntatu, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkē. atip piṉpaṟṟiyatē camaṟkiruta vaṇṇamāli. ataṟkum pintiṉavē, teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa maliyāḷat teṉṉāṭṭu neṭuṅkaṇakkukaḷum vaṅka kucarātti vaṭanāṭṭu neṭuṅkaṇakkukaḷum. tamiḻeḻuttu, cila tamiḻp pakivar kūṟukiṟapaṭi, acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmiyeḻuttiṉiṉṟu tirinta taṉṟu.
pakuttaṟivuḷḷa makkaḷiyē uyarvākavum maṟṟellāvaṟṟiyum tāḻvākavuṅ koṇṭu potumakkaḷē tamiḻmoḻiyi amittu viṭṭamiyāl, ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇam iyampum muṟiyil, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷi maṭṭum ilakkaṇa āciriyar iṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
peyarkaṭku mutaṉmutalāka eṭṭu vēṟṟumi vakuttuk kūṟiyatum tamiḻilakkaṇamē. atip piṉpaṟṟiyatē camaṟkirutam. kālṭuvelār karutukiṟapaṭi talimāṟāka nikaḻntataṉṟu.
9. ceyyuṭ ciṟappu
paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyam, uriyum (Commentary), uriccoṟ ṟokutiyum (nikaṇṭum) uṭpaṭa, muṟṟum ceyyuḷ vaṭivilēyē iruntatu.
``pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
aṅkatam mutucolō ṭavvēḻ nilattum
...........................................
yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar'' 	(1336)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟutal kāṇka.
kātalum vaḻuttumpaṟṟip pāṭutaṟkuc ciṟanta kalippāp pōṉṟa ceyyuḷvaki, vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇpataṟkilli.
akapporuṭ ceyyuḷkaḷil, paṇṭit tamiḻaka niliyi aṟiyak kūṭiyavāṟu ceytikaḷi yamittup pāṭum pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam eṉṉum ceyyuṇ marapu tamiḻukkē ciṟappām.
ovvoru  talimuṟiyilum, kaṭuttup pāṭavum ceyyuḷilēyē uriyāṭavum valla pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa pulavar paṇṭit tamiḻakat tiruntaṉar.
10. aṇicciṟappu
poruḷaṇiyil, uḷḷuṟiyuvamam tamiḻukkuc ciṟappākavuriya tākum.
collaṇiyil, tiruppukaḻ pōṉṟa vaṇṇaṅkaḷum kakarappāṭṭum takarap pāṭṭum pōṉṟa ōreḻuttup pāṭṭum, valliṉap pāṭṭum melliṉap pāṭṭum iṭiyiṉap pāṭṭum ākiya ōriṉap pāṭṭum, orē ceyyuḷip palvēṟu ceyyuḷkaḷākap pakukkak kūṭiya patiṉpaṅki (tacapaṅki), nūṟṟuppaṅki (catapaṅki) mutaliyaṉavum, āḻikkaṭṭu (cakkarapantam), tērkkaṭṭu (ratapantam), eṇṇākap piṇiyal (aṭṭanāka pantam) mutaliya miṟippākkaḷum tamiḻiṟpōl vēṟemmoḻiyilum pāṭa muṭiyātu.
11. nūṟciṟappu
āriya varukikku muṟpaṭṭa āyirakkaṇakkāṉa taṉittamiḻ ilaṅku nūlkaḷellām aḻintum aḻikkappaṭṭum pōṉapiṉpum, tolkāppiyam pōṉṟa ilakkaṇa nūlum tirukkuṟaḷ pōṉṟa aṟanūlum, vēṟemmoḻiyilum illiyeṉpatu veḷḷiṭimali.
ittakiya viḻumiya nūliyum viyattaku paṇpiyuṅ koṇṭa ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyi, iṟṟit tamiḻaṉ iṉi mēlāyiṉum pōṟṟik kāttu muṉṉēṟa muyalvāṉāka.
- ``kaḻakap poṉviḻā malar'' kaṭakam 1970
3
tamiḻiṉ taṉiyiyalpukaḷ
tamiḻ, varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta mutupaḻan toṉmoḻiyātalālum, atu tōṉṟiya paḻam pāṇṭināṭu teṉmāvāriyil muḻukiyum, an nilat teḻunta mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷum tamiḻp pakivarāl aḻiyuṇṭum pōṉataṉālum, ataṉ taṉiyiyalpukaḷ neṭuṅkālat tiṭamaṉa naṭunili ārāycciyiṉṟit talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyarālum aṟiyavoṇ ṇātaṉavā yirukkiṉṟaṉa. avviyalpukaḷāvaṉa:-
1. toṉmi
``ōṅka liṭivan tuyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷakaṟṟum - āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatiroṉ ṟēṉiyatu
taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ''
eṉpatu, taṇṭiyalaṅkāra uriyil mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa paḻam pā.
tamiḻi ulakat takaviruḷi yakaṟṟum cuṭarākac coṉṉatiṉ karuttu, atu ulaka mutaṟṟāy uyartaṉic cemmoḻi yeṉpatē. ataṉālēyē, ap pāvip pāṭiyavar, tamiḻi oppuyarvaṟṟa moḻiyeṉṟuṅ kūṟi muṭittār.
eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatākac collappaṭum puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māliyiṉ āciriyarākiya aiyaṉāritaṉār, kuṟiñciyum mulliyuṅ kalanta pālinilattu maṟavar kuṭiyiṉ paḻimiyik kuṟikkumiṭattu, 
``poyyakala nāḷum pukaḻviḷitta leṉviyappām
viyakam pōrtta vayaṅkolinīr - kiyakalak
kaṟṟōṉṟi maṇṭōṉṟāk kālattē vāḷōṭu
muṟṟōṉṟi mūtta kuṭi''	(pu. ve. 35)
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. kuṟiñci mullivāṇar mikap paḻimiyāṉa tamiḻ vakuppār. avar kuṭiyiṉ toṉmi kūṟavē, tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyum uṭaṉ kūṟiyavāṟām.
iṉi, muttamiḻt tuṟipōki muṟṟat tuṟantu mūvēntariyum muttamiḻ nāṭṭiyum oppap pukaḻnta cēramuṉivar iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟutiyil tāmiyaṟṟiya cilappatikārattuḷ,
``paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyu mimayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi''	(11:19-22)
eṉṟu pāṭiyiruppatum; aṭiyārkkunallār, ``toṭiyōḷ puvamum'' eṉṉum cilappatikāra vēṉiṟkātit toṭarukku,
``akkālattu, avar nāṭṭut teṉpāli mukattiṟku vaṭavelli yākiya paḵṟuḷiyeṉṉu māṟṟiṟkum kumariyeṉṉu māṟṟiṟkumiṭiyē eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatavāṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ nīrmali vāṉeṉa malinta ēḻteṅka nāṭum, ēḻmaturi nāṭum, ēḻ muṉpāli nāṭum, ēḻ piṉpāli nāṭum, ēḻkuṉṟa nāṭum, ēḻkuṇakāri nāṭum, ēḻkuṟumpaṉi nāṭum eṉṉum inta nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭum, kumari kollam mutaliya paṉmali nāṭum kāṭum natiyum patiyum taṭanīrkkumari vaṭaperuṅ kōṭṭiṉkāṟum, kaṭal koṇṭoḻitalāṟ kumariyākiya puvameṉṟāreṉṟuṇarka'' eṉṟu viḷakkavuri varintiruppatum; teṉmāvāriyil mūḻkippōṉa paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭiyum ataṉ parappiyu muṇarttum.
paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum kumariyāṟṟiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa toliviṉ aḷavum, palvēṟu nilappakutikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭa muntu nūlkaḷuḷ allatu urikaḷuḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. aṭiyārkkunallār avaṟṟik kaṭṭik kūṟiyirukka muṭiyātu.
iṟiyaṉā rakapporuḷuriyiṟ kāṇum mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa, teṉmaturit talikkaḻakamum kapāṭa (katava?) pura iṭikkaḻakamum, muḻukippōṉa paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭuṭpaṭṭaṉavē.
oru kālattil, teṉṉintiyā, muḻukippōṉa teṉṉila vāyilāka āttirēliyāvuṭaṉum, teṉṉāppirikkāvuṭaṉum iṇikkappaṭṭiruntata ṉālum, tamiḻ ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyātalālum, tamiḻ ulaka mutaṉmoḻi yātalālum, māntaṉ piṟantakam maṭakācukkarp (Madagascar) pakkam eṉṟu ekkēl (Haeckel) pērāciriyarum, taṅkaṉiyikkāp (Tanganyika) pakkam eṉṟu ilīkki (Leakey) eṉṉum māntaṉūl ārāycciyāḷarum, kūṟiyiruppavi, paḻam pāṇṭināṭṭi oru pakutiyākak koṇṭa kumarik kaṇṭam eṉṉum oru perun teṉṉilap parappu, orukālattirun tamikkup puṟaccāṉṟukaḷām.
tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷātalāl, tamiḻ varalāṟṟiṟkuk kumarināṭṭuk koḷki iṉṟiyamiyāta aṭippaṭic ceytiyākum.
mūvēṟu kaṭalkōḷkaḷāṟ paṭippaṭiyāka muḻukippōṉa kumari nāṭṭiṉ teṉkōṭiyiliruntatu kumarimali yeṉṟum, vaṭakōṭiyi liruntatu kumariyāṟu eṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.
2. meṉmi
māntaṉ piṟantakamum tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamum paḻam pāṇṭināṭum ākiya kumarināṭu, nākarika nāṭāyiṉum, ulaka mutal nāṭātalāl, atil vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa tamiḻariṉ vāyil, uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭumāka muppateḻuttukaḷē tōṉṟiṉa. avaṟṟuḷḷum, ḻa, ḷa, ṟa, ṉa eṉṉum nāṉkum pintit tōṉṟiyaviyākum.
tamiḻccoṉ mutalil taṉimeyyum cila uyirmeyyum, colliṭiyil, cila meykaṭkup piṉ cila vēṟṟumeyyum, colliṟuti yil valliṉa meyyum, varuvatēyilli. ik kaṭṭuppāṭum vilakkum vēṇṭumeṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭavi yalla. māntaṉ vāyoli vaḷarcci nōkkiṉ, akkālat tamiḻar ciṟupiḷḷi niliyiliruntataṉāl, avar vāyil eḷiya muṟiyil olikkakkūṭiya taṉiyolikaḷum kūṭṭoli kaḷumē piṟantaṉa. ikkālattum, nelli māvaṭṭa nāṭṭuppuṟa makkaḷ, cākṣi eṉṉum vaṭacollic cākki eṉṟum, jāti eṉṉum vaṭacollic cāti eṉṟum, olippatik kāṇalām.
iṅṅaṉam eḷiya volikaḷik koṇṭiruntum, tamiḻiṉ ōciyiṉimikku eḷḷaḷavuṅ kuṟivilli.
``taṇṭali mayilka ḷāṭat tāmari viḷakkan tāṅkak
koṇṭalkaḷ muḻavi ṉēṅkak kuvaḷikaṇ viḻittu nōkkat
teṇṭiri yeḻiṉi kāṭṭat tēmpiḻi makara yāḻiṉ
vaṇṭuka ḷiṉitu pāṭa marutamvīṟ ṟirukku mātō''	(nāṭṭup. 4)
eṉṉuṅ kamparāmāyaṇac ceyyuḷip pāṭik kāṇka.
oru moḻikku vēṇṭiyavi coṟkaḷē yaṉṟit taṉiyeḻuttuka ḷalla. kumarināṭṭup potumakkaḷ, muppatolikaḷik koṇṭē, akkālattil maṭṭumaṉṟi iṉivaruṅ kālattum māntaṉ maṉattil tōṉṟakkūṭiya ellāk karuttukaḷiyum kuṟikkattakka, vērccoṟ kaḷip piṟappittiruppatāl, tamiḻukkup piṟamoḻi yolikaḷē tēviyilli.
urappiyum eṭuttum kaṉittum olikkum mūvakic ceyaṟki vallolikaḷum, mūccoliyum, tamiḻiliṉmiyāl atu ciṟu piḷḷi kaḷum nōyāḷiyarum kaḻiperu mūppiṉarum eḷitāy olikkat takkatā yiruppatuṭaṉ; ēṉi moḻikaḷi liyalāta nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tiruppukaḻ vaṇṇaṅkaḷum; valliṉap pāṭṭu, melliṉap pāṭṭu, iṭiyiṉap pāṭṭu aṭimaṭakku, iru poru ḷiraṭṭuṟal, palporu ḷiraṭṭuṟal, naṭuveḻuttaṇi, muppāki, patiṉpaṅki, patiṟṟup patiṉpaṅki mutaliya collaṇikaḷum; kōmūttiri, cuḻikuḷam, tāmarik kaṭṭu, cakkarakkaṭṭu, tērkkaṭṭu, irunākap piṇiyal, eṇṇākap piṇiyal mutaliya miṟip pākkaḷum (cittirak kavikaḷum); pāṭaṟkēṟṟatāyu mirukkiṉṟatu.
3. tāymi
makkaṭ perukkam, vaṇikam, koḷḷikkum pōrukkum paḻikkum tappal, putunāṭu kāṇal, kaṭalkōḷ mutaliya pala karaṇiyaṅkaḷāl, tamiḻar vaṭakku nōkkic ceṉṟu, nāḷaṭivil tamiḻ tirāviṭamākat tirintatāl tāmum tirāviṭarāyiṉar. piṉṉart tirāviṭaruḷ oru cārār, mēṟkūṟiya karaṇiyam paṟṟiyē vaṭamēṟkākac ceṉṟu, airōppāvi yaṭintu āriyarāka māṟiṉar.
ātalāl, kumarināṭṭut tamiḻē tiraviṭattiṟkut tāyum āriyat tiṟku mūlamumāyiṟṟu.
tamiḻ eṅṅaṉan tiraviṭamākat tirintateṉpataṟku, oru teluṅku viṉiccolliṉ kūṟukaḷē pōtumāṉavi.
e-ṭu: āku eṉṉum viṉiccol
colvaki	tamiḻ	teluṅku
mutaṉili	ā, āku	avu
ēval orumi	ā, āku	kā
ēval paṉmi	ākum,	kammu, kaṇṭi
		ākuṅkaḷ
i. kā. ā. pā. viṉimuṟṟu	āyiṉāṉ	ayiṉāṭu
i. kā. peyareccam	āṉa	ayiṉa, aiṉa
i. kā. viṉiyeccam	āy, āki	ayi, ai
e. kā. viṉiyeccam	āka	kā, ava
nilippāṭṭu viṉiyeccam	āyiṟṟēl	ayittē
e. kā. viṉimuṟṟu	ākum, ām	avuṉu
maṟuppiṇippuc col	āṉāl,	kāṉi,	
		āyiṉ,	ayiṉaṉu
		āyiṉāl
ottukkoḷ viṭiccol	ām (yes)	avuṉu
paṭarkki oṉṟaṉpāl
e. kā. etirmaṟi viṉimuṟṟu	ākātu	kātu
toḻiṟpeyar	ātal,	avuṭṭa,
		ākutal	kāvaṭamu
kūṭṭuviṉi	ākavēṇṭum	kāvaleṉu
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷellām piṟkālattut tirimoḻikaḷātalāl, avaṟṟil tamiḻilillāta vallolikaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu:
tamiḻ	teluṅku
kuṭi (=vīṭu, kōvil)	gudi
cey	ceyu (cēyu)
caṟuku	jaṟu (jaru)
tēṅkāy	tenkaya (teṅkkāya)
miñcu	mincu (miñccu)
kuṇṭu (ciṟukuṭṭi)	kunta (kuṇṭṭa)
monti	munta (muntta)
kumpu	gumpu (kumppu)
eṉṟār	antaru (aṇṭṭāru)
eṭuppolikaḷ (voiced sounds) āriyattiṟ pōṉṟē tiraviṭa moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkuvatāl, kumarināṭṭut tamiḻt tōṟṟatti aṟiyātavarum oppukkoḷḷātavarum, tamiḻilum muṟkālattil ga, ja, da, da, ba eṉṉum eṭuppolikaḷ vaḻaṅkip piṉṉark ka, ca, ṭa, ta, pa eṉṉum eṭuppilā (unvoiced) volikaḷāka māṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉpar. iṅṅaṉaṅ koḷpavar ayalnāṭṭārum tamiḻp pakivarum viyāpurikaḷum āvar. piñcu muṟṟik kāyāṉa piṉ mīḷap piñcākātatupōl, eṭuppilā voli polintu eṭuppoliyāṉapiṉ, mīḷa eṭuppilā voliyāka māṟāteṉa aṟika. teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷi mēṉāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantēṟikaḷākat talikīḻāyk koṇṭataṉālēyē, iṅṅaṉam talikīḻāṉa muṭipiṟku vara nērntatu. itu iyaṟkikku māṟāṉateṉpati, kuḻanti vāyoli kaḷiyum vaḷarntōṉ vāyolikaḷiyum oppunōkkik kāṇka.
tamiḻ āriyattiṟku mūlam eṉpati, palvēṟu tamiḻ vērccoṟ kaḷiṉiṉṟum tirintu kuṭumpaṅ kuṭumpamākavum kulaṅkulamākavum vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷuṭ ciṟcila, āriyattiṟ kuṭumpat toṭarpuṅ kulattoṭarpumiṉṟit taṉittaṉiyāy vaḻaṅkuvataṉāl aṟiyalām.
e-ṭu: kul - kulavu. kulavutal = vaḷital.
L. clino = to bend. Gk. klino = to slope.
kul - kuli - kuḷi - kuḷiki = māttiri yuruṇṭi.
kuḷi - kuḻi - kuḻiyam = 1. vaḷitaṭi, 2. naṟumaṇa vuruṇṭi.
kul - kuḷ - kuḷu - kuḻu - kuḻavi = uruṇṭu nīṇṭa arikal (ammikkuḻavi).
kuḷiki - va. (Skt.) kuḷikā.
L. globus, E. globe = spherical body kulavu - kuravu, kuravutal = vaḷital.
L. curvo = to bend. E. curve.
kuravu - kuravi = eḻuvar allatu paṉṉiruvar vaṭṭamāka niṉṟu āṭippāṭic cuṟṟivaruṅ kūttu.
Gk. choros = (orig.) a dance in a ring. L. and E. chorus.
kuravu - kuraku - kuraṅku. kuraṅkutal = vaḷital.
AS. cringan, crincan, E. cringe, crinkle, crank, D. krinkel, W. crom (bent).
kul - kur - kuru - kuruḷ. kuruḷutal = curuḷutal.
E. curl (formerly `crull'), D. krullen, Dan. krolle, E. scroll, scrowl.
kuru - kuruku = 1. vaḷiyal, 2. vaḷinta kaḻuttuḷḷa nāri, kokku, ōtimam (aṉṉam) mutaliya nīrppaṟavip potu.
AS. cran, E. crane, D. craan, Ger. krahn, kraniah, Ice. trani, Dan. trane.
Amor. karan, W. garan, Gk. geranos, L. grus, OF. grue.
kuru - kuṟu - kiṟu. kiṟukiṟuttal = 1. cuṟṟutal, 2. cuḻalutal.
kiṟukiṟuppu = tali cuṟṟuvatu pōṉṟa mayakkam.
kiṟukiṟuvāṇam = cuḻaṟṟi vīcum poṟivāṇam.
Gk. guros = ring, E. gyre, gyrate = to go in circle, to whirl.
kuṟu - kaṟu - kaṟaku - kaṟaṅku=1. cuḻaṟci, 2. kāṟṟāṭi. kaṟaṅku-kaṟaṅkal = vaḷitaṭi.
OE., OS., OHG. hring, ON. hringr, E. ring = circle, circular ornament, (mōtiram).
kul - kuḷ - kuḷam = 1. vaḷinta neṟṟi, 2. vellavuruṇṭi.
kuḷ - kuṇṭu = uruṇṭiyāṉatu.
marā. kuṇṭa. kuṇṭu - kuṇṭalam = vaṭṭam, cuṉṉam, kātu vaḷiyam. va. kuṇṭala.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭāṉ, kuṇṭā = uruṇṭu vāyakaṉṟa kaṇṇāṭic caṭṭi. marā. kuṇṭā (gunda).
kuḷam - kuṭam = 1. vaḷivu. 2. uruṇṭiyāṉa nīrkkalam. va. kuṭa, kaṭa (ghata)
kuṭam - kuṭantam = uṭampi vaḷittuk (kuṉintu) kumpiṭum vaḻipāṭu.
kuṭantam - kuṭanti = vaḷivu.
kuṭam - kuṭi = 1. vaḷinta puruvam. va. (Skt) pru - kuṭī.
(mutaṟkālattil vaṭṭamāka amikkappaṭṭa) ciṟuvīṭu, vīṭu.
AS. cote, cyte, OE, E., cot, Ice., MDu., ON. kot, ML G. kot, kote, koth,
E. cot, Skt. kuti.
