E. cottage.
kuṭi - kuṭil = 1. āṭṭukkuṭṭikaḷi yaṭikkum vaṭṭamāṉa kūṭu.
2. ciṟu kuṭici.
Skt. kutira (kuṭīra).
kuṭil - kuṭilam = vaḷivu. Skt. kut@ila
kul - kol - kōl = uruṇṭu nīṇṭa kampu.
kōl - kōli = ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭuk kuṇṭu. marā. kōlī (goli).
kuḷ - koḷ - koṭu = vaḷinta. koṭuṅkōl = vaḷinta kōl.
koṭu - koṭukku = vaḷinta uṟuppu, koṭṭum muḷ.
Ice. krokr, Sw. krok, Dan. krog, D. cruk, W. crwg, Gael. crocan, OF. crok, E. crook.
ṭa - ra. o. nō; paṭavar - paravar, mukaṭi - mukari.
kuṭaku - Coorg, taraṅkampāṭi - Tranquebar.
koḷ - koḷḷu - koṭku - koṭku - kokki.
AS. hoc, hooc, D. hock, Ice. haki, Ger. haken, OHG. hako, L.G. hakc, E. hook.
koḷ - kōḷ - kōḷam = uruṇṭi. Skt. gola (kōla).
kōḷ - kōṇ - kōṇam. Gk. gonia, E. penta gon.
kōṇ - kōṭu - kōṭṭam = 1. vaḷivu. 2. matil cūḻnta kōvil, matil cūḻnta ciṟiccāli. Skt. kostha (kōṣṭa).
kōṭṭam - kōṭṭi = 1. nilāvic cūḻum vaṭṭakkōṭu, 2. matil cūḻnta araṇmaṉi allatu nakar, va. (Skt.) kōṭṭa. L. castrum.
ivaṟṟi oru pāṉic cil parukkip patam pōlak koḷka. iṅṅaṉam ērāḷamāṉa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ āriyac coṟkaḷōṭu oliyum poruḷum ottiruppatu, koṭivaḻit toṭarpiṉālaṉṟit taṟceyalāka irukka muṭiyātu.


4. tūymi
ulaka moḻikaḷuḷ, tūymiyi vēṇṭuvatum, atik kāttuk koḷḷa vallatum tamiḻ oṉṟē. itaṉ viḷakkatti `vaḷam' eṉṉum ciṟutalippiṉkīḻk kāṇka.
5. cemmi
tamiḻar pēriṉamāyp perukip perunilattiṟ paraviyapiṉ, coṟkaḷiṉ vaṭivu potumakkaḷ vāyil iṭantōṟum vēṟupaṭuvatiyum, av vēṟupāṭṭiṟku elliyiṉmiyiyum, kaṇṭa ilakkaṇa nūlār, tiruttamāṉa vaṭiviyē aḷavippaṭutti ataṟkuc centamiḻ eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar.
irukkutu, irukku, ikkutu, ikku, kītu eṉa vevvēṟiṭattil vevvēṟu vaṭivil vaḻaṅkiṉālum, ēṭeṭutteḻutum pōtum mēṭiyēṟip pēcum pōtum, irukkiṉṟatu allatu irukkiṟatu eṉṉum vaṭiviyē āḷavēṇṭumeṉpatu, tollāciriyar kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭa cemmiyeṉṉum varampām.
``makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē.'' ``uyartiṇi yeṉmaṉār makkaṭ cuṭṭē'' eṉṟu makkaḷ iyal variyaṟi ceyyappaṭṭiruppatāl ataṟkēṟpa ellā viṉikaḷiyum paṇpaṭṭa muṟiyil iyaṟṟuvatē vilaṅkiṉum uyarnta nilimiyik kāṭṭuva tākum.
āyiṉum, ic cemmiyit tamiḻaḷavu ēṉi moḻikaḷ pēṇa iyalātu. tamiḻil vaḻuniliyāyiruppaṉa pala ēṉimoḻikaḷil vaḻā niliyām. ātalāl, teṉmoḻikaḷ tamiḻum tirāviṭamum eṉa iraṇṭākap pirittaṟiyappaṭum. tūymiyuñ cemmiyum uṇmi yiṉmikaḷē itaṟkuk karaṇiyam.
6. mummi
ilakkiya niliyil, iciyum nāṭakamum iyaloṭu cērkkap paṭuvatāl, tamiḻ muttamiḻ eṉappaṭum.
7. iyaṉmi
ēṉi yilakkiyap perumoḻikaḷilum, ilakkaṇam eḻuttum collum yāppum aṇiyum eṉa nāṟkūṟē koṇṭatu. tamiḻilō, ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum aṟiviyal muṟiyiṟ pākupaṭuttum poru ḷilakkaṇamum uṇṭu.
cilar, kātalum pōrum ākiya iraṇṭiyē poruḷilakkaṇaṅ kūṟumeṉpar. avar aṟiyār. kātalallāta ellāp poruḷkaḷum puṟapporuḷāka vākit tiṇiyuḷ aṭaṅkum.
8. viyaṉmi
cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ ulakeṅkum paraviyuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: ammi appaṉ mutaliya peṟṟōr peyarkaḷ.
9. vaḷami
teṟkil īrāyiraṅ kal tolivu nīṇṭirunta paḻam pāṇṭināṭu nīruḷ mūḻkiviṭṭataṉāṟ pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkuc           coṟkaḷum, mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāṟ pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭu mīḷā niliyaṭintaṉa.
āyiṉum, ikkālattum, ili, tāḷ, tōki, ōli eṉṉum nālvaki ilip peyarkaḷum; arumpu, pōtu, malar, vī, cemmal eṉṉum ainnilip pūppeyarkaḷum; kaccal (vāḻi), vaṭu (mā), mūcu (palā), kurumpi (teṉṉi, paṉi) eṉṉum palvēṟu piñcukaḷiṉ ciṟappup peyarkaḷum, uḷḷaṉa.
yāṉiyik kuṟikka irupattintiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa peyarkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. 
tottunōy (Infectious disease), oṭṭuvāroṭṭi (Contagious disease), tikkutal (to stammer), koṉṉutal (to stutter) eṉa nuṭpa vēṟupāṭu kuṟikkum oruporuṭ palacoṟkaḷum nirampavuḷḷaṉa.
iv viyalpi yaṟiyātār vaṭacoṟ kaṭaṉkōḷ tamiḻukku iṉṟiyamiyāta teṉpar. vaṭacoṟ kalappāl, teṉcoṟkaḷ vaḻak kiṟantum vaḻakkuk kuṉṟiyum poruḷ māṟiyum vērpporuḷ maṟintum iṟantupaṭṭumē pōyiṉa. ataṉāl tīmiyēyaṉṟi  naṉmiyilli.
e-ṭu:
teṉcol	vaṭacol
vaḻakkiṟattal:
	cūḷ, kaḻuvāy	āṇi, pirāyaccittam
vaḻakkuk kuṉṟal:
	āṇṭu, makiḻcci	varuṣam, cantōṣam
poruḷmāṟal:
	uyirmey, tōḷ	pirāṇi, pujam
vērpporuḷ maṟital:
	akaṅkari, pattiṉi	ahaṅkāra, patnī
mēlum vaṭamoḻiyeṉṉum camaṟkirutamē āyirakkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷit tamiḻiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷatu. am moḻic coṟkaḷuḷ, ainti lirupakuti tamiḻ; ainti lorupakuti mēliyāriyam; ainti lorupakuti vaṭanāṭṭup pirākirutam; ainti loru pakuti putitākap puṉiyappaṭṭavi. vaṭamoḻittuṇiyiṉṟit tamiḻ vaḻaṅka muṭiyum; āyiṉ tamiḻt tuṇiyiṉṟi vaṭamoḻi vaḻaṅkamuṭiyātu. eṉ ``The Primary Classical Language of the World'' eṉṉum nūlip pārkka.
10.	iḷami
tamiḻ ulaka mutaṟṟāy moḻiyāyiruntu patiṉeṇ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip piṟappittum, ēṟattāḻa aimpāṉ āriya moḻikaḷit tōṟṟuvittum, iṉṟum iḷamiyāyiruppatu, ataṉ aḻiyāt taṉmiyik kāṭṭum. ``eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟamiḻi yampiyici koṇṭāṉ'' eṉṟār kampar. ``āriyampō lulakavaḻak kaḻintoḻintu citiyāvuṉ, cīriḷamit tiṟamviyantu ceyaṉmaṟantu vāḻttutumē'' eṉṟār cuntarampiḷḷi.
- urimi vēṭki poṅkal malar 1974
4
tamiḻ paṟṟiya aṭippaṭi uṇmikaḷ 
oruvar tamiḻiṉ iyalpi allatu ciṟappic cevviyāy aṟiya vēṇṭumeṉiṉ, mūvaṭippaṭi yuṇmikaḷi muṟpaṭa vuṇartal vēṇṭum. aviyāvaṉa:
1. tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiyateṉpatu
tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiyateṉpataṉāl, tamiḻar teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ eṉpatum uṭaṉ peṟappaṭum.
``paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk 
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi''	(cilap. 11 : 19 - 22)
eṉṟu  patiṉeṇ nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē, naṟṟiṟa naṭunili muttamiḻ muṉivaṉ iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟiya tērtal vēṇṭāt teṇporuṭ kūṟṟum, ``toṭiyōḷ puvamum'' eṉṉum cilappatikāra vēṉiṟkātit toṭarukku aṭiyārkkunallār uritta vuriyum, iṟiyaṉā rakap poruḷuri mukkaḻaka varalāṟum, tamiḻiṉ kumarināṭṭut tōṟṟattiṟkup pōtiyaṉavum maṟukka voṇṇātaṉavumāṉa cāṉṟukaḷām.
tamiḻ varalāṟṟiṟkeṭṭāta toṉmutu paḻimiyāṉa ulaka mutaṉ moḻiyātalāl, kiṟittuviṟkup piṟpaṭṭa kālattu mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṟ pala kālamuraṇpaṭṭa kuḻaṟupaṭikaḷ kuḻampik kiṭappatu iyalpē. ac cikkalkaḷik kaḷintu uṇmikaḷi vaṭitteṭuttal varalāṟṟārāycci yāḷaṉ kaṭamiyākum. ak kaṭamiyi mēṟkoṇṭē, tiru. (P.T.) cīnivācayyaṅkārum pērā. (V.R.) irāmaccantira tīṭcitarum tamiḻariṉ teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭimiyit tattam nūlkaḷil aiyantiripaṟa nāṭṭic ceṉṟaṉar.
āyiṉum, elliyaṟṟa iṉaveṟiyum moḻiveṟiyum pittoṭu kalanta pēykōḷ pōl varuttuvatāl, pērā. (kē) nīlakaṇṭa cāttiri yārum avar māṇavarāṉa par. (Dr.) (N.) cuppiramaṇiyaṉārum, piṟarum, iṭikkālattil tīttiṟamākavum teṟṟumāṟṟākavum pukuttappaṭṭa camaṟkiruta mēmpāṭṭi eṉṟum pōṟṟik kāttaṟporuṭṭu, tamiḻariṉ kumarināṭṭut tōṟṟatti viṭāppiṭiyāy maṟittu varukiṉṟaṉar.
iṉṟum tirāviṭa moḻikaṭkuḷ muntiyatum talimiyāṉatum tamiḻēyātalāl. āḻnta tamiḻc collārāycci ceyyātavar tamiḻaṉ piṟantakatti āyntu kāṇṭalaritu. coṟkaḷiṉ vaṭivoppumi kāṇṭal vēṟu; avaṟṟiṉ muṉmi piṉmi āyntaṟital vēṟu. par. (Dr.) (N.) ilākōvāri (Lahovary) tam `tiraviṭat toṟṟamum mēṟkum' (Dravidian Origins and the West)  eṉṉum nūlil, coṟkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟi ārāyātu orucār vaṭivoppumi yoṉṟē koṇṭu, tiraviṭaṉ piṟantakam naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkarip pāṅkar eṉak kāṭṭa muyalkiṉṟār. tiraviṭattiṟku mūlam (kumarināṭṭut) tamiḻ eṉṟum, tiraviṭaṉukku muntiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉ eṉṟum, tamiḻam eṉṉuñ collē tramiḷa--tramiṭa--traviṭa--trāviṭa eṉat tirintateṉṟum, aṭippaṭi yuṇmikaḷiyē avar aṟiyavilli. pala coṟkaḷit tavaṟākavum pirittuḷḷār.
kālṭuvelār kālattil, tolkāppiyamum kaṭikkaḻaka (caṅka) ilakkiyamum talimit tamiḻp pulavarkkun teriyātu maṟiyuṇṭu kiṭantamiyālum, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ pōlum vaḻikāṭṭiyiṉmiyālum, avar ayalnāṭṭiṉarātalālum, ētēṉ tōṭṭak katiyi eḻuttuppaṭi nampiya `kiṟittavak kuravarātalālum, avar tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōri mēṉāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantēṟiyarākak koṇṭatiṟ kuṟṟamoṉṟumilli. avar aṅṅaṉaṅ koṇṭaviṭattum, tamiḻ āriyattiṟku mūttateṉṟum, ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyoṭu neruṅkiya terṭarpuṭiyateṉṟum, kūṟiyatu mikamikap pārāṭṭattakkatām.
iṉṟu, ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka ārāycciyitaḻil (Journal of the Madras University), tiraviṭat tōṟṟap putirviṉā--oru moḻiyiyal māntaṉūliyal paḻam poruṭ kaliyiyal aṭuttāyvu (The Problem of Dravidian Origins--A Linguistic, Anthropological and Archeological Approach) eṉṉum talippil, 1956 - 57-l pērā. (T.) pālakiruṭṭiṇa nāyar nikaḻttiya vayavar villiyam meyer māṉiyac coṟpoḻivukaḷ (Sir William Meyer Endowment Lectures) veḷiyiṭappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. avi āḻnta tamiḻc colliya lārāycciyillārkku muḻu meypōlat tōṉṟum, tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam mēṉāṭṭateṉpataṟku avi kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷiṉ pōlimi, aṭutta āṇṭu nāṉ veḷiyiṭavirukkum The Lemurian Language and Its Ramifications eṉṉum nūlil virivāka viḷakkap peṟum.
aṭippaṭik koḷki tavaṟāyiruppiṉ ettuṇip pēraṟiñar uṟaḻāṭiṉum muṭivu tavaṟākavē yirukkum, teṉkumari nāṭṭāṉi mēṉāṭṭāṉeṉṟu talikīḻākak koṇṭataṉālēyē, camaṟkiruta neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉiṉṟu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkut tōṉṟiṟṟeṉṟum, tamiḻ vēṟṟumip pākupāṭu camaṟkiruta vēṟṟumip pākupāṭṭit taḻuviyateṉṟum, kālṭuvelārum; g, j, d, t, b ākiya āriya eṭup polikaḷ ka, ca, ṭa, ta, pa ākiya tamiḻ eṭuppilā volikaḷāka māṟiṉaveṉṟu par. (Dr.) cu. ku. caṭṭarciyum; vrutta eṉṉum vaṭacol pirākirutattil vaṭṭa eṉṟum tamiḻil vaṭṭam eṉṟum tirintateṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyāḷarum; talikīḻāka urippārāyiṉar.
2. ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉaveṉpatu
``ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē.''	 (peyariyal,1)
eṉpatu tolkāppiya nūṟpā''	
tamiḻ iyaṉmoḻiyātalāl, atiluḷḷa peyar viṉi yiṭi yeṉṉum mūvakippaṭṭa ellāc collum, vērpporuḷuṇarttum karaṇiyak kuṟikaḷē. āriyam tirimoḻiyātaliṉ, atiluḷḷa cila pala coṟkaḷ, mēṉmēlun tirintu mutaṉiliyurut teriyāvāṟu muṟṟuñ citintu, vērpporuḷ aṟiya muṭiyā niliyil uḷḷaṉa. ataṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇa nūlār, vērpporuḷ viḷaṅkāc coṟkaḷi iṭukuṟi yeṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ, ivaṟṟi vaṇṇaṉimoḻi nūlārō, ellā moḻiyum iṭukuṟit tokutiyeṉṟoru neṟiyīṭu ceytu, tam iru kaṇṇiyum iṟukak kaṭṭikkoṇṭaṉar.
ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavēṉum, vērpporuḷ cilavaṟṟil viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum; cilavaṟṟil viḷaṅkit tōṉṟātu. ati āyntē kāṇal vēṇṭum. itaṉiyē,
``moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā''	(uriyiyal, 96)
eṉṟu kuṟittatu tolkāppiyam''	
e-ṭu:
cul-cuḷ-cuṭu-cuṭal-cuṭali (viḻippat tōṉṟal)
pul-puḷ-puḻu-puḻal-puṭal-puṭali (viḻippat tōṉṟāmi)
kālañ ceṉṟa viyāpuriyār, pūṉi taṉ kaṇṇi mūṭikkoṇṭu ulaka muḻutum iruṇṭuviṭṭateṉṟu koṇṭāṟpōla, tam aṟiyāmiyit tolkāppiyar mītum ēṟṟi, ``Tolkappiyar only says that the origin of words is beyond ascertinment'' eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭār.
vaṭa moḻiyeṉṉum camaṟkirutattil ainti lirupakuti tamiḻāta lāl, ati maṟittaṟporuṭṭup pala vaṭacoṟkaṭkup poruntap poyttalākavum poruntāp poyttalākavum poruḷ kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.
e-ṭu :
teṉcol	vaṭacol	vaṭavarkūṟum
		mūlapporuḷ
iñcivēr 	cruṅkavēra	(māṉ) kompupōṉṟa 					vaṭivuṭiyatu.
ulaku-ulakam	lōka	pārkkappaṭuvatu.
muttu-muttam	mukta	(cippiyiṉiṉṟu) viṭutali 					peṟṟatu.
vaṭam-vaṭavi 	paṭapā	peṭṭik kutiri mukattil
	(mukam)		tōṉṟiyatu (ūḻittī)
``kālam ulakam uyirē uṭampē
pālvari teyvam viṉiyē pūtam
ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ coleṉa varūum
āyī rintoṭu piṟavum aṉṉa
āvayiṉ varūuṅ kiḷavi yellām
pālpirin ticiyā vuyartiṇi mēṉa''	(kiḷaviyākkam, 58)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāviṉ poruḷiyuṇarntu uṇmi teḷika.
3. 	camaṟkirutattit tākkātu tamiḻi vaḷarttal iyalāteṉpatu
tamiḻ civamatamum tirumāl matamum tōṉṟiya oppuyarvaṟṟa ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyā yiruntum, vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉa kīḻiyāriyam kaṭaliṟ kāya muraciṉatu pōl vaṭanāṭṭup pirākirutattoṭu kalantu pōṉataṉālākiya vēta moḻiyi, tamiḻoṭu kalantākkiya oru kālum ulaka vaḻakkilillāta ilakkiya naṭimoḻiyākiya camaṟkirutatti, tēvamoḻiyeṉṟu ēmāṟṟi, vaḻipāṭṭiṟkut takāta iḻintamoḻiyeṉṟu tamiḻit taḷḷi, ataṟkut talimāṟākat tirukkōvil vaḻipāṭṭu moḻiyākavum tirumaṇak karaṇamuḷḷiṭṭa caṭaṅku moḻiyākavum, kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭāka āṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar āriyap pūcāriyar.
mutaṉ mutal tōṉṟiya kumarināṭṭut tamiḻeḻuttu, acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmiyeḻuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
aṭippaṭit tamiḻccoṟka ḷellām āriyac collākak kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ici nāṭakam kaṇiyam maruttuvam mutaliya tamiḻaṟiviyalka ḷellām, āriya vaṇṇamākkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iruvaki aṟaneṟiyum araciyal muṟiyum immiyiṉpamum kūṟum ulakap potumaṟiyām tirukkuṟaḷi, iṟantupaṭṭatākak kūṟum tirivarkkattiṉ moḻipeyarppeṉṟum, taruma cāttiram arutta cāttiram kāma cūttiram ākiya vaṭa nūlkaḷit taḻuviya teṉṟum, añcātu alappi varukiṉṟaṉar.
aivaki yilakkaṇamuṅ kūṟum tolkāppiyatti, eḻuttum collumē kūṟum pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷiyum pāṇiṉīyattiyum piṉpaṟṟiyateṉṟu pitaṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.
meypporuḷ tirippālum toṉmak katikaḷālum civamatamum tirumāl matamum āriyamākkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻar kaṇṭa aṟamporu ḷiṉpa vīṭeṉṉum nāṟporuṭ pākupāṭu, `tarmārtta kāmamōṭca' eṉṉum āriyac coṟṟoṭariṉ moḻipeyarppākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tamiḻaṉ piṟappiṟ pirāmaṇaṉukkut tāḻntavaṉeṉṟum, at tāḻvu maṟumiyiltāṉ nīṅkumeṉṟum kūṟum neñcat timirum vāykkoḻuppum veḷippaṭiyāka iruntuvarukiṉṟaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam, aṭimutal muṭivari, tamiḻmoḻi yilakkiya nākarikap paṇpāṭaṉittum tarukkap poruḷākkappaṭṭiruppatāl, camaṟkirutat tiṟkum tamiḻukkum iṭippaṭṭa uṟavu, tākkuvōṉukkum taṟkāp pōṉukkum iṭippaṭṭatākum.
ākavē, aṭimiyarum aṟiviliyarum kōḻiyaruṅ kōṭaṉ mārumā yirātu, uṇmiyi eṭutturittut tām iḻantavuyarvi mīḷap peṟuvatē uyartiṇi makkaṭkuriya paṇpām.
``vēḷāṇmi ceytu viruntōmpi veñcamattu
vāḷāṇmi yālum valiyarāyt - tāḷāṇmi
tāḻkkum maṭikō ḷilarāy varuntātār
vāḻkki tiruntuta liṉṟu.''	(paḻa. 151)
- ilaṇṭaṉ tamiḻccaṅka āṇṭu malar 1972-73
5
tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyum muṉmiyum
iv vulaka moḻikaḷ ēṟattāḻa mūvāyiram. avaṟṟuḷ, tamiḻē toṉmiyum, muṉmiyum, tāymiyum, talimiyum vāyntateṉpatu, uṇmiyāṉa ārāycciyāḷarellārālum aṭikkaṭi collavum eḻutavumpaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.
ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil mutal mutalākat tamiḻiyum, tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiyum tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntu niliyāka ārāyntu, tām kaṇṭa allatu tamakkut tōṉṟiya muṭivukaḷi ulakiṟkeṭuttuk kūṟiya periyār kālṭuvel mēṟkāṇiyār. avar kālattil, kaṭikkaḻaka ilakkiyamum tolkāppiyamum tamiḻp pulavarkkē teriyātavāṟu maṟintu kiṭantaṉa; maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ pōṉṟa taṉittamiḻp pulavar illi; talikkaḻakak kumarināṭṭip paṟṟi oruvarkkum oṉṟun teriyātu; kā.cu.pōṉṟa ārāycciyāḷarumilli; nayaṉmik kaṭci tōṉṟāmiyāl iṉa vuṇarcciyum oruvar uḷḷattilum eḻavilli; ellāt tuṟiyilum tamiḻar tammip pirāmaṇarkkut tāḻntavarāka veḷippaṭiyākak kāṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. tamiḻar veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantavareṉṟu tām nampiyata ṉālum, ellāt tuṟiyilum pirāmaṇar mēmpaṭṭiruntataṉālum, intiya nākarikattik kāṭṭum ilakkiyam iṉṟu camaṟkirutattilēyē uṇmi yālum tamiḻar vaṭacoṟkaḷi virumpik kalantu tamiḻik kalavi moḻiyākavē vaḻaṅkiyataṉālum, tamiḻa nākarikattiṉ uyarnta kūṟu āriyar kaṇṭateṉṟu kālṭuvelār tavaṟākak kūṟa nērntuviṭṭatu. āyiṉum, tamiḻ āriyattiṟku muntiṉateṉṉum uṇmimaṭṭum, avar kaṇṇiṟkut tappamuṭiyātu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiviṭṭatu. āṉālum, tamiḻ māperuñcol vaḷattateṉṟum, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻit tuṇi yiṉṟit taṉittu vaḻaṅkakkūṭiyateṉṟum kaṇṭaṟintār. iv vīruṇmi kaḷiyum paṟṟi avar kūṟiyiruppaṉa varumāṟu:
“tiraviṭa moḻik kuṭumpatti, intō airōppiyat tokutiyi yum cittiyat tokutiyiyum iṇikkum aṇṭāka (vaḷiyamāka) maṭṭumaṉṟi cila kūṟukaḷil, ciṟappāka mūviṭac cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ toṭarpāka, māntaṉ moḻi varalāṟṟil, intō - airōppiya nilikku muntiyatum, cittiya nilikku muntiyatum, av viraṇṭum oṉṟiliruntu oṉṟu pirivataṟku muntiyatumāṉa, oru kāla nilimiyik kāṭṭi eñci niṟkum taliciṟanta paṭinikariyākavum (cunayīcanaḷanavēyaviena) karuta ētu viruppatākat tōṉṟavilliyā?’``cila tiraviṭac col vaṭivukaḷum vērkaḷum māntariṉ mutaṟ peṟṟōriṉiṉṟu mutaṟkālat tiraviṭarkku vaḻivaḻiyāka iṟaṅkivantuḷḷa moḻiyiyaṟ pēṟṟiṉ oru kūṟāka amiyāvā eṉpatu vēṟoru viṉā. eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, attakiya moḻiyiyal vaḻimuṟip pēṟṟiṉ taṭaṅkaḷi, mika eḷiyaṉavum mikat tēviyāṉavum ataṉāl mika muntiyaṉavumāṉa moḻik kūṟukaḷāṉa curuṅkiya cūḻaliltāṉ kāṇa vēṇṭiyavaṉāka irukkiṉṟēṉ.''
``eṅṅaṉamum, uḷḷiruntu tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum mika uyarvākap paṇpaṭuttap peṟṟuḷḷa tamiḻ vēṇṭumeṉiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyi muṟṟum vilakkit taṉittu vaḻaṅka maṭṭumaṉṟu, taḻittōṅkavuñ ceyyum.''
``.... tiraviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam, camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiṉavum mikat toṉmi vāyntaṉavumāṉa kūṟukaḷip pōṟṟik koṇṭuḷḷatu. ciṟappāka, ataṉ cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷ camaṟkirutattiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koḷḷap peṟāmal antap paḻiya yāppēttiya aṭikaḷi ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. av vaṭikaḷiṉiṉṟē, camaṟkirutattilum āriyak kuṭumpattic cērnta vēṟu pala moḻikaḷilum uḷḷa cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ tirintuḷḷaṉa.''
iṅṅaṉam kālṭuvelārē ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tiṭṭavaṭṭamākavum teḷḷat teḷivākavum kūṟiyirukka, aṇmiyil tiruvaṉantapurattil naṭipeṟṟa tiraviṭa moḻiyārāycci mānāṭṭil, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟit taṉittu vaḻaṅkaviyalāteṉṟum, ataṉ maṭiyilēyē vaḷarntu vantateṉṟum 542 tiraviṭac coṟkaḷē vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷaṉa veṉṟum, vaṅkanāṭṭup pirāmaṇarum vaṭamoḻi veṟiyarum tamiḻp pakivarāṉa teṉṉāṭṭup pirāmaṇar tamiḻippaṟṟi āṅkilattil tavaṟāka eḻutiyuḷḷavaṟṟip paṭittut tamiḻaṟintavarumāṉa par. caṭṭarci tuṇintu kūṟiyuḷḷār. atiyē, par. te.po.mī.yārum piṟa viyā purikaḷum pārāṭṭik kēṭṭukkoṇṭiruntirukkiṉṟaṉar.
tiruvaṉantapurattuk kēraḷap palkalikkaḻakam maṭṭumaṉṟi, tamiḻ nāṭṭu mup palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷum iṉṟu caṭṭarciyiyum, te.po.mī. yāriyum piṉpaṟṟi niṟkiṉṟaṉa. itaṟku aṅkuḷḷa talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyaṉmārē aṭippaṭik karaṇiyam.
camaṟkirutam mēliyāriyamum, pirākirutamum tamiḻuṅkalanta kalavi moḻiyeṉṟum atil aintiliraṇṭu paṅku tamiḻeṉṉum uṇmiyaṟika.
- mutaṉmoḻi 2.7.1971
6
tamiḻum tirāviṭamum teṉmoḻiyum
tamiḻ, tiraviṭam, teṉmoḻi eṉṉum muppeyarum oruporuṭ coṟkaḷāyt  tamiḻiyē neṭuṅkālam kuṟittu vantiruppiṉum, iṉṟi nilikkēṟpa, tamiḻiṉ mūvēṟu nilikaḷi uṇarttaṟkuriyaṉavāy uḷḷaṉa.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻmakkaḷuḷ oru cārārum avar vaḻiyiṉarum makkaṭ perukkam, kaṭalkōḷ mutaliya karaṇiyam (kāraṇam) paṟṟi mella mellap paṭippaṭiyāyp paṉimali (imayam) vari paṭarntu paraviya piṉ, vēṅkaṭak kōṭṭattiṟku vaṭakkiluḷḷa nāvalantēyap pakuti, taṭpa veppanili, uṇṭi, paḻakkavaḻakkam, cuṟṟuc cārpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vēṟu pāṭṭiṉālum, pāṇṭināṭṭut toṭarpiṉmiyālum, nāḷaṭivil ciṟitu ciṟitāy vaṭakku nōkki moḻi peyar tēyamāy māṟiṟṟu. aṅkuc cen tamiḻp pulavarum avarip pōṟṟum cīriya puravalarum iṉmiyāl makkaḷ moḻiyuṇarcciyum palukkal (uccarippu) muyaṟciyum kuṉṟi muṉṉōr moḻiyip palvēṟu vakiyil tirittum citittum pēcalāyiṉar. ataṉ piṉ tiriyāt tamiḻ centamiḻ eṉṟum, tirinta tamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari āyiṭit
tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
eṉṟu ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatākiya tolkāppiyattiṟkuc ciṟappup pāyiram iyaṟṟiya paṉampāraṉār kūṟuvatāl, tolkāppiyar kālattilum vēṅkaṭam (tiruppati) variyum centamiḻ nāṭum ataṉ vaṭakkē koṭuntamiḻ nāṭum paraviyiruntami peṟappaṭum.
teṉ peruvāriyil muḻukip pōṉa kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻnilam muḻuvatum paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭātalālum, tamiḻ tōṉṟi vaḷarntatum muttamiḻk kaḻakamum niṟuvappeṟṟatum tamiḻnāṭeṉac ciṟappittu collap paṭuvatum pāṇṭināṭē yātalālum. iṉṟum tamiḻ teṟku nōkkiyē ciṟantu teṉkōṭiyil tūymi mikkiruppatālum, kumarikkaṇṭam muḻukum muṉ vēṅkaṭa mutal teṉpālimukam variyum (ēṟattāḻa 2500 kal tolivu) centamiḻ nāṭāyē iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. iṉi, intiyāviṉ vaṭamēṟkil pirākuvī eṉṉum tiraviṭa moḻiyum vaṭakiḻakkil māler (māliṟṟō) eṉṉum tiraviṭa moḻiyum iṉṟum pēcappaṭuvatālum, vaṭa intiya āriyamoḻikaḷākak karutappaṭum inti, vaṅkam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭi tiraviṭamā yiruppatālum, nēpāḷa moḻiyil cila coṟkaḷ iyaṟcollāy vaḻaṅkuvatālum, kuccaram (kucarātti), marāṭṭi (makārāṭṭiram) ākiya viraṇṭum paḻam pañca tiraviṭattiṟ cērkkap peṟṟiruntamiyālum, ceṉṉiyiliruntu vaṅkam variyum kīḻiyintiyāvil tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ toṭarntu pēcap peṟuvatālum, vintiyatti yaṭutta naṭuvintiyāvilum kōṇṭi mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatālum, vēta kālattil cōṭar eṉṉum teluṅkac cōḻar kaṅkik kariyil āṇṭu vantatākac collap paṭuvatālum, vēṅkaṭattiliruntu paṉimali variyum (ēṟattāḻa 2000 kal tolivu) koṭuntamiḻ nāṭā yiruntatākavum terikiṉṟatu.
mutaṉmutal tamiḻarāṟ pirittuṇarap peṟṟa koṭuntamiḻ moḻi vaṭuku allatu vaṭukam eṉṉum teluṅkē. vaṭuku eṉpatu vaṭaku eṉpataṉ uyiricivu māṟṟat (Harmonic Sequence of Vowels) tiripu. vaṭakkil vaḻaṅkuvatāl vaṭaku eṉappaṭṭatu. vakaram kaṉṉaṭattil pakaramākat tirital iyalpātalāl, teluṅka nāṭṭiyaṭuttu vāḻnta orucār kaṉṉaṭiyar nīla maliyiṟ kuṭiyēṟiyapiṉ paṭakar (vaṭakar) eṉappaṭṭaṉar. vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu vanta karunaṭar vaṭakar.
coṉmutal uyirmeyyiṉiṉṟu uyiri nīkki rakara mēṟṟum vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkiṟkēṟpa, tamiḻ allatu tamiḻam eṉṉum col vaṭanāṭṭil mutaṟkaṇ `tramiḷa' eṉṟu tirintu, piṉṉar muṟiyē tramiṭa - traviṭa - trāviṭa - trāviṭī eṉa vaṭivu peṟalāyiṟṟu. ḻakaram piṟamoḻik kiṉmiyāl, vaṭanāṭṭil ḷakaramākat tirital iyalpē.
koṭuntamiḻ moḻikaḷ centamiḻiṉiṉṟu vaṭanāṭṭārāl pirit tuṇarappaṭumuṉ, traviṭa eṉṉum peyar tamiḻukkiṉamāṉa moḻikaḷi yellām tamiḻuḷḷēyē aṭakkiṟṟu. ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa kumārilapaṭṭar, teluṅkit tamiḻiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭutti, tamiḻum ataṉ iṉa moḻikaḷum cērnta tokutiyi āntira - tirāviṭap - pāci (āntra - trāviṭa pāṣā) eṉṉum iṇimoḻip peyarāṟ kuṟittaṉar. tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, marāṭṭi, kucarātti ākiya aimmoḻi nāṭukaḷum, vaṭavarāl muṟiyē tirāviṭam, āntiram, kaṉṉaṭam, makārāṭṭiram, kūrccaram eṉṉum pañca tirāviṭam eṉa aḻikkappeṟṟaṉa. marāṭṭiyum kucarāttiyum iṉṟu āriya moḻikaḷāy māṟiviṭṭaṉa. pañca tirāviṭam eṉṉum pākupāṭu atāvatu marāṭṭiyum kucarāttiyum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷōṭu cērkkap peṟṟami. oru kālattil iv viru moḻikaḷum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷāy iruntamiyi uṇarttum itu, tamiḻ tiraviṭa        māyum. tiraviṭam āriyamāyum tirintamikkum, āriyam paravap paravat tiraviṭap parappum tiraviṭam paravap paravat tamiḻp parappum kuṉṟi varuvataṟkum, oru cāṉṟām. tamiḻ eṉiṉum centamiḻ eṉiṉum oṉṟē.
centamiḻiṉiṉṟu koṭuntamiḻ allatu koṭuntamiḻkaḷ pirittuṇarappaṭu muṉpum, pirittuṇarappaṭṭapiṉpum, tamiḻit tirāviṭam eṉṉum peyarālēyē vaṭavar kuṟittu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. vēta kālattu vaṭṭāra moḻikaḷākiya pirākirutaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa tamiḻ tirāviṭī eṉappaṭṭatu. āntira - tirāviṭap pāci eṉṉum kūṭṭup peyarilum, pañca tirāviṭap peyarkaḷuḷḷum tamiḻ tirāviṭam eṉṟē kuṟikkap peṟṟatu. ki.pi.5ām nūṟṟāṇṭil maturiyil putta neṟiyiṉarāl niṟuvap peṟṟa tamiḻk kaḻakam, tamiḻc caṅkam eṉappaṭṭatu. iṟṟikku ēṟattāḻa 480 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpirunta piḷḷi lōkārya cīyar tamiḻnūlit tirāviṭa cāttiram (trāviṭa cāttaram) eṉṟaṉar. ki.pi.18ām nūṟṟāṇṭi lirunta tūya tamiḻarāṉa tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷum vaṭanūl vaḻakkiyoṭṭi.
``vallā ṉoruttaṉ varavuntirā viṭattilē
vantatā vivakarip pēṉ''
eṉṟār. itaṉāl tamiḻ (tamiḻam) eṉṉum peyarē vaṭamoḻiyil tirāviṭam eṉat tirintuḷḷami tēṟṟam.
``centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi''	(883)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷip paṉṉiraṇṭākak kūṟiṉar. āyiṉ, avi evi eṉak kuṟittilar. avar kālattil vēṅkaṭam cen tamiḻnāṭṭu vaṭavelliyā yiruntamiyālum, ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari yirunta kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilum vēṅkaṭam vari tamiḻakamā yiruntamiyālum, tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟikkappeṟṟa paṉṉiru koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷum vēṅkaṭattiṟku appāṟpaṭṭavi yāyē iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. tamiḻilakkaṇa uriyāciriyarellām ki.pi.11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭavarātalāl, paṇṭi varalāṟṟi yaṟiyāmal tam kāla nilikkēṟpa,
``teṉpāṇṭi kuṭṭaṅ kuṭaṟkaṟkā vēṇpūḻi
paṉṟi yaruvā ataṉvaṭakku - naṉṟāya
cīta malāṭu puṉaṉāṭu centamiḻcēr
ētamil paṉṉirunāṭ ṭeṇ''
eṉak koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷip piḻipaṭak kuṟittaṉar.
iṉi, tolkāppiyattiṟku orumaruṅku māṟāka,
``centamiḻ nilañcēr paṉṉiru nilattiṉum
oṉpatiṟ ṟiraṇṭiṉil tamiḻoḻi nilattiṉum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccol eṉpa''	(naṉ. 273)
eṉak ki.pi.12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa naṉṉūlār mikipaṭak kūṟiyatum vaḻuvuṟṟatē.
tamiḻiyum ataṉ iṉamoḻikaḷiyum mottam patiṉmūṉṟeṉa mutaṉmutaṟ kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār kuṟittaṉar. iṉṟu amerikkap pērāciriyar emaṉōvum, āṅkilap pērāciriyar parōvum avaṟṟip pattoṉpatākak kāṭṭiṉar. it tuṟiyārāycciyit toṭaṅki vitta kālṭuvelār kālattil, tamiḻi ataṉ iṉamoḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu pirit tuṇarun tēviyē yiṉmiyāl, avar av viru pāliyum tirāviṭam eṉṉum potuppeyarāṟ kuṟittup pōntār. iṉṟō, iv vārāycci mikuntu tamiḻarum āḻntu īṭupaṭṭut tamiḻiṉ taṉittaṉmiyit teḷivāy uṇarntiruppatālum moḻivārip pitira (mākāṇa)p piriviṉāl teṉṉāṭṭut tamiḻiṉap perumoḻikaḷ nila vakiyiṟ pirintu pōṉamiyālum, intiyip potumoḻiyāy ēṟṟukkoḷvatupaṟṟit tamiḻarkkum āntira kaṉṉaṭa maliyāḷiyarkkum nērmāṟāṉa nilimi vēṟupāṭiruppatālum, tamiḻi ataṉ iṉamoḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu pirittuc cuṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟa tēvi ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉāl, tamiḻit tamiḻ eṉṟum, ataṉ iṉamoḻikaḷiyē tiraviṭam eṉṟum, iv virupāli yum potuppaṭat teṉmoḻi eṉṟum kūṟuvatē iṉit takkatām. centamiḻ, koṭuntamiḻ eṉṉum pākupāṭu toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻakki liruntu varuvatālum, centamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirinta koṭun tamiḻ moḻikaḷit tamiḻ eṉṉum centamiḻc colliṉ tiripāṉa tiraviṭam eṉṉum tiricollāṟ kuṟippatē takkatāmātalālum, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷi yellān taḻuvum vaṭamoḻiyeṉṉum peyarpōl teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷiyellān taḻuvuvatu teṉmoḻi eṉṉum peyarēyātalālum, tamiḻ, tiraviṭam, teṉmoḻi eṉṉum muppeyarkkum pakuttotukkiya poruṭpāṭu ellā vakiyilum ēṟṟuḷḷamiyaṟintu kaṭippiṭikka.
- ``teṉmoḻi'' pippiravari 1963
7
tamiḻ vēṟu, tiraviṭam vēṟu
tamiḻum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷum ōriṉamāyiṉum mika vēṟupaṭṭavi eṉpatip palar aṟintilar.
centamiḻum koṭuntamiḻum
kumarikkaṇṭattil tōṉṟi vaḷarntu pallūḻikaḷākap paṇpaṭuttap paṭṭa tamiḻ, mutaṟkālattu evvaki aṭimoḻiyum peṟātu tamiḻ eṉṟē vaḻaṅkiyiruppiṉum, iṭik kālattup pulavarālum aracarālum pōṟṟap peṟāta nāṭukaḷil palvakiyil tirintu koṭuntamiḻ eṉap peyar peṟṟapiṉ, oppu nōkkiya muṟiyil, centamiḻ eṉa iṉac cuṭṭup paṇpukoḷ peyarkkoṭi peṟṟatu.
centamiḻc coṟkaḷ, colliyal (Etymology) muṟipaṟṟi (1). iyaṟcol, (2) tiricol eṉa iru pālākap pakukkap peṟṟaṉa. iyaṟ collāvatu iyalpāṉa muntunilic col (Primitive); tiricollāvatu, ataṉiṉṟu tirikkappaṭṭa col (Derivative). veḷ eṉpatu ōr iyaṟ col. veḷḷam, veḷḷanti, veḷḷi, veḷḷil, veḷḷi, veḷi, veḷil, veḷiṟu,veḷu, veḷku (veṭku), veṭṭi, veṟu mutaliyaṉa ataṉiṉṟu tirikkappaṭṭa tiricoṟkaḷ ivvāṟu, coṟkaḷ meṉmēlum paṉmaṭiyākat tiriyumātalāl, iṉṉatu iyaṟcolleṉṟum iṉṉatu tiricol leṉṟum variyaṟuttuk kūṟa iyalāteṉavum, orē col taṉ pakutiyi nōkkat tiricollum taṉ tiripi nōkka iyaṟcollumāku meṉavum aṟintukoḷka. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka maṇal eṉṉum col maṇ eṉpati nōkkat tiricollum, maṇali eṉpati nōkka iyaṟcol lum, ātal kāṇka. iv viḷakkam tolkāppiyar kālattilēyē maṟintu pōṉatākat terikiṉṟatu.
koṭuntamiḻc colliyalpu palvakippaṭṭatēṉum, coṟṟiripu poruṭṭiripu, ceyyuṭ col vaḻakku, putuc collākkam eṉa nāl vakiyuḷ aṭakkappeṟum.
e-kā.
tamiḻ 	teluṅku
pōyiṉāṉ	pōyiṉāṭu	-	coṟṟiripu
ceppu (viṭicol)	ceppu (col)	-	poruṭṭiripu
mūṅkil	veturu (vetir)	-	ceyyuṭ col
	tūṭa (kaṉṟu)	-	putuccol
ivaṟṟuḷ poruḷ tiricollum ceyyuṭ collum putuc collum ticiccol eṉappaṭum.
nālvaki ilakkiyaccol
tolkāppiyar, tolkāppiyattu āḷappaṭṭa niccal, cāti, viciyaṉ, cūttiram eṉṉum coṟkaḷ pōl akkālat tamiḻilakkiyattil vēṇṭātu pukuttappaṭṭa orucila vaṭacoṟkaḷik kaṇṭu, aviyum tami ḻukku vēṇṭuvaṉavākap piḻipaṭak koṇṭu, tamiḻilakkiyac coṟkaḷi iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticiccol, vaṭacol eṉa nāṉkāka vakuttu.
``iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticiccol, vaṭacoleṉ
ṟaṉittē ceyyuḷ īṭṭac collē.''	(880)
``avaṟṟuḷ
iyaṟcoṟ ṟāmē
centamiḻ nilattu vaḻakkoṭu civaṇit
tamporuḷ vaḻāmi icikkuñ collē.''	(881)
``oruporuḷ kuṟitta vēṟucol lākiyum
vēṟuporuḷ kuṟitta orucol lākiyum
irupāṟ ṟeṉpa tiricoṟ kiḷavi.''	(882)
``centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi.''	(883)
``vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē.''	(884)
eṉa nūṟpā iyaṟṟiṉār.
ivaṟṟuḷ, iyaṟcol eṉpaṉa centamiḻ nāṭṭu vaḻakkoṭu poruntit tattam poruḷit tappātu uṇarttum col eṉṟār iḷampūraṇarum teyvacciliyārum. centamiḻ nāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟik koṭuntamiḻ nāṭṭilum tappātu poruḷuṇarttuvaṉa eṉṟār, cēṉāvariyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum. iv īruriyum ēṟattāḻa oṉṟē. āyiṉ, centamiḻnilat telliyip paṟṟit teyvacciliyār oruvarē ōraḷavu teḷinta aṟivuṭiyavarākat tōṉṟukiṉṟār. piṟarellām,
``centamiḻ nilamāvatu, viyi yāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum marutayāṟṟiṉ teṟkum karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum maruvūriṉ mēṟkum'' eṉa urippa, ivar maṭṭum.
``ivvāṟu urittaṟku, ōr ilakkaṇaṅ kāṇāmiyāṉum; viyi yāṟṟiṉ teṟkākiya koṟkiyum, karuvūriṉ mēṟkākiya koṭuṅ kōḷūrum, maruta yāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkākiya kāñciyum, tamiḻtiri nilamātal vēṇṭumātalāṉum; aḵtu uriyaṉṟu eṉpar urikkumāṟu:
``vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
āyiṭit tamiḻkūṟu, nallulakattu
vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum āyiru mutaliṉ
eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭi''
eṉṟamiyāṉum, itaṉuḷ tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakam eṉa vicēṭittami yāṉum. kiḻakkum mēṟkum elli kūṟātu teṟkelli kūṟiyavataṉāṟ kumariyiṉ teṟkākiya nāṭukaḷi yoḻittu vēṅkaṭa maliyiṉ teṟkum, kumariyiṉ vaṭakkum, kuṇakaṭaliṉ mēṟkum, kuṭa kaṭaliṉ kiḻakkumākiya nilam centamiḻ nilameṉṟurippa'' eṉa varintiruttal kāṇka.
pāṇṭināṭṭit tamiḻnāṭeṉṟum pāṇṭiyaṉit tamiḻ nāṭaṉeṉṟum uriccoṟṟokutikaḷ (nikaṇṭukaḷ) vitantu kūṟuvatālum, tamiḻ vaḷartta mukkaḻakaṅkaḷum viyikkut teṟkēyē iruntami yālum 
``................................ nallaravap
pāṭṭuṭittu cōmaṉ vaḻivanta pāṇṭiyaniṉ
nāṭṭuṭittu nalla tamiḻ''
eṉṟu auviyār pāṭiyiruppatālum, iṉṟum viyikkut teṟkiluḷḷa nelli māvaṭṭattil nalla tamiḻ vaḻaṅkuvatālum, centamiḻ nilatti viyikku vaṭakkeṉak kūṟuvatu uriyāciriyar aṟiyāmiyiyē uṇarttu meṉka.
tiricoṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟap pukunta tolkāppiyar, oruporuḷ kuṟitta vēṟu col, vēṟuporuḷ kuṟitta oru col eṉat tiricolli iraṇṭākap pakuttaṉar. ataṟku ilakkaṇam kūṟiṟṟilar. oruporuṭ pala col, palaporu ḷorucol, eṉṉum iraṭṭip pakuppu, peyar viṉi yiṭiyuri eṉṉum nāṉkiṟkum iyaṟcol mutaliya nāṉkiṟkum potuvātaliṉ, itu tiricolliṉ ilakkaṇamākāmi kāṇka. iyaṟ collit ``tamporuḷ vaḻāmi yicikkuñ col'' eṉṟu tol kāppiyar kūṟiyatiṉālum, uriyāciriyarellām aruñcoṟkaḷiyē tiricollāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyamiyālum, naṉṉūlār, eḷituṇar poruḷaṉa iyaṟcol eṉṟum arituṇar poruḷaṉa tiricolleṉṟum kūṟip pōntār. ik karuttiṟku iyalpāyp poruḷuṇarttum col iyaṟcol, avvāṟu poruḷuṇarttum taṉmiyiṉiṉṟu tirinta col tiricol eṉpaṉa poruḷām āyiṉ,
``kiḷḷi. maññi eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉa oru kūṟu niṟpa oru kūṟu tirintaṉa. unti, aṭukkal eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉa muḻuvatūum vēṟupaṭat tirintaṉa eṉṟu iḷampūraṇarum,
``tiricollatu tirivāvatu uṟupput tiritalum muḻuvatun tiritalumāṉa iruvakittu, kiḷḷi. maññi eṉpaṉa uṟupput tirintaṉa; vilaṅkal, viṇṭu eṉpaṉa muḻutun tirintaṉa.'' eṉṟu cēṉāvariyarum,
``av viyaṟ collit tirikkuṅkāl tam eḻuttuc ciṟitu niṟpat tirippaṉavum av viyaṟcoṟṟammiyē piṟacoṟ koṇarntu muḻuvatūun tirippaṉavum eṉa iru vakiyavām'' eṉṟu nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum,
``ivvāṟu tirintu varutaliṟ ṟiricollāyiṟṟu'' eṉṟu teyvac ciliyārum, urittiruppatiṉiṉṟu iyaṟcol eṉpatu muntunilic col (Primitive) eṉṟum, tiricol eṉpatu ataṉiṉṟu tirikkappaṭṭa col (Derivative) eṉṟum , mutaṟkaṇ poruḷpaṭṭirukkalā meṉṟu uyttuṇaraṟ kiṭamuṇmi kāṇka.
ticiccol eṉpatu koṭuntamiḻc col. koṭuntamiḻ nāṭu centamiḻ nāṭṭic cērnta allatu cūḻnta paṉṉiraṇṭeṉat tol kāppiyar toki kuṟittaṉarēyaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ peyar kūṟiṟṟilar. uriyāciriyar perumpālum 10 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭavarātaliṉ, avaruṭ palar tolkāppiyar karuttiyum paṇṭit tamiḻaka elli yiyum uṇarātu, taṅkālattiṟ kēṟpa,
``centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa:
1. potuṅkar (poṅkar)	7. cītam
2. teṉpāṇṭi	8. pūḻi
3. oḷi	9. mali (maliyamāṉ)
4. kuṭṭam	10. aruvā 
5. paṉṟi	11. aruvā vaṭatali
6. kaṟkā	12. kuṭam
eṉa ivi'' eṉṟurippar.
ivaṟṟic centamiḻ nāṭṭut teṉkīḻpāl mutalāka vaṭakīḻiṟutiyāka eṇṇikkoḷḷac colvar cēṉāvariyarum nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum.
piṟkālattār, potuṅkar, oḷi eṉṉum iraṇṭi nīkki avaṟṟukkup patilāka vēṇ, cōḻam eṉṉum iraṇṭic cērppar.
teyvacciliyār koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷip paṟṟi, `` paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa................. (poṅkar ) nāṭu, ......................... aruvāvaṭatali'' eṉpa, ivi centamiḻ nāṭṭakatta. centamiḻ nāṭeṉṟamiyāl, piṟa nāṭākal vēṇṭumeṉpār utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭumāṟu: ``kaṉṉit teṉkarik kaṭaṟ paḻantīpam kollaṅ kūpakañ ciṅkaḷameṉṉum elliyiṉ puṟattavum, kaṉṉaṭam vaṭukam kaliṅkam teliṅkam koṅkaṇam tuḷuvam kuṭakam kuṉṟakam eṉpaṉa kuṭapālirupuṟac ciyattuṭaṉuṟipu kūrun tamiḻ tiri nilaṅkaḷum, muṭiyuṭiyava riṭunilavāṭciyiṉ aracu mēmpaṭṭa kuṟunilak kuṭumikaḷ patiṉmarum uṭaṉirup piruvarumākiya paṉṉiruvar aracarum paṭitta paṉṉiru tēyattiṉum, tamiḻc collātaṟku viruppuṭiyaṉa'' eṉṟamiyāṉum; ``tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu, vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu māyiru mutaliṉ, eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭi'' eṉa niṟuttup piṉṉum ``centamiḻiyaṟki civaṇiya nilattoṭu muntunūl kaṇṭu'' eṉa ōtiyavataṉāṟ civaṇiya nilamāvatu elli kuṟitta nilattic cārnta nilameṉa vēṇṭutalāṉum; paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa;- kumariyāṟṟiṉ teṉkarippaṭṭa paḻantīpamum kollamum kūpakamum ciṅkaḷamum, ciyattiṉ mēṟkuppaṭṭa koṅkaṇamum tuḷuvamum kuṭakamum kuṉṟaka mum,  kiḻakkuppaṭṭa karunaṭamum vaṭukum teluṅkum kaliṅkamum, eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
``ivaṟṟuḷ, kūpakamuṅ kollamuṅ kaṭal koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ, kumariyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakariyik kollameṉak kuṭiyēṟiṉār pōlum. pañca tirāviṭameṉavum vaṭanāṭṭār urippavākalāṉ. aviyintum vēṅkaṭattiṉ teṟkātaluṅ kūṭāmiyuṇarka'' eṉa urittiruppatu cālac ciṟantatākum.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ``centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum'' eṉpataṟku, ``centamiḻ nāṭṭic cūḻnta paṉṉiraṇṭu nilattum paṉṉiraṇṭiyum puṟañ cūḻnta paṉṉiraṇṭu nilattum'' eṉap poruḷ kūṟi,
``iṉip paṉṉiraṇṭiyuñ cūḻnta paṉṉiraṇṭāvaṉa: ciṅkaḷamum paḻantīvum  kollamum kūpamum koṅkaṇamum tuḷuvum kuṭakamum karunaṭamum kūṭamum vaṭukum teluṅkum kaliṅkamumām.'' eṉa urit tiruppatu. teyvacciliyārip piṉpaṟṟip pōlum!
iv vurikaḷālum, ticiccoṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟavaṟṟuḷ, vaṭukar collutalic cepputal eṉṟum, karunaṭar akappaṭutalic         cikkutal eṉṟum, tuḷuvar māmarattik kokku eṉṟum, kuṭanāṭṭār tantiyi accaṉ eṉṟum colvar, eṉa varum pakutikaḷāṉum; ticic col eṉpatu koṭuntamiḻc collē yeṉpatum, atu centamiḻ nilattic cūḻap pala ticiyiṉum vaḻaṅkiyatāl ticiccol eṉappaṭṭa teṉpatum, centamiḻ nilam varavarak kuṟukiyum koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷ varavarap palki virintum pōṉatiṉāl, piṟkāla uriyāciriyarkaḷ paṇṭic centamiḻ nilattiṟkuḷḷēyē paṉṉiru koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷiyum vakukka nērntateṉpatum, koṭuntamiḻ nilattu moḻikaḷ piṟkālattut tiripu mikutiyālum āriyac cērkkiyālum kiḷimoḻi niliyuṅ kaṭanta uṭaṉpiṟappu moḻikaḷākavum vēṟṟu moḻikaḷākavum karutappaṭṭatiṉāl,
``centamiḻ nilañcēr paṉṉiru nilattiṉum
oṉpatiṟ ṟiraṇṭiṉil tamiḻoḻi nilattiṉum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccol eṉpa''
eṉa naṉṉūlār nūṟpā yākka nērntuviṭṭa teṉpatum, piṟavum aṟiyappaṭum.
vaṭacol eṉpatu vaṭamoḻic col. iyaṟcol mutaliya nāṉkaṉuḷ iḵtoṉṟē ayaṉmoḻic col.
ākavē, iyaṟcol, tiricol ākiya iraṇṭum centamiḻ eṉṟum, ticiccol eṉpatu koṭuntamiḻ eṉṟum, vaṭacol oṉṟē ayaṟcol leṉṟum, akkālattut tamiḻil kalanta ayaṉmoḻic col vaṭacol oṉṟē yeṉṟum, āṅkilam pōrttukkīciyam mutaliya piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷi avvam moḻip peyarālēyē aḻittal vēṇṭumeṉ ṟum, avaṟṟit ticiccolluḷ aṭakkuvatu vaḻuveṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
tiraviṭam eṉṉum peyarum moḻikaḷum
tamiḻ eṉṉum col, teluṅkam kuṭakam tuḷuvam eṉpaṉa pōl ciṟupāṉmi `am' īṟu peṟṟut tamiḻam eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. kaṭal kōḷukkut tappiya kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻaruḷ orucārār vaṭakkē cellac cella, taṭpaveppanili vēṟupāṭṭālum palukkal (uccarippu). tavaṟṟālum moḻikkāppiṉmiyālum, avar moḻi mella mellat tiraviṭamākat tirintatum. tiraviṭarāka uṭaṉ tirintaṉar. tamiḻ ciṟitu ciṟitākap peyarntu tiraviṭamāyt tirintatiṉālēyē, vaṭanāṭukaḷi vēṟṟu moḻi nāṭeṉṉātu ``moḻipeyar tēyam'' eṉṟaṉar muṉṉōr.
``moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum'' eṉpatu kuṟuntoki (11) tamiḻ tiraviṭa moḻikaḷākat tirinta muṟikkiṇaṅkat tamiḻam eṉṉum peyarum tiramiḷam - tramiṭam - traviṭam eṉat tirintatu.
o.nō: tōṇi - trōṇi (va.), pavaḻam - pravāḷam (va.) pittaḷi - ittaṭi (te.), kumi - kuvi ( ta.).
tamiḻam eṉṉum peyarē tiraviṭam eṉat tirintamiyālum, tiraviṭam eṉac collappaṭum moḻikaḷukkuḷ tamiḻ oṉṟilēyē paṇṭik kālattil ilakkiya miruntamiyālum, muttamiḻ vēntarāṉa cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻakattip pukaḻpeṟa āṇṭu vantamiyālum, mutalāvatu, tirāviṭam eṉṉum peyar tamiḻiyē cuṭṭit tiraviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam muḻuvatiyuṅ kuṟittatu. vētakālattup pirākiruta moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟu trāviṭī eṉappaṭṭatiyum, ainnūṟāṇṭu kaṭku muṉpirunta piḷḷi lōkāryacīyar tamiḻilakkaṇattit `trāviṭa cātram' eṉak kuṟittiruppatiyum, kāṇka.
tiraviṭam eṉappaṭum moḻikaṭkuḷ tamiḻiṉiṉṟu mutaṉmutal piṟarāṟ pirikkappaṭṭatu. teluṅkākum. ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta kumārilapaṭṭar tiraviṭa moḻik kuṭumpatti `āntra - trāviṭa pāṣā' eṉṟār.
vaṭuku eṉṟu tamiḻarāṟ potuvākak kuṟikkappeṟum teluṅku, ki.mu. paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē taṉimoḻiyākap pirintuviṭṭatu. iti,
``vaṭatici maruṅkiṉ vaṭukuvaram pākat 
teṉṟici yuḷḷiṭ ṭeñciya mūṉṟum'',
eṉṉum ciṟukākki pāṭiṉiyār kūṟṟālum, 
``kullik kaṇṇi vaṭukar muṉiyatu
palvēṟ kaṭṭi naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar
moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum''
eṉṉum māmūlaṉār kūṟṟālum, aṟiyalām.
``koṅkaṇar kaliṅkar koṭuṅkaru nāṭar''
eṉṟu cilappatikāram karunāṭakatti oru koṭuntamiḻ nāṭupōṟ kuṟit tiruppatāl, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kālattiṟ kaṉṉaṭam oru naṭi moḻiyāka (Dialect) maṭṭumiruntami aṟiyappaṭum. kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil vēṅkaṭam tamiḻaka vaṭavelliyā yiruntatum iti valiyuṟuttum, piṉpu, 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉ (9ām nūṟṟāṇṭupōl) atu taṉimoḻiyāki viṭṭami,
``vaṭakali teṉkali vaṭuku kaṉṉaṭam
iṭamuḷa pāṭiyā toṉṟi ṉāyiṉum.''
eṉṉum kampar kūṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. itaṉāṟ kampar kālattil mali yāḷam taṉimoḻiyāyp piriyavilli yeṉpatum uṇarttappeṟum.
17ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta tuñcattu eḻuttaccaṉ, `āriya eḻuttu' eṉṉum vaṭaveḻuttuk kalanta neṭuṅkaṇakki maliyāḷattiṟku vakutta piṉṉarē, atu taṉimoḻiyākap pirintatu.
ivaṟṟiṟkiṭiyē vevvēṟu kālattil tuḷu, kuṭakam eṉṉum tiruntiya moḻikaḷum, totuvam, kōttam, kōṇṭi mutaliya tiruntā moḻikaḷum pirintaṉa.
kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār tiraviṭa moḻikaḷip patiṉmūṉṟākak kaṇakkiṭṭār. iṟṟi mēli moḻinūl vallār avaṟṟoṭu parci, kōlāmi, nāyakki, ollāri mutaliyavaṟṟic cērttup pattoṉpatum mēlumākak kāṭṭuvar.
maliyāḷattiṉ nālnilikaḷ
maliyāḷam 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari centamiḻākavum, ataṉ piṉ 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari koṭuntamiḻākavum, ataṉ piṉ 17 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari kiḷimoḻiyākavum, iruntu, piṉpu taṉimoḻiyāyp pirintuviṭṭatu.
maliyāḷattiṉ iṭinilimi
paḻañ cēranāṭṭut tamiḻē palvakiyil tirintum āriyattoṭu kalantum maliyāḷam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatālum, iṉṟum atu āriyac coṟkaḷ nīṅkiṉaviṭattup perumpālum tamiḻākat tirumpuvatālum, paḻañ cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭṭup pakutikaḷākiya iṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkāta paṇṭit tamiḻccoṟkaḷiṟ pala maliyāḷa nāṭṭil muṭaṅkik kiṭappatālum, maliyāḷiyar maṉam vippiṉ iṉṟum kēraḷa (cērala) nāṭu tamiḻnāṭāka māṟum vāyppuṇmiyālum, maliyāḷa moḻi tamiḻiṟkum piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkum iṭippaṭṭa nilimi tāṅkuvatākum.
tamiḻiṉ tūymiyum tiraviṭattiṉ āriyat taṉmiyum
tamiḻ oṉṟē vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟit taṉittiyaṅka valla teṉṟum, tiraviṭa moḻikaḷukkuḷ mikat tiruntiyateṉṟum, mutu toṉmi vāyntateṉṟum, vaḻakkaṟṟa paḻan tiraviṭac coṟkaḷiyum colvaṭivukaḷiyum taṉṉakattuk koṇṭuḷḷateṉṟum, kālṭuvel kūṟiyiruppiṉum, iṉattoṭarpu paṟṟit tamiḻiyum  teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiyum tiraviṭam eṉṉum potuppeyarāṟ kuṟittaṟkuḷḷa tēviyi avar uṇarntārēyaṉṟi, tamiḻiṉiṉṟu piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷi oru taṉiccollāṟ pirittuk kāṭṭaṟkuḷḷa tēviyi uṇarntārallar. ataṟkuriya cūḻniliyum akkālatti lilli.
tamiḻukkum piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭukaḷāvaṉa;
	tamiḻ	piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ
1.	mellōci koṇṭatu	mellōciyum vallōciyum 						koṇṭaṉa.
2.	vaṭacoṟ kalappāl tāḻvatu	vaṭacoṟ kalappāl uyarvaṉa.
	tamiḻ	piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ
3.	āriyaccārpaṟṟa paṇṭi	perumpālum āriyac cārpāṉa
		ilakkaṇavilakkiyamuḷḷatu.		piṟkāla ilakkaṇa vilakkiyamē 					yuḷḷaṉa.
4.	intic coṟkaḷi ēṟkātatu.	intic coṟkaḷi ēṟpaṉa.
5.	tūymi, iyakkam mēṟkoṇṭatu.	tūymiyiyakkam mēṟkoḷḷātaṉa.
itaṉāl, tamiḻ eṉṟum tamiḻākavē niṟka, tiraviṭam tirintu āriyamākavum ceyyum. vaṭintiyāviṟ cila tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ ari yāriyamākavum muḻu āriyamākavum tirintuḷḷaṉa. latā, pariyā eṉṉum iru tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ ari yāriyamum (arit tiraviṭam) alapī eṉpatu muḻu āriyamum āyiṉaveṉṟu, 1906-l intiya moḻik kaṇakkeṭutta kiriyarcaṉ kūṟiyuḷḷār. marāṭṭiyum,kuccaramum (kucarātti) paḻam `pañca tirāviṭa' moḻikaḷuḷ iraṇṭākak koḷḷappaṭṭamiyum, iṉṟu avi āriyattō ṭeṇṇappaṭuvatiyum, nōkkuka.
vantē mātaram, jey hint, namakāram (namatē), vaṉa makōtsavam, pārat cēvak caṅk, ākācavāṇi mutaliya āriya allatu intic coṟkaḷip piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ muḻumaṉamāy varavēṟka, tamiḻ avaṟṟit tāynāṭē pōṟṟi, intiyā (nāvalam) velka. vaṇakkam, maram naṭṭu viḻā, intiya vūḻiyak kaḻakam, vāṉoli eṉa moḻipeyarttē vaḻaṅkum tiṟattatāyiruppataṉālum, pāl tirintu tayirāṉa piṉ mīṇṭum pālākāmi pōl tamiḻt tiripāṉa tiraviṭam mīṇṭum tamiḻākāmi yālum, tamiḻallāta piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiyē tiraviṭam allatu tirāviṭam eṉṟum, tamiḻiyum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiyum oruṅkē tamiḻiyam (Tamulic) eṉṟum, iṉimēl vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum.
tamiḻ, tamiḻaṉ, tamiḻnāṭu
tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ peluccittāṉamum vaṅkāḷamum vari paraviyum citaṟiyum kiṭappatālum, tamiḻum tiraviṭamum oṉṟucēra muṭiyāvāṟu vēṟu paṭṭu viṭṭamiyālum, āntira kaṉṉaṭa kēraḷa nāṭukaḷ taṉi mākāṇaṅka ḷākap pirintu pōṉamiyālum, tiraviṭar tamiḻaroṭu kūṭa virumpāmi yālum, āriyac cārpik kuṟikkum tirāviṭam, tirāviṭaṉ, tirāviṭa nāṭu eṉṉum coṟkaḷi aṟavē yoḻittu, tūymiyuṇarttum tamiḻ, tamiḻaṉ, tamiḻnāṭu eṉṉum coṟkaḷiyē iṉi vaḻaṅkavum muḻaṅkavum vēṇṭum.
tamiḻaṉ, tamiḻaṉallātāṉ
ennāṭṭilum, makkaḷ paṟṟiya iraṭṭip pakuppil, uḷnāṭṭu makkaḷi mutaṟ kuṟippatē marapum muṟimiyumātalāl, iṉit tamiḻnāṭṭu makkaḷiyum tamiḻar, tamiḻarallātār eṉṟē pirittal vēṇṭum, āyiṉum, tamiḻip pōṟṟuvārellām tamiḻareṉṟē koḷḷap peṟutal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻaṉ uyarntavaṉ
ulakil mutaṉmutal nākarikattiyum paṇpāṭṭiyum aṭintu, avaṟṟip piṟa nāṭukaḷiṟ parappiṉavaṉ tamiḻaṉē. ātalāl, avaṉ eṭṭuṇiyum ēṉiyōrkkut tāḻntavaṉallaṉ. tamiḻ vallōciyaṟṟa moḻiyeṉa mūkkaṟiyaṉ muṟiyiṟ paḻikkum tiraviṭar kūṟṟiyum, piṟappoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa āriyakkulap piriviṉi paṟṟit tamiḻaṉē iḻittuk kūṟum āriya ēmāṟṟiyum, poruṭpaṭuttātu, `nāṉ tamiḻaṉ' eṉa ēkkaḻuttattuṭaṉ ēṟupōṟ pīṭu naṭi naṭakka.
tāḻvuṇarcci nīṅkun takimikkaṭ ṭaṅkiṟṟē 
vāḻvuyarcci kāṇum vaḻi.
tamiḻ vāḻka!
- aṇṇal cuppiramaṇiyaṉār maṇimalar 1959
8
centamiḻum koṭuntamiḻum
centamiḻ, koṭuntamiḻ eṉṉum toṭarkaḷiluḷḷa cemmi koṭumi eṉṉum aṭikaḷ, muṟiyē nērmi,kōṇal eṉṉum poruḷkaḷi uṇarttum. ilakkaṇa nērmiyuḷḷa tamiḻ centamiḻ; atu kōṇiya tamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ.
cemmi, koṭumi eṉṉum paṇpukaḷi maṭṭum eṭuttuk koḷḷiṉ, avaṟṟuḷ etu muntiyatu etu pintiyatu eṉac colla viyalātu. ēṉeṉiṉ, iyaṟkiyil nērāṉa poruḷkaḷ ceyaṟkiyil kōṇalākkappaṭuvaṉavum, iyaṟkiyil kōṇalāṉa poruḷkaḷ ceyaṟkiyil nērākkappaṭuvaṉavum ettuṇiyō kaṇkūṭākak kāṇkiṉṟōm.
 āyiṉ, centamiḻ, koṭuntamiḻ eṉṉum iraṇṭaṉuḷ etu iyalpu etu tiripeṉiṉ, centamiḻē iyalpām, koṭuntamiḻē  tiripām. itu maṟukkoṇāta vuṇmiyāyiṉum, ārāycci yillātārkku mayaṅkaṟ kiṭaṉām. centamiḻ, koṭuntamiḻ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ iyalkaḷi allatu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi  aṟivārāyiṉ, evarum mayaṅkār.
centamiḻiṉ tiripē koṭuntamiḻ eṉṉum uṇmiyi uṇartaṟku, mutalāvatu av viraṇṭum vaḻaṅkum ticiyi nōkkutal vēṇṭum. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭapāluḷḷa nāṭukaḷē paḻantamiḻ ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷuḷ, moḻipeyar tēyam eṉṟum tamiḻtiri nilam eṉṟum cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟum, nāvalan tēyattiṉ teṉkōṭiyilēyē tamiḻ vaḻaṅkavum, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭakkum vaṭamēṟkum koṭuntamiḻt tiripāṉa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷē vaḻaṅkavum, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉkaṇṇum teṟku nōkkiyē tamiḻ tiruntic cellavum  kāṇkiṉṟōm.
koṭuntamiḻiṉ paṇpāṭē centamiḻāyiṉ, centamiḻ nilam  muntuniliyil koṭuntamiḻ nilamāyiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. itaṟkoru cāṉṟu millāmiyōṭu, paṇṭorukāl centamiḻ nilamāyirunta pakutikaḷ iṉṟu koṭuntamiḻ  nilaṅkaḷākat tirintuḷḷamiyuṅ kāṇkiṉ ṟōm.
kakara voliyiṉ nuṇukkamākiya āytamoḻinta (uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭumākiya) muppāṉ olikaḷē, tamiḻukku allatu centamiḻukku uriyavām. koṭun tamiḻ moḻikaḷākiya tiraviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ, tamiḻukku ēlātaṉavum urappiyum eṭuttum kaṉittum olippaṉavumāṉa ceyaṟki vallolikaḷiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivi āriyak kalappuṟṟa piṟkālattu viḷivalla; ēṉeṉiṉ, tūya tiraviṭac coṟkaḷilum ivvolikaḷ colluṟuppāka amintuḷḷaṉa.
	tamiḻ	teluṅku
e-ṭu :	Fo-Gudi	(kuṭiki)
	vēṅkaṭā	veṅkkaṭṭa
	entu (eṉṉatu)	enttu
tamiḻil coṉmutal varāta eḻuttukaḷ, koṭuntamiḻil ilakkaṇap pōlit tiripu kāraṇamākac coṉ mutal varukiṉṟaṉa.
	tamiḻ	teluṅku
e-ṭu :	ilatu	lētu
	poḻutu	prottu
urappiyum eṭuttum kaṉittum olikkum āriya allatu tiraviṭa volikaḷellām, tamiḻiluḷḷa (ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa) eṉṉum valliṉattiṉ tiripukaḷē. ataṉālēyē, aivarukka mutalil tamiḻeḻuttukaḷē allatu tamiḻoli yeḻuttukaḷē variyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
orē tamiḻ valliya veḻuttu, tiraviṭattil vevvēṟiṭattil vevvēṟoliyāyt tirivatuṇṭu.
	tamiḻ	teluṅku
e-ṭu : 	cey	ccēyu
		jēcu (`toliṉē jēsiṉa')
	cāla	tccālā
āriyattilum iṅṅaṉamē.
	tamiḻ	āriyam
e-ṭu :	poṟu	A.S. beru, L. Fero, gero,
		Gk. Phero, Skt. bhri.
	vitir	Skt. vithura, Vidhura.
iṉi, āriya moḻikaḷil ayaṟcoṟkaḷaṉṟit taṉ coṟkaḷkūṭa orēyeḻuttoliyi eṭuttum eṭukkātum āḷvatumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	urappāvoli	eṭuppoli
	purse	bursar, disburse.
ivveṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷāl, vavliṉa veḻuttolikaṭkellām tamiḻ vallolikaḷē mūlam eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
``aivarukkat tiṭiyiṉ mūṉṟum avvam mutalum'' eṉṟu pavaṇantiyār kūṟiyatum iṅku nōkkattakkatu.
eṭukkā voliyiṉiṉṟu eṭuppoli tōṉṟumēyaṉṟi, eṭuppoliyiṉiṉṟu eṭukkā voli tōṉṟātu. kuḻanti vāyil eṭukkā voliyē muntit tōṉṟum. muntiyal māntaṉ kuḻanti pōṉṟavaṉ. muṉṉit tamiḻaṉ muntiyal māntaṉpāṟpaṭṭavaṉ. ākiyāl, eṭukkā voliyē iyaṟkiyām. teṉkōṭiyil vāḻum anākarika makkaḷ moḻiyil eṭuppolikaḷ illi. muṉṉāriyamākiya mēli moḻikaḷil avi tōṉṟip piṉṉāriyamākiya camaṟkirutattil muṟṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻc coṟkaḷ tirintē koṭuntamiḻ tōṉṟum. attirivu : (1) coṟṟiripu, (2) poruṭṭiripu eṉa iru vakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, coṟṟiripu palvēṟu vakiya. avaṟṟuḷ cilavē īṇṭuk kāṭṭappaṭum.
tamiḻ	teluṅku	tiripuvaki
ūr	ūru	miki
kaṇ	kaṉṉu	tiritalum mikiyum
nīr	mīru	pōliyum mikiyum
avaṉ	vāṭu	ilakkaṇap pōliyum tiritalum
māṟṟam	māṭṭa	tiritalum keṭutalum
eḻupatu	ṭeppati	ilakkaṇap pōliyum 
		     pōliyum tiritalum
curuṭṭu	cuṭṭu	tokuttal
veṇṇey	veṉṉa	citivu
poruṭṭiripu, (1) āṭci vēṟupaṭal, (2) ciṟappu nīṅkaḷ eṉa iruvakittu.
`atē' eṉṉum colli `ām' eṉṉum poruḷilum, `mati' eṉṉum collip `pōtum' eṉṉum poruḷilum, maliyāḷattil vaḻaṅkal āṭci vēṟupaṭal. viṭicollutalik kuṟikkum ceppu eṉṉum collic collutal eṉṉum poruḷilum, viral maṭakkiya kiyāl allatu kuccāl aṭittalik kuṟikkum  koṭṭu eṉṉum colli aṭittal eṉṉum poruḷilum, teluṅkil vaḻaṅkal ciṟappu nīṅkalām.
tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkātu piṟa tiraviṭa nāṭukaḷil maṭṭum vaḻaṅkum ciṟappuc coṟkaḷ ticiccoṟkaḷākum. tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkuḷḷēyē oru poruṭku ōriṭattil oru collum maṟṟōriṭattil maṟṟoru collum vaḻaṅkumpōtu, centamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkāta putuc coṟkaḷ koṭuntamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkuvatu viyappaṉṟu. piyāṉi(maliyāḷam), alluṇṭu (teluṅku),   kokku(tuḷu) eṉpaṉa ittakiya.
(piyāṉi = oruvaki naccup pāmpu, alluṇṭu = marumakaṉ, kokku = māmaram.)
tamiḻiluḷḷa oruporuṭ pala coṟkaḷil cila, tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyiṉum, koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷil  vaḻakkaṟātuḷḷaṉa. aḵtāvatu, tamiḻnāṭṭu ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ cila koṭuntamiḻ nāṭṭu ulakavaḻakkil uḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: 
	tamiḻ    		teluṅku
	vetir 		veturu
	cāla 		tcālā
	paṇi		paṉi
	neyttōr 	netturu
iṅṅaṉam olittiripu, coṟṟiripu, poruṭṭiripu, ticiccol, ilakkiyac colvaḻakku ākiya aivaki iyalpē centamiḻik koṭuntamiḻākkukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟoṭu vaṭamoḻik kalappum cēriṉ, koṭuntamiḻ tiraviṭamākat tirikiṉṟatu. cēranāṭṭuc centamiḻ koṭun tamiḻākip piṉpu maliyāḷamāyt tirintuḷḷami kāṇka.
itukāṟum kūṟiyatu moḻittiṟamē. ilakkaṇa vilakkiyattiṟam nōkkiṉ, tamiḻukkuḷḷa taṉippaṭṭa toṉmutu ilakkaṇa vilakkiyam koṭuntamiḻukkilli. avaṟṟukkuḷḷaṉa vellām  piṟkālattaṉavum āriyattiṟ kuriyaṉavumē. itaṉāl, moḻiniliyāl maṭṭumaṉṟi ilakkiya niliyālum muṟpaṭṭa centamiḻ, ataṟkup piṟpaṭṭatum ataṉ tiripāṉatumāṉa koṭuntamiḻiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruttal muṭiyāteṉpatu tēṟṟam.
centamiḻiṉ tiripē koṭuntamiḻ; tiripiṉṟēl koṭuntamiḻilli. ākavē tamiḻt tiripē tiraviṭam; tiripiṉṟit tiraviṭamilli. teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻikaḷellām tiruntiya vaṭivil pēcavum eḻutavum paṭiṉ, tamiḻēyaṉṟi avvat tiraviṭa moḻikaḷākāmi kāṇka.
cilar, uṇṭi(uruṇṭi), koṭu(koṇṭu) eṉat tamiḻil  iruvaki vaḻakkilum aruki vaḻaṅkum aintāṟu coṟkaḷik koṇṭu, tamiḻum tiraviṭampōl tiripuṭiyatē eṉa nāṭṭa  virumpukiṉṟaṉar. evvitikkum vilakkuṇṭātaliṉ, vilakkikkoṇṭu vitiyi maṟukka muṭiyātu. oru poruḷiṉ perumpāṉmi yiyalpiyē ataṉ uṇmi yiyalpākak koḷḷalvēṇṭum. tamiḻ perumpālum iyalpuṭiyateṉ ṟum, tiraviṭam perumpālum tiripuṭiyateṉṟum aṟitalvēṇṭum.  itaṉālēyē centamiḻiyum koṭuntamiḻiyum muṟiyē tamiḻum tiraviṭamum eṉa iṭikkālattu ilakkaṇikaḷ cilar vaḻaṅkivantaṉar. itaṉip piṉpaṟṟit tamiḻukkiṉamāṉa moḻikaḷiyellām tiraviṭa meṉap pirittuk kūṟuvatē cālap porutta muṭittām. tiraviṭam eṉṉum tokuti tamiḻi uḷappaṭuttāmiyiṉ, av viraṇṭiyum oruṅkē kuṟikkat tamiḻam eṉṉum colliyē āḷavēṇṭum.
nūṉ, nūm, nuṅkaḷ; yāṉ, yām, yāṅkaḷ; nāṉ, nām, nāṅkaḷ; nīṉ, nīm, nīṅkaḷ; tāṉ, tām, tāṅkaḷ; avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avi; eṉa moḻik kaṭippaṭiyāṉa mūviṭap   peyarkaḷum, tamiḻil iyalpākavum oḻuṅkākavum irukkavum; teluṅkil, nēṉu, mēmu; maṉamu, nīvu, mīru; tāṉu, tāmu; vāṭu, ati(āme), vāru, ati, avi eṉat tirintum, oḻuṅkaṟṟum iṉamiḻantum iruttal kāṇka. moḻikkaṭippaṭiyāṉavum perumpālum tiriyātaṉavum ōracippaṭṭaṉavum ulaka moḻikaṭkellām toṭarpu kāṭṭuvaṉavum aṭikkaṭi collappaṭuvaṉavum nilittu vaḻaṅkuvaṉavu māṉa mūviṭap peyarkaḷē ittuṇit tiripaṭintiruppiṉ, vēṟu coṟkaḷippaṟṟic collavēṇṭuvatilli. im mūviṭap peyarkaḷi oru cōṟṟup patamākavē koḷka. iṅṅaṉam iyalpiyum tiripiyum muṟiyē tam ciṟappiyalpākak koṇṭa tamiḻiyum tiraviṭattiyum eṅṅaṉam oṉṟāka iṇikkavoṇṇum?
tamiḻ iyalpākavē cemmiyuṭimiyiṉ, tamiḻ eṉiṉum centamiḻ eṉiṉum oṉṟē. tamiḻiṉ tiripākiya koṭuntamiḻiṉiṉṟum pirittuk kūṟavē centamiḻ eṉappaṭṭatu, iyalpāṉa pāl taṇṇīrp pāliṉiṉṟum pirittuk kūṟat taṉippāl eṉappaṭṭāṟpōla.
tamiḻ oṉṟē mikunta ilakkaṇa varampuṭiyatu.
kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷum kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻ ēṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō?
eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivar tarukkik kūṟiyatit tamiḻaraṉivarum,  ciṟappākat tamiḻccivar, kavaṉittuk kāṇpārāka!
ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa eḻuttu, col, yāppu, aṇi ākiya nāṉkumē, tamiḻil mikunta varampum virivum koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉi, piṟamoḻikaṭ killāta poruḷilakkaṇattip paṟṟiyō collavē vēṇṭuvatilli. piṟamoḻikaḷi makkaḷ eṅṅaṉamellām pēciyum eḻutiyum varukiṉṟaṉarō, aṅṅaṉamellām avaṟṟiṉ iyalpum ilak kaṇamum amikiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, tamiḻ eṅṅaṉam pēcappaṭiṉum, mēṭi yēṟip pēcumpōtum ēṭeṭut teḻutum pōtum ilakkaṇa varampuṭa ṉēyē pēcavum eḻutavumpaṭa  vēṇṭum. ituvē teṉṟamiḻukku ilak kaṇikaḷ iṭṭa eṉṟumuḷa varampu. iv varampuṭiyatē tamiḻ allatu centamiḻ.
tamiḻ iyalpu taṉippaṭṭatu. itaṉālēyē, taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ  eṉappeṟṟatu. ākiyāl pirākirutattiṉiṉṟu camaṟkirutam ākkap peṟṟāṟpōl, koṭuntamiḻākiya tiraviṭattiṉiṉṟu centamiḻākiya tamiḻ ākkappeṟṟatu eṉpatu oruciṟitum poruntātu.
tamiḻ mikat tiruntiyatum paṇpaṭṭatumāṉa moḻiyākum. ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻi eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivar iḻittuk kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻiyākiya camaṟkirutamum oṉṟu eṉpati maṟantu viṭal kūṭātu. tillicciṟ ṟampalat tumeṉ cintiyuḷḷum uṟivāṉ uyarmatiṟ kūṭaliṉ āyntoṇ ṭīntamiḻ eṉṟu māṇikkavācakarē viyantum nayantum kūṟuvarāyiṉ, tamiḻ ettuṇi viḻumiya moḻiyākum.
eṇṇukku meṭṭāt toṉmitoṭṭut tamiḻ vaḻaṅkivariṉum,  iṉṟum ataṉ perumpāṟ coṟkaḷ vērpporuḷ tōṉṟum  iyalpu niliyiluḷḷaṉa; koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷō perumpālum vēṟupaṭṭum vēraṟṟum uḷḷaṉa.
āka, ākātu, āyiṟṟēl, ām(ākum) eṉṟa coṟkaḷ, teluṅkil muṟiyē kā, kātu, ayittē, auṉu eṉat tirintuḷḷaṉa. rāy (aṟi), ley(eḻu), lō(uḷ), lu(kaḷ), rōlu(ural),  rā (vār) mutaliya teluṅkuc coṟkaḷil eṅṅaṉam vērkāṇa muṭiyum? āka, uḷ eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tirintu, kā, lō eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tōṉṟumā? allatu, kā, lō eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tirintu āka, uḷ eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tōṉṟumā? iyalpiliruntu tiripu tōṉṟiyatā? tiripiliruntu iyalpu tōṉṟiyatā? ivaṟṟi yellām āyntu pārātu kallūrikaḷilum palkalikkaḻakaṅ kaḷilum talimit tamiḻp pulami naṭāttum  perum pulavaruṅkūṭap pirākirutattiliruntu camaṟkirutam tōṉṟiyavāṟē,  koṭuntamiḻiliruntu centamiḻ tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟa kuruṭṭuk koḷki yuṭiyarāyirukkiṉṟaṉar.  iṉimēlāyiṉum, avar āyntu uṇmi kāṇpārāka!
tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ palavāyiṉum, avaṟṟuḷ, muntit tirintatum talimiyāṉatum tarukkuṟṟatum tamiḻip paḻippatumāṉa teluṅkē iṅkup perumpālum eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭa teṉka.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' ceppiṭempar 1948
9
ticiccol evi?
tolkāppiyar tam kālat tamiḻc ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷi iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticiccol, vaṭacolleṉa nāl vakippaṭuttiṉār. avaṟṟuḷ, iyaṟcollum, tiri collum centamiḻ nāṭṭuc collum, ticic col koṭuntamiḻ nāṭṭuc collum ākum. vaṭa col eṉṟatu āriyac colliyum vaṭa nāṭṭuc colliyum  ākum. vaṭanāṭṭuc collāvatu, pirākirutam eṉṉum vaṭa tiraviṭac col. aḵtu āriyattiṟku muntiyatu.
akkālattut tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya allatu pukuttappaṭṭa ayaṉ moḻic col āriyac col oṉṟē. ataṉāl atit ticipaṟṟi vaṭa col eṉṟu pirittuk kūṟiṉar. avvāṟē ikkālattu ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷiyum , āṅkilac col, ilattīṉac col, kirēkkac col, cīṉaccol eṉa avvam moḻip peyarālēyē kuṟittal vēṇṭumē yaṉṟit ticic colluḷ aṭakkutal kūṭātu.
mēṟkuṟitta nālvakic collum paṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷ varumāṟu:
``iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol vaṭacoleṉ
ṟaṉittē ceyyuḷ īṭṭac collē.''	(tol. ecca. 1)
``avaṟṟuḷ,
iyaṟcoṟ ṟāmē
centamiḻ nilattu vaḻakkoṭu civaṇit
tamporuḷ vaḻāmi icikkuñ collē.''	(tol. ecca. 2)
``oruporuḷ kuṟitta vēṟucol lākiyum
vēṟu poruḷ kuṟitta orucol lākiyum
irupāṟ ṟeṉpa tiricoṟ kiḷavi.''		(tol. ecca. 3)
``centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi''	(tol. ecca. 4)
``vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē.''	(tol. ecca. 5)
``centamiḻ cērnta ...... ticic coṟ kiḷavi'' 
eṉṟataṉāl, koṭuntamiḻ nāṭṭuc collē ticiccol eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. `cērnta' eṉpatu `aṭutta' eṉṟu poruḷpaṭumē yaṉṟi, `cūḻnta' eṉṟu poruḷpaṭātu. tamiḻiṉ piṟantakam kumarināṭēyātalāl, centamiḻ nāṭu teṟkum koṭuntamiḻ nāṭu ataṉ vaṭakkum uḷḷaṉa eṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum. pāṇṭiyaṉukkē tamiḻnāṭaṉ eṉṟu ciṟappākap peyaruṇmiyum, ``tiruttak kalliṟkut teṟkiṭṭup piṟantavaṉ'' eṉṉum vaḻakkum, kavaṉikkat takkaṉa.
koṭuntamiḻ nāṭṭuc col vēṟu. koṭuntamiḻc col vēṟu. koṭuntamiḻ nāṭṭil tōṉṟic centamiḻc ceyyuḷil iṭam peṟat takka tiruntiya collē ticiccollām. atu camāḷi (camāḷicu) eṉṉuṅ kaṉṉaṭac col pōlvatu. jšÈ (bj.), tammuṭu (te.) eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa koccic coṟkaḷ centamiḻc ceyyuḷil iṭampeṟā. ``ēmirā vōri yeṉpāḷ entuṇṭi vati yeṉpāḷ'' eṉṉum koṇṭukūṟṟum, ``ēmirā vōri yeṉpāḷ entuṇṭi vatti yeṉpāḷ'' eṉṟē yiruttal vēṇṭum. allākkāl, ayaleḻuttup puṇarntu ayaṉmoḻiyākiviṭum eṉa aṟika.
tollilakkaṇa nūlār ticiccol vaḻakkiṟkum iṭantantā reṉṟu, ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷiyellām ticiccolleṉṟu koḷvatu tiripuṇarcciyākum.
``centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattun
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi''	(tol. ecca. 4)
itaṉ iḷampūraṇar uri: centamiḻ nāṭṭi aṭiyum puṭiyum kiṭanta paṉṉiru nilattār taṅ kuṟippiṉiyē ilakkaṇamāka vuṭiya ticiccoṟ kiḷavikaḷ eṉṟavāṟu, 
varalāṟu:
tāyit taḷḷi eṉpa kuṭanāṭṭār.
nāyi ñamali eṉpa pūḻināṭṭār.
piṟavum aṉṉa.
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa: potuṅkar nāṭu, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭu, oḷināṭu, kuṭṭa nāṭu, paṉṟi nāṭu, kaṟkā nāṭu, cīta nāṭu, pūḻi nāṭu, mali nāṭu, aruvā nāṭu, aruvā vaṭatali nāṭu, kuṭanāṭu.
cēṉāvariyar uri: centamiḻ nilattic cērnta paṉṉiraṇṭu nilattun tāṅkuṟitta poruḷ viḷakkun ticiccol eṉṟavāṟu eṉṟatu, avvan nilattut tāṅkuṟitta poruḷ viḷakkuvatallatu av viyaṟcol pōla ennilattun tam poruḷ viḷakkā veṉṟavāṟām.
paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa: poṅkar nāṭu, oḷināṭu, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭu, kuṭṭa nāṭu, kuṭa nāṭu, paṉṟi nāṭu, kaṟkā nāṭu, cīta nāṭu, pūḻi nāṭu, mali nāṭu, aruvā nāṭu, aruvā vaṭatali nāṭu eṉac centamiḻ nāṭṭut teṉ kīḻppāṉ mutalāka vaṭakīḻppā liṟutiyāka eṇṇik koḷka.
teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭār ā, erumi eṉpavaṟṟip peṟṟameṉṟum, tammāmi yeṉpataṉit tantuvi yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa. piṟavumaṉṉa.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri: ticiccollākiya col, centamiḻ nāṭṭic cūḻnta paṉṉiraṇṭu nilattum, paṉṉiraṇṭiyum puṟañcūḻnta paṉṉiraṇṭu nilattun tāntāṅ kuṟitta poruḷiyē viḷakkun taṉmiyi uṭiyaṉa e-ṟu.
ummiyi eccavummiyākkip poruḷurikka. eṉavē, iyaṟ col pōla ennilattun tam poruḷ viḷakkāvāyiṉa.
paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa: poṅkar nāṭu, oḷi nāṭu, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭu, kuṭṭa nāṭu, kuṭa nāṭu, paṉṟi nāṭu, kaṟkā nāṭu, cīta nāṭu, pūḻi nāṭu , maliyamā nāṭu, aruvā nāṭu, aruvā vaṭatali nāṭu eṉat teṉkīḻppāl mutalāka vaṭakīḻppā līṟāka eṇṇuka.
iṉip paṉṉiraṇṭiyuñ cūḻnta paṉṉiraṇṭāvaṉa: ciṅkaḷamum paḻantīvuṅ kollamuṅ kūpamuṅ koṅkaṇamun tuḷuvuṅ kaṭakamuṅ karunaṭamuṅ kuṭamum vaṭukun teluṅkuṅ kaliṅkamumām.
teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭār ā, erumi eṉpavaṟṟip peṟṟa meṉṟum: kuṭṭa nāṭṭār tāyit taḷḷi eṉṟum nāyi ñeḷḷi yeṉṟum; kuṭa nāṭṭār tantiyi accaṉeṉṟum; kaṟkā nāṭṭār vañcarik kiyareṉṟum, cīta nāṭṭār ēṭāveṉpataṉi eluvaṉeṉṟum, tōḻiyi ikuḷiyeṉṟum, tammāmi yeṉpataṉit tantuvi yeṉṟum, pūḻi nāṭṭār nāyi ñamaliyeṉṟum, ciṟukuḷattip pāḻiyeṉṟum; aruvā nāṭṭār ceyyic ceṟuveṉṟum, ciṟukuḷattik kēṇiyeṉṟum; aruvā vaṭataliyār kuṟuṇiyik kuṭṭiyeṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa.
iṉic ciṅkaḷam antōveṉpatu, karunaṭam kariya, cikka, kuḷira eṉpaṉa; vaṭuku ceppeṉpatu; teluṅku eruttip pāṇṭi leṉpatu; tuḷu māmarattik kokkeṉpatu. oḻintavaṟṟiṟkum vantuḻik kāṇka.
teyvacciliyār uri: centamiḻ nāṭṭic cērnta paṉṉiru nilattumuḷḷār tattaṅ kuṟippiṉiyuṭiya, ticiccollākiya col - e-ṟu.
paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa: viyi yāṟṟiṉ... nāṭu, oḷi nāṭu, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭu, karuṅkuṭṭa nāṭu, kuṭa nāṭu, paṉṟi nāṭu, kaṟkā nāṭu, cīta nāṭu, pūḻi nāṭu, malāṭu, aruvā nāṭu, aruvā, vaṭatali nāṭu eṉpa. ivi centamiḻnāṭṭakatta, centamiḻ nāṭeṉṟamiyāl, piṟa nāṭākal vēṇṭumeṉpār utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭumāṟu: ``kaṉṉit teṉkarik kaṭaṟ paḻantīpam, kollaṅ kūpakañ ciṅkaḷameṉṉum elliyiṉ puṟattavum kaṉṉaṭam vaṭukam, kaliṅkam, teliṅkam, koṅkaṇam, tuḷuvam, kuṭakam, kuṉṟakam eṉpaṉa kuṭapā lirupuṟac ciyat tuṭaṉuṟipu kūrun tamiḻtiri nilaṅkaḷum, muṭiyuṭiyava riṭunilavāṭciyiṉ aracu mēmpaṭṭa kuṟunilak kuṭumikaḷ patiṉmaru muṭaṉirup piruvarumākiya paṉṉiruvar aracarum paṭitta paṉṉiru tēyattiṉum tamiḻc collātaṟku viruppuṭiyaṉa'' eṉa niṟuttup piṉṉum ``centamiḻiyaṟki civaṇiya nilattoṭu muntu nūl kaṇṭu'' eṉa ōtiyavataṉāṟ civaṇiya nilamāvatu elli kuṟitta nilattic cārnta nilameṉa vēṇṭutalāṉum, paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa: kumariyāṟṟiṉ teṉkarippaṭṭa paḻantīpamum kollamum kūpakamum ciṅkaḷamum, ciyattiṉ mēṟkuppaṭṭa koṅkaṇamun tuḷuvamuṅ kaṭakamuṅ kuṉṟakamum, kiḻakkuppaṭṭa karunaṭamum vaṭukum teliṅkum kaliṅkamum eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
ivaṟṟuḷ, kūpakamuṅ kollamuṅ kaṭal koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ kumariyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakariyik kollameṉak kuṭiyēṟiṉār pōlum. pañcatirāviṭameṉavum vaṭanāṭṭār urippavākalāṉ, avi yintum vēṅkaṭattiṉ teṟkātaluṅ kūṭāmi yuṇarka.
annilattu vaḻaṅkuñ collākic ceñcolliṉ vēṟupaṭṭuc cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷakattu varuvaṉa nīkkappaṭā e-ṟu.
kuṭāvaṭi yuḷiyam eṉṟavaḻik kuṭā eṉpatu kuṭakattār piḷḷi kaṭku iṭṭapeyar. antō eṉpatu ciṅkaḷavar aiyō eṉpataṟkiṭṭa peyar. yāṉ taṟkariya varutu eṉṟavaḻik karital eṉpatu karunāṭar viḷipporuḷuṇarak kūṟuvatu. ceppu eṉpatu vaṭukar collutaṟkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. pāṇṭil eṉpatu teliṅkar pacuviṟkum eruttiṟkum peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. kokku eṉpatu tuḷuvar māvukkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. piṟavum ivvāṟu varuvaṉa palavaṟṟiyum vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka.
ceñcol = iyaṟcol
``centamiḻ nilañcēr paṉṉiru nilattiṉum
oṉpatiṟ ṟiraṇṭiṉil tamiḻoḻi nilattiṉum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticicco leṉpa.''	(naṉ. peya. 16)
caṭakōpa irāmānuca kiruṭṭiṇamāccāriyār uri: centamiḻ nāṭākiya pāṇṭi nāṭṭic cērnta paṉṉiraṇṭu koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷi lum, patiṉeṭṭut tēcaṅkaḷuḷ tamiḻnāṭu oḻinta patiṉēḻu nāṭukaḷilum, vacippavarkaḷ tamatu pēcum pāḻikaḷiluḷḷa pataṅkaḷ, ap poruḷōṭu centamiḻil vantu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa ticic coṟkaḷām eṉṟu colvar (pulavar) e-ṟu.
ticiccol - ticikaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa tēca pāḻikaḷiliruntu centamiḻil vantu vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ.
koṭuntamiḻnāṭu paṉṉiraṇṭāvaṉa: teṉpāṇṭi nāṭu, kuṭṭa nāṭu, kuṭa nāṭu, kaṟkā nāṭu, vēṇāṭu, pūḻināṭu, paṉṟi nāṭu, aruvānāṭu, aruvā vaṭatali nāṭu, cītanāṭu, maliyamāṉāṭu, cōḻa nāṭu eṉpaṉa. teṉpāṇṭi nāṭu - centamiḻp pāṇṭi nāṭṭukkut teṉticiyiluḷḷatu.
e-ṭu :
1.	pacuvip peṟṟam eṉpatum		teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭuccol
	cōṟṟic coṉṟi eṉpatum	}
2.	tāyit taḷḷi eṉpatu 	-	kuṭṭanāṭṭuc col
3.	tantiyi accaṉ eṉpatu 	-	kuṭanāṭṭuc col
4.	vañcakarik kiyar eṉpatu 	-	kaṟkā nāṭṭuc col
5.	tōṭṭattik kiḻār eṉpatu	-	vēṇāṭṭuc col
6.	ciṟukuḷattip pāḻi eṉpatu	-	pūḻi nāṭṭuc col
7.	vayalic cey eṉpatu	-	paṉṟi nāṭṭuc col
8.	ciṟukuḷattik kēṇi eṉpatu	-	aruvā nāṭṭuc col
9.	puḷiyi ekiṉ eṉpatu	-	aruvāvaṭatali nāṭṭuc col
10.	tōḻaṉi eluvaṉ eṉpatum
	tōḻiyi ikuḷi eṉpatum	-	cīta nāṭṭuc col
11.	nalla nīri veḷḷam eṉpatu	-	maliyamāṉāṭṭuc col
12.	tāyi āy eṉpatu	-	puṉaṉāṭṭuc col
tamiḻnāṭu oḻinta nāṭu patiṉēḻāvaṉa: ciṅkaḷam, cōṉakam, cāvakam, cīṉam, tuḷuvam, kuṭakam, koṅkaṇam, kaṉṉaṭam, kollam, teluṅkam, kaliṅkam, vaṅkam, kaṅkam, makatam, kaṭāram, kuṭam kucalam.
māmarattik kokku eṉpatu	-	tuḷuvanāṭṭuc col
akappaṭutalic cikkutal eṉpatu	-	kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭuc col
karuttāka iruttali eccarikki eṉpatum
   collutalic cepputal eṉpatum	-	teluṅka nāṭṭuc col
maṟṟaviyum ippaṭiyē āṅkāṅku varutal kaṇṭukoḷka.
aiyō eṉpati antō eṉpatu ciṅkaḷa nāṭṭuc col eṉṟu muṉṉiya uriyāciriyarkaḷ eḻutiyiruntālum, atu `hanta' eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ citivu eṉṟu teritalāl, iṅkuk koḷḷappaṭavilli.
paḻañ ceyyuḷkaḷ
``teṉpāṇṭi kuṭṭaṅ kuṭaṅkaṟkā vēṇpūḻi
paṉṟi aruvā ataṉvaṭakku - naṉṟāya
cītam malāṭu puṉaṉāṭu centamiḻcēr
ētamil paṉṉirunāṭ ṭeṇ''
``ciṅkaḷañ cōṉakañ cāvakañ cīṉan tuḷukkuṭakam
koṅkaṇaṅ kaṉṉaṭaṅ kollan teluṅkaṅ kaliṅkavaṅkam
kaṅka makataṅ kaṭāraṅ kavuṭaṅ kaṭuṅkucalan
taṅkum pukaḻttamiḻ cūḻpati ṉēḻpuvi tāmiviyē.''
paṇṭik koṭuntamiḻ moḻikaḷē variyiṟantu vaṭacoṟ kalantu, iṉṟu tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ, āriyac col oṉṟum vēṇṭāta tūya moḻiyātalālum, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭa, mali yāḷam mutaliya ēṉiya iṉamoḻikaḷ āriyatti yakaṟṟum āṟṟalaṟṟup pōṉamiyālum, tamiḻiyum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiyum pirittaṟital vēṇṭum.
talikkālak koṭuntamiḻ moḻikaḷ pirākiruta moḻikaḷākavum, iṭikkālak koṭuntamiḻ moḻikaḷ tiraviṭa moḻikaḷākavum pirintu pōṉatiṉālum; kaṭal tāṇṭiya veḷināṭṭu iṭappeyarkaḷ pala vaṇikat toṭarpāl vantu vaḻaṅkiyataṉālum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, teyvac ciliyārum, paṉṉiraṇṭiyuñ cūḻnta paṉṉiru nilaṅkaḷiyum koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷākak koṇṭaṉar. pavaṇanti muṉivarō, patiṉeṇ moḻi nāṭukaḷuḷ tamiḻoḻinta piṟavaṟṟiyellām koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷākak kūṟiviṭṭaṉar. ituvē piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyellām ticiccolleṉac cilar mayaṅkutaṟku iṭan tantuviṭṭatu.
`antō' eṉpatu `attō' eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa centamiḻc collē. itu vaṭamoḻiyil `hanta' eṉṟu tiriyum. ātalāl, itu ciṅkaḷac collumaṉṟu; vaṭacoṟ citivu maṉṟu.
vaṭacol, moḻiyārāycci illāk kālattil vēṇṭātu pukuttappaṭṭataṉāl, atu iṉi aṟavē vilakkappaṭum. ataṉōṭu piṟa ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷum vilakkappaṭum. tūymiyē tamiḻukku uyirnāṭiyātalāl, tamiḻ vāḻtaṟku, ayaṉmoḻic col vilakku iṉṟiyamiyātateṉa aṟika.
- ``tamiḻam'' 1.7.1974
10
maliyāḷamum tamiḻum
in nāvalan tēyattup paḻiya moḻikaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa, vaṭa moḻi, teṉmoḻi eṉṉum iruperu moḻikaḷuḷ aṭaṅkum. avaṟṟuḷ, vaṭamoḻic cārpiṉa vaṭintiyāvilum, teṉmoḻic cārpiṉa teṉṉintiyāvilum vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa. āriyam intiyāviṟkuṭ pukumuṉṉarē, tiraviṭam iṅku eṇṇaru nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka vaḻaṅki vantatāl, intiyāviṉ toṉmutumoḻi tiraviṭam eṉṉum  teṉ moḻiyē. kumari mutal paṉimali (imayam) vari oru kālattil intiyā muḻutum teṉmoḻiyē vaḻaṅkivantateṉpataṟku, peluccit tāṉattilum vaṅkāḷattilum, muṟiyē, pirākuvi  aracamakāl eṉṉum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkivaruvatum, vaṭanāṭṭu āriyamoḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi orumaruṅku tiraviṭamāyiruppatum, kumariyoṭu vaṭavimayat toru moḻivit tulakāṇṭa cēralātaṟku eṉṉum cilappatikāra vāḻttuk kāti urip pāṭṭumaṭi yeṭupputtoṭarum pōtiya cāṉṟām.
iṉi, kaṭikkaḻakak kālamvari vēṅkaṭattiṟkut teṟkuppaṭṭa nāvalnilam muḻuvatum tamiḻnāṭā yiruntami, 
vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari
āyiṭit
tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu 	(tol. ci. pā.)
vaṭatici maruṅkiṉ vaṭukuvaram pākat
teṉṟici yuḷḷiṭ ṭeñciya mūṉṟum
varimaruḷ puṇariyoṭu porutu kiṭanta
nāṭṭiyal vaḻakka nāṉmiyiṟ kaṭikkaṇ
yāppiṉa tilakkaṇa maṟikuvaṉ muṟiyē. 	 (ciṟukākkipāṭiṉiyam) 
neṭiyōṉ kuṉṟamun toṭiyōḷ puvamun
tamiḻvaram paṟutta taṇpuṉa ṉaṉṉāṭṭu.	(cilap. vēṉiṟkāti)
kumari vēṅkaṭaṅ kuṇakuṭa kaṭalā
maṇṭiṉi maruṅkiṟ ṟaṇṭamiḻ varippil	(cilap. nūṟkaṭṭuri)
eṉṉum ceyyuṭ pakutikaḷālum, iṟṟi maliyāḷa nāṭu aṟṟic cēranāṭā yiruntamiyālum aṟiyappaṭum.
tamiḻakam, toṉṟutoṭṭuk ki.pi. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari,  cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyam eṉṉum muttamiḻ nāṭākavē yiruntuvantatu.
vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippu	(tol. 1336)
eṉṟār tolkāppiyar.
kaṭantaṭu tāṉi mūviruṅ kūṭi 	(puṟam. 110)
eṉṟār kapilar.
potumi cuṭṭiya mūva rulakamum 	(puṟam. 337)
eṉṟār piramaṉār.
mummaliyu munnāṭum
eṉpatu tiruvaḷḷuvamāli.
mūvar kōviyum mūviḷaṅ kōviyum
eṉṟār auviyār.
ki.pi. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkuppiṉ paḻañcēra marapu aṟṟuppōyc cēranāṭum kēraḷam eṉa vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭatēṉum, atu 16ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari koṭuntamiḻ nāṭāka iruntuvantirukkiṉṟatu. 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻntirunta tuñcattu eḻuttaccaṉē, āriya eḻuttu eṉṉum camaṟkirutaṅ kalanta neṭuṅkaṇakkip pukuttiyum vaṭamoḻip paṉuvalkaḷi moḻipeyarttum, aḷaviṟanta vaṭacoṟkaḷit taṟcama muṟiyil taḻuviyum, aṭippaṭiyiliruntē vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇattik kiyāṇṭum cēranāṭṭut tamiḻik keṭuttāṉ. aṉṟiruntu, atu tamiḻeṉappaṭātu kēraḷam allatu maliyāḷam eṉṉum peyarāl oru tiripuṭit tiraviṭamoḻiyākavē vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu.
maliyāḷam, mūkkoli mikiyālum ticiccoṟ ṟokutiyālum koccit tiripālum tamiḻiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum, iṉṟum atiṟ kalantuḷḷa āriyak kūṟu nīkkappeṟiṉ, eñci niṟpatu collaḷavil tamiḻēyām. maliyāḷam eṉṉum peyarē  tamiḻccollātal kāṇka. maliyil allatu malināṭṭil  vāḻpavaṉ maliyāḷi. maliyāḷiyiṉ moḻi maliyāḷam. kēraḷam eṉpatu cēralam eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ tiripu. cēral - cēralaṉ - cēralam - kēraḷam.
maliyāḷam vaṭamoḻi kalanta paḻantamiḻē yāyiṉum,  tamiḻoṭum tamiḻaroṭum neṭuṅkālam toṭarpiṉmiyālum paṇṭi varalāṟṟi aṟiyāmiyālum, iṭikkālattil āriyarkkum āriyattiṟkum uyarvum tiraviṭarukkum tamiḻukkum iḻivum ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatiṉālum, maliyāḷiyar, tamiḻiṉ perumiyi uṇarātiruppatuṭaṉ, maliyāḷat tiṉiṉṟu tamiḻ vantateṉṟum vaṭamoḻiyē maliyāḷattiṟkut tāy eṉṟum, kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar tamiḻaruḷ cila vakuppārē tammi kṣattiriyar eṉṟum, vaṭacoṟ kalavātu taṉittamiḻiṟ pēcuvatu tāḻveṉṟum koṇṭiruppati nōkkumpōtu. maliyāḷiyar kūṟṟil eḷḷaḷavum viyappiṟkiṭamiṉṟām.
tamiḻic cariyāy aṟitaṟkup piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷippaṟṟiya aṟivum iṉṟiyamiyātateṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār kālṭuvel  kaṇkāṇiyār. itu uyarvunaviṟcipōl tōṉṟiṉum uṇminaviṟciyē. varalāṟṟuk kālat tamiḻakattiṟkup puṟampāṉa vaṭuku, kōṇṭu mutaliya vaṭapāl tiraviṭamoḻi aṟivē tamiḻic cevviyāy aṟitaṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta teṉiṉ, toṉṟutoṭṭut  tamiḻakattiṉ akattuṟuppāṉatum atil mūṉṟiloru kūṟāṉatumāṉa cēranāṭṭut tamiḻum vaṭamoḻik kalappā ṉēyē aṇmiyiṟ pirintupōṉatumāṉa maliyāḷamoḻi yaṟivu, ataṟku  ettuṇi iṉṟiyamiyātateṉac collavum vēṇṭumō?
tamiḻam eṉṉum peyar vaṭanāṭṭil allatu vaṭamoḻiyil tiraviṭam(tamiḻam-tramiḷam-tramiṭam-traviṭam) eṉṟu tirintiruppatu pōl tamiḻmoḻiyum palvēṟu tiraviṭamoḻikaḷākat tirintiruppa tālum, tamiḻallāta tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkum nāṭukaḷil āṅkāṅkuc ciṟappāka uriya poruḷkaṭkup putuccoṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruppatālum, pala tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ tāymoḻiyām tamiḻiṉiṉṟu terintukoṇṭa kūṟukaḷuṭ cila tamiḻil iṟantupaṭṭamiyālum, cila tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ taṉittaṉi orōvoru kūṟṟiṉi vaḷarttuḷḷamiyālum, ellāt tiraviṭa moḻikaḷum cērntatē muḻuttamiḻ eṉṟu koḷvatu iḻukkākātu.
tiraviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ iṭam paṟṟiyum iyalpupaṟṟiyum tamiḻukku mika nerukkamāṉatu maliyāḷamākum. atil vaṭacoṟkaḷ aḷaviṟantu kalantuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār kūṟiyuḷḷa nilimi, ataṉ nūlvaḻakkic cārntatēyaṉṟi  ulakavaḻakkic cārntataṉṟu. iṉṟum, maliyāḷap pēccu  vaḻakki nōkkiṉ, atu peritum vaṭacoṟ kalappaṟṟa teṉpatu pulaṉām. iṟṟit tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum cila vaṭacoṟkum āṅkilac coṟkum nēr teṉcol maliyāḷa moḻiyiltāṉ   vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:  	vaṭacol 	teṉcol
	mittuṉaṉ	aḷiyaṉ
	mattiyāṉam-matiyam 	ucci
	varuṣam 	āṇṭu, kollam(nakar)
	ārampam 	tuvakkam
	upāttiyāyar-vāttiyār 	eḻuttaccaṉ
	amāvāci 	kaṟutta vāvu(kāruvā)
	pūraṇi-purṇami 	veḷutta vāvu(veḷḷuvā)
	āṅkilaccol 	tamiḻccol (cēranāṭu)
	peṉ  	tūval
	pātrūm 	kuḷimuṟi
	ṭirāyar(mēci) 	valippu
	kāmpavuṇṭu 	parampu
	vēkkaṉci 	oḻivu
	kōṭṭu 	kuppāyam
	verāntā 	kōlāyi
ucci, āṇṭu, tuvakkam mutaliya teṉcoṟkaḷ tamiḻnāṭ ṭilum vaḻaṅkiṉum maliyāḷa nāṭṭiṟpōl attuṇip peruvaḻakkāka vum nāṭu muḻumiyum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟila.
iṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum cila teṉcoṟkaṭku etirppāṟ coṟkaḷ iṉṟu maliyāḷanāṭṭu vaḻakkilēyē  uḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: 	tamiḻ 	 maliyāḷam
	acci(tāy, tamakki) 	 accaṉ(tanti)
	ciṟukki 	 ceṟukkaṉ(ciṟukkaṉ)
	tampirāṉ 	 tampirāṭṭi
cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭukaḷil vaḻakkaṟṟuppōṉa cila orucol viṉi kaḷ, cēranāṭākiya maliyāḷa nāṭṭilēyē iṉṟu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:	maliyāḷam	iṟṟit tamiḻ
	(orucolviṉi)	(irucolviṉi)
	kaṭkuṉṉu-kakkuṉṉu 	kaḷavu ceykiṟāṉ
	kulekkuṉṉu 	kulitaḷḷukiṟatu
	muruṭuṉṉu 	muruṭākiṉṟatu
	kuḻikkuṉṉu 	kuḻi tōṇṭukiṟāṉ.
cēranāṭṭut tamiḻiṉ ciṟanta colvaḷam, maliyāḷamoḻiyiṉ vāyilākavē iṉṟu aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu:  	āḻcca = kiḻami, āḻccavaṭṭam = vāram
	pakarkkuka = paṭiyeṭukka.
	piyāṉi = taciyik kottip piṭuṅkum oruvakip pāmpu.
	noṇṇu = uḷvāy
	muttāḻam(muṟṟālam) = kāliyuṇṭi
	attāḻam(aṟṟālam) = irāvuṇṭi.
ayakkūṟa, ākōli, ēṭṭa, kuḷavaṉ, nōṅṅal(kal), mālā mutaliyaṉa, toṉṟutoṭṭuc cēranāṭṭil vaḻaṅkivarum mīṉ peyarkaḷ. ticiccol muṟiyil maliyāḷattil vaḻaṅkum teṉcoṟkaḷ māperun tokiyiṉa.

	kariccal = kuṭali

	kōḷāmpi = paṭikkam
	pakaram = patil, patilāka
	mēṭi = vāṅku(ē.vi.)
	veṭippākku = tuppuravākku(ē.vi.)
	veḷuttōṭaṉ, alakkukkāraṉ = vaṇṇāṉ

maliyāḷattil vaḻaṅkum ticiccoṟkaḷuṭ perumpālaṉa caṟṟē poruḷ tirinta tamiḻccoṟkaḷē.
e-ṭu: 	atē = ām (yes)
	aṭukkal = pakkam, kiṭṭa, iṭam
	eṉṟe aṭukkal = eṉṉiṭam
	oṭukkam = muṭivu, muṭivil
	kaḷi = viḷiyāṭu (kaḷittāṭu)
	kuṭṭi = piḷḷi

	nērākka = ceppaṉiṭuka
	ceṟumaṉ = kaḷamaṉ, kaḷattaṭimi.
	ceṟu = vayal
	valiya = periya.

	viṭakku = keṭṭa, viṭakkukuṭṭi = keṭṭa piḷḷi
	kaḻiyum = muṭiyum. ceyvāṉ kaḻiyum = ceyyamuṭiyum
	kaḷa(kaḷi) = viṭu. vaṉṉukaḷa = vantuviṭu
	mati = pōtum (pōtiya aḷavu)
kēraḷattil ciṟappākap payiriṭappeṟum teṉṉimarampaṟṟi maliyāḷattil vaḻaṅkum paṟpala peyarkaḷ kavaṉikkattakkaṉa.

nālvakik kaḷ 
1.  iḷaya kaḷḷu
2. maturak kaḷḷu
3. kippuk kaḷḷu
4. mūtta kaḷḷu
cila teṉcoṟkaḷ paḻam poruḷilēyē iṉṟum maliyāḷa nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: 	kōyilakam = araṇmaṉi
	ampalam = kōyil
poṉṉampalam, veḷḷiyampalam, ciṟṟampalam, pērampalam eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
veyileṉ kiḷavi maḻiyiyal niliyum 	(tol. puḷḷi. 82)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāppaṭi veyil eṉṉum nilimoḻi attuc cāriyi peṟṟum puṇarum puṇarcciyi, iṉṟu, veyilattuc ceṉṉu, veyilattup pōkarutu eṉa maliyāḷa nāṭṭu vaḻakkiltāṉ kāṇkiṉ ṟōm.
patiṟṟuppattu eṉṉum puṇarmoḻip peyarōṭotta muppatiṟṟuppattu, ampatiṟṟu nālu mutaliya iṟṟuc cāriyip puṇarccit toṭarmoḻikaḷum iṉṟu maliyāḷa nāṭṭiltāṉ  vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
cila maliyāḷa nāṭṭut teṉcoṟkaḷ muntu niliyiyē iṉṟum tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:	maliyāḷam 	 tamiḻ
	ari 	 arici
	uḷḷu(uḷ) 	 uṇṭu
	nallu(nal) 	 naṉṟu
	el 	 elumpu
	parantu 	 paruntu
	pōḻ 	 pōḻtu
``ari yeṟiññāl āyiram kākka'', ``karayuṉṉa kuṭṭikkē pāluḷḷu'', vampaṉōṭu vaḻutu nallu eṉpaṉa maliyāḷap paḻamoḻikaḷ.
puram = uyarvu. puriyuyar pākum eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (uri. 4).
puram - param = mēl. coṟṟirivil ukaramutal akaramutalātal iyalpu.
e-ṭu: muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku, kuṭumpu - kaṭumpu, kuṭam - kaṭam.
parampu - paṟampu = (uyarnta) mali. paraṇ = mēliṭam. parantu - paruntu = uyaramākap paṟakkum paṟavi.  uyaravuyarap paṟantālum ūrkkuruvi paruntākumā? eṉṉum tamiḻp paḻamoḻiyum, paṇattiṉṉu mīte parantum paṟakkayilla eṉṉum maliyāḷap paḻañcollum iṅku nōkkattakkaṉa. parantu eṉṉum colliṉ iṭiyiluḷḷa akaram, īṟṟiluḷḷa ukaraccārpāl ukaramāyiṟṟu. itu uyiricivu māṟṟam (Harmonious sequence of vowels) eṉṉum neṟimuṟiyām. o.nō: parampu-parumpu. parāntu, pirāntu eṉṉum ulakavaḻakkum iṅku nōkkattakkaṉa . 
 pōḻ-pōḻtu-poḻutu-pōtu. pōḻtu-pōḻtam.
o.nō: vīḻ-vīḻtu-viḻutu.
tamiḻiluḷḷa cila ilakkaṇac colvakikaḷiṉ varaṉmuṟi, maliyāḷac coṟṟuṇi koṇṭē aṟiyamuṭikiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu: 
(1) nikaḻkāla iṭinili
kiṉṟu eṉṉum nikaḻkāla iṭinili, kunnu, unnu eṉṟu maliyāḷattilum, kun-un(uṉ) - n eṉṟu tamiḻilum    tiriyum.
e-ṭu : 	ceykiṉṟāṉ-ceykunnāṉ-ceykunaṉ. 
	ceykunnāṉ-ceyyunnāṉ - ceyyunaṉ-ceynaṉ.
makiḻnaṉ, vāṇar (vāḻnar), āakuna (tol. 932), ``kaḷiyunar    (kuṟaḷ. 879), pāṭunar,`īkunar' (puṟam. 235), `pakarunar', `ōcunar', `tarunar'(cilap. intira.) mutaliya nikaḻkāla viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarkaḷellām, mēṟkūṟiya vakiyil amintaviyē. kiṉṟu eṉṉum iyalnilikkum kun-un-n  eṉṉum iṟutit tirinilikaṭkum uḷḷa toṭarpi, kunnu, unnu eṉṉum iṭittirinilikaḷākiya maliyāḷa vaṭivukaḷē  kāṭṭi niṟṟal kāṇka. iv nikaḻkāla iṭiniliyiṉ iyalpi virivāka viḷakkiṉām.  āṇṭuk kāṇka.
(2) etirmaṟi ēvalviṉi
ceyyātē eṉṉum etirmaṟi ēval allatu muṉṉili vilakkuviṉi, maliyāḷattil ceyyarutu eṉṉum vaṭivu koṇṭu niṟkum. tamiḻ vaṭivil viḷaṅkāta iyalpu maliyāḷa vaṭivil viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ceyyarutu eṉpatu ceyyaritu eṉpataṉ tiripu. ceyyaritu eṉpatu ceyya muṭiyātu eṉṉum poruḷatu. perumpālum muṭiyāmi allatu iyalāmipporuṭṭuc coṟkaḷē vilakku viṉiyākiṉṟaṉa. ceyyakkūṭum, ceyyakkūṭātu; ceyyappaṭum, ceyyappaṭātu eṉṉum viṉikaḷ, uṭaṉpāṭṭu vaṭivil muṭitaṟ poruḷiyum etirmaṟi vaṭivil muṭiyāmip poruḷi aṭiyākak koṇṭu vilakkupporuḷiyum, muṟiyē, uṇarttutal kāṇka. ceyyātē eṉṉum viṉi ceyyarutē eṉṉum ēkāravīṟṟu vaṭiviṉ tiripām. āyiṉ, ceyyātē eṉpatu tamiḻil orumikku maṭṭum urittāyirukka, ceyyarutu eṉpatu maliyāḷattil orumi paṉmi irumikkum potuvām. ceyyarutē eṉpatu maliyāḷattil ceyyarute eṉṟum niṟkum. iṉi, arutu eṉpatu maliyāḷattil tuṇiviṉiyāy maṭṭumaṉṟit taṉiviṉiyākavum varum.
e-ṭu :	ī āḷkku vēṟē paṇi arutu = iv āṭku vēṟu vēli kūṭātu.
(3) ikaravīṟṟu iṟantakāla viṉiyeccam
iṟantakāla viṉiyecca vaṭivuṭaṉ āl vikuti cērvatāl, orucār etirkāla viṉiyeccan tōṉṟum.
e-ṭu: 	vantu + āl - vantāl
	kaṇṭu + āl - kaṇṭāl
	ceṉṟu + āl - ceṉṟāl
	kiṭṭi + āl - kiṭṭiyāl-kiṭṭiṉāl
yakaram cilaviṭattu nakaramāka māṟuvatu iyalpu.
o.nō:  yāṉ - nāṉ, yamaṉ - namaṉ.
im muṟiyil, eḻutiyāl - eḻutiṉāl, ūtiyāl - ūtiṉāl, tīṇṭiyāl - tīṇṭiṉāl, kūṭiyāl - kūṭiṉāl, tōṉṟiyāl - tōṉṟiṉāl, tēṟiyāl - tēṟiṉāl eṉa yakara vuṭampaṭumey peṟṟa etirkāla viṉiyeccaṅkaḷi lellām, av yakaramey ṉakarameyyākat  tiriyap peṟum. im muṟiyilēyē, āyāl-āṉāl, pōyāl - pōṉāl eṉavum varum. āyiṉ, āyiṉāl, pōyiṉāl eṅṅaṉam āyiṉaveṉiṉ āyi pōyi eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷē āl vikuti cērntu āyiyāl-āyiṉāl, pōyiyāl - pōyiṉāl eṉa muṟiyē tirintaṉaveṉka. itaṉāl iṉ eṉa ōr iṟantakāla viṭinili illi eṉpatum peṟṟām.
iṟantakāla viṉiyecca vaṭivukaḷē, mutaṟkālattil iṟṟi maliyāḷattiṟpōl muṟṟākavumiruntu piṉṉar muṟṟi vēṟupaṭuttu vāṉ vēṇṭi pālīṟu peṟṟaṉavātaliṉ, iṟantakāla  muṟṟukaḷi uṟuppup pirittuk kāṭṭaṟku mutaṟkaṇ eccamum īṟumākavē pirittuk kōṭal vēṇṭum.
e-ṭu: 	vantu + āṉ - vantāṉ
	kaṇṭu + āṉ - kaṇṭāṉ
	ceṉṟu + āṉ - ceṉṟāṉ
	kāṭṭi + āṉ - kāṭṭiyāṉ-kāṭṭiṉāṉ
	pōyi + āṉ - pōyiyāṉ-pōyiṉāṉ
	pōyi-pōy + āṉ - pōyāṉ-pōṉāṉ
iṅṅaṉam pirittuk kāṇumpōtu, nilimoḻikku ukara vuyirmey yīṟum ikara vuyirmey yīṟumaṉṟi iṉ īṟiṉmi kaṇṭu teḷika.
eḻutiyāl,  kiṭṭiyāl eṉṉum yakara vuṭampaṭumey peṟṟa etirkāla viṉiyeccaṅkaḷum, āyi, pōyi eṉṉum ikaravīṟṟu iṟantakāla viṉiyeccaṅkaḷum, cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭukaḷil vaḻakku vīḻntu pōyiṉum, maliyāḷa(cēra) nāṭṭil iṉṟum  vaḻakkāṟṟil iruntuvarukiṉṟaṉa. iviyē muntiya vaṭivukaḷām.
ikara yakaram iṟuti viravum 	(tol. moḻi. 25)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
nāi - nāyi - nāy eṉpatupōṉṟē āi - āyi - āy eṉpatum eṉa aṟika.
iṉi, vaṭanāṭṭu moḻiyākiya intiyilum piṟa āriya moḻi kaḷilum eḻuvāyp peyariyum peyarp payaṉiliyiyum iṇikkum  iru eṉṉum puṇarppuc coṟkum (copula),  maliyāḷam aṭikōli yiruntirukkiṉṟatu eṉṉalām.
e-ṭu: 	itu entākuṉṉu(entāṇu)?
	itu oru maramākuṉṉu(maramāṇu).
	yaḵ kiyā hi? What is this?
	yaḵ pēṭ hi. This is a tree.
iṉi, iṟutiyāka, maliyāḷa nāṭṭiṟ ciṟappākavum ciṟappāṉa vaṭivilum vaḻaṅkum paḻamoḻikaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟiyum, eṭuttukkāṭṭuvām.
maliyāḷattil ēṟattāḻa īrāyiram paḻamoḻikaḷ vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oru pakuti vaṭacoṟ  kalantatu; iṅkuk kāṭṭap peṟṟavaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭoṉṟu nīṅkalāka  ēṉiyavellām tūya teṉcoṟ paḻamoḻikaḷē. maliyāḷattiṟ paḻamoḻi paḻañcol eṉappeṟum. mutucol eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.

oru kompu piṭikkūlum puḷikkompu piṭikkēṇam. (piṭikkūlum  		= piṭikkilum) 
oruttaṉāyāl orutti vēṇam.
kakkuvāṉ(kaṭkuvāṉ) pariccāl ñēluvāṉ parikkēṇam.
 ( = kaḷavu ceyyap paṭittāl tūkkiliṭappaṭavum paṭikkavēṇṭum.)
kaṭikkuṉṉatu karimpu, piṭikkuṉṉatu irimpu.
kaṇṭaṟiyāññāl koṇṭaṟiyum. (= tāṉē kaṇṭaṟiyātavaṉ  			taṇṭaṉiyāl aṟivāṉ.)
kaṇṭatellām koṇṭāl koṇṭatellām kaṭam.

kampiḷikkuṇṭō kaṟa?
karikkaṭṭa kaḻukuntōṟum kaṟukkum.
kaḻuttaṟuttāl kaṇkaṭṭu.
kākka nōkkaṟiyum, kāṭṭi āḷaṟiyum.

	kōṭāte kāṇi koṭuttāl kōṭi koṭutta palam.

	ācaviṭā. (vaṟṟōṉ = tūṇṭilāl mīṉ piṭippōṉ)
vāyikku nallatu vayiṟṟiṉ ṉākā.

viḷikkāte vaṉṉāl viḷampāte pōkēṇam.
vētam aṟiññālum vētaṉi viṭā.
(kuṟippu: maliyāḷattil ṟaṉṉakaram vaḻakkaṟṟuppōy ataṟkīṭākat tannakaramē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. vacatipaṟṟi īṇṭut tamiḻ muṟiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu.)
iṉi, moḻittuṟiyil maṭṭumaṉṟik kalittuṟiyilum paḻantamiḻ marapu maliyāḷa nāṭṭilēyē pōṟṟappaṭṭu varuvatik kāṇalām. mātaṅkaḷi ōrip peyarāṟ kuṟippatum katakaḷi   eṉṉuṅ kūttuvakiyum paḻantamiḻp paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷē.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, maliyāḷam vaṭamoḻi kalanta cēra nāṭṭuk koṭuntamiḻē eṉṟum, atu tamiḻttūymikkum tiraviṭac collārāycci moḻiyārāyccikkum peritum tuṇiyāvateṉṟum, atiluḷḷa teṉcoṟkaḷ kalantālaṉṟit tamiḻccoṟṟokuti nirampā teṉṟum teṟṟeṉat terintu  koḷka.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' navampar 1955
