tamiḻaṉiṉ piṟantakam
oru vīṭṭiṟku āvaṇam pōṉṟē, oru nāṭṭiṟku varalāṟu urimic cāṉṟākum. āyiṉ, ōr āvaṇattil etirikaḷāl ētēṉum karavaṭamākac cērkkappaṭalām. atupōṉṟē, orunāṭṭu varalāṟum pakivarāl avarkkēṟṟavāṟu māṟṟappaṭalām. ātalāl, iv viru vakiyilum, urimiyāḷar viḻippāyiruntu tam urimiyip pōṟṟik kāttukkoḷḷal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻaka varalāṟu, āriyar teṉṉāṭu vantatiliruntu kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭāka maṟikkappaṭṭum māṟṟappaṭṭum vantuḷḷatu. itaṉ uṇmiyāṉa nilimiyi aṟitaṟku, intiya varalāṟṟit teṟkiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅkal vēṇṭum. intiya varalāṟṟut tantiyākak karutappaṭum viṉceṉṟu cimitu (Vincent Smith), tam `intiya muntiya varalāṟu' (Early History of India) pottakattil, pērā. cuntaram piḷḷiyiṉ kūṟṟip piṉvarumāṟu eṭuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār:
INDIA PROPER IN THE SOUTH
``The attempt to find the basic element of Hindu civilization by a study of Sanskrit and the history of Sanskrit in Upper India is to begin the problem at its worst and most complicated point. India, South of the Vindhyas the Peninsular India-still continues to be India Proper. Here the bulk of the people continue distinctly to retin their pre-Aryan features, their pre-Aryan languages, their pre-Aryan social institutions. Even here, the process of Aryanization has gone indeed too far to leave it easy for the historian to distinguish the native warp from the foreign woof. But. if there is anywhere any chance of such successful disentanglement, it is in the South: and the farther South we go the larger does the chance Grow.
The scientific historian of  India, then, ought to begin his study with the basin of the Krishna, of the Cauvery, of the Vaigi, rather than with the Gangetic plin, as it has been, now long, too long, the fashion.''			- (Tamilan Antiquary, No. 2 (1908), p.4.)
in neṟimuṟiyik kaṭippiṭittut tamiḻaka varalāṟu varintavar pi.ṭi. cīnivāciyaṅkār, vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar eṉṉum iruvarē.
oru nāṭṭu varalāṟu varitaṟkup payaṉpaṭum mūlavāykaḷ (Sources) piṉvarumāṟu aivakippaṭum:
1. marapu vaḻakku (Tradition)
2. ayal nāṭṭār kuṟippu
3. tolporuṭ cāṉṟu (Archeological evidence)
4. nāṭṭilakkiyam
5. nāṭṭumoḻi.
paḻam pāṇṭināṭāṉa paṇṭit tamiḻakam kaṭaluḷ muḻukip pōṉataṉālum, āriyar varukikku muṟpaṭṭa aṉittilakkiyamum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭataṉālum tolporuṭ cāṉṟum paṇṭi ilakkiya mum tamiḻaka varalāṟṟiṟkup payaṉpaṭāvāṟu aṭiyōṭu iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl, iṉṟu varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟāka utavuvatu perumpālum moḻiyē.
iṟṟi ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ
tamiḻ tōṉṟiyatu teṉmāvāriyil muḻukippōṉa kumarināṭē. ātalāl, tamiḻaṉ tōṉṟiyatum an nāṭē. kumarikkaṇṭat teṉ kōṭiyil, paṉimalit toṭarpōlum paṉmaliyaṭukkattuk kumarimalit toṭarum, ataṉiṉṟu pāyntōṭiya paḵṟuḷiyeṉṉum kaṅki pōlum pēriyāṟum iruntaṉa. ap paḻam pāṇṭināṭṭi yāṇṭa pāṇṭi vēntaruḷ oruvaṉ neṭiyōṉ eṉpāṉ.
``munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ
naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē.'' 	(puṟam. 9: 10-11)
avaṉ kālattilō, piṉpō, oru peruṅ kaṭalkōḷ nikaḻntu, kumarimaliyum paḵṟuḷiyāṟum ataṉ kariyiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ talinakarākiya (teṉ) maturiyum uḷḷiṭṭa paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṉperum pakutiyi muḻukkiviṭṭatu. atuvē tamiḻilakkiyaṅ kūṟum mutal kaṭalkōḷ. kaṭalkōḷukkut tappiya allatu piṟpaṭṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ oruvaṉ, vaṭaticiyiluḷḷa paṉimaliyiyum kaṅkiyāṟṟiyuṅ kippaṟṟi, tāṉ iḻanta kumarimalikkum paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum īṭu ceytukoṇṭāṉ. itiyē,
``aṭiyiṟ ṟaṉṉaḷa varacark kuṇartti
vaṭivē leṟinta vāṉpaki poṟātu
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi''	(cilap. 11 : 17-22)
eṉṟu pāṭiṉār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ.
aṭiyiṟ ṟaṉṉaḷa varacark kuṇartti vaṭivēleṟintu vāṉpaki koṇṭavaṉ, neṭiyōṉukku muntiya vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ. neṭiyōṉ munnīrkku viḻāk koṇṭāṭi ataṉoṭu naṭpup pūṇṭavaṉ.
kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉpakuti mūḻkiyapiṉ, vaṭapakutiyil neytal nilattil, katavam (kapāṭam) allatu putavam allatu alivāy eṉṟu peyar peṟṟa iraṇṭām pāṇṭiyat talinakar tōṉṟiṟṟu. piṉpu atiyuṅ kaṭal koṇṭatu. aḵtu iraṇṭām kaṭalkōḷ.
ataṟkut tappiya pāṇṭiyaṉ, taṉpōl eñciyirunta taṉ kuṭikaḷic cōḻanāṭṭilum cēra nāṭṭilum ovvoru pakutiyik kippaṟṟik kuṭiyiruttiṉāṉ. itaṉiyē,
``malitiri yūrntutaṉ maṇkaṭal vuvaliṉ
meliviṉṟi mēṟceṉṟu mēvārnā ṭiṭampaṭap
puliyoṭu vilnīkkip pukaḻpoṟitta kiḷarkeṇṭi
valiyiṉāṉ vaṇakkiya vāṭāccīrt teṉṉavaṉ''	(104 : 1-4)
eṉṟu mullikkali kūṟum.
``cōḻanāṭ ṭelliyilē muttūrk kūṟṟamum cēra mānāṭṭuk kuṇṭūrk kūṟṟamum eṉṉum ivaṟṟi, iḻanta nāṭṭiṟkāka āṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ'' eṉṟu aṭiyārkkunallār urittatum itupaṟṟiyatē.
iṅṅaṉam, iru kaṭalkōḷkaḷāl kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭi yilirunta paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum vaṭakōṭiyilirunta kumariyāṟṟiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa nila nīṭciyi ``eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatavāṟu'' (ēṟattāḻa īrāyiram kal) eṉṟum, an nilapparappup pakutikaḷi, ``ēḻteṅka nāṭum, ēḻmaturi nāṭum, ēḻmuṉpāli nāṭum, ēḻpiṉpāli nāṭum, ēḻ kuṟumpāli nāṭum eṉṉum inta nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭum kumari, kollam mutaliya paṉmali nāṭum kāṭum natiyum patiyum'' eṉṟum, aṭiyārkku nallār urittiruppatu, kaṭukaḷavuṅ kaṭṭuc ceytiyāyirukka  muṭiyātu.
aruccuṉaṉ teṟkē tirunīrāṭṭiṟku vanta pāratak kālattilum, ataṟkup piṟpaṭṭa tolkāppiyar kālattilum (ki.mu. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭu) paḵṟuḷi maturi nōkki, vaṭamaturiyeṉa vaḻaṅkiya viki maturi tōṉṟa villi, kumariyāṟu ōṭiṟṟu. piṉṉar atuvum kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiṟṟu. ataṉ piṉṉarē, ``toṭiyōḷ puvam'' eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kuṟikkavum, ``taṭanīrk kumari vaṭaperuṅ kōṭṭiṉkāṟuṅ kaṭalkoṇ ṭoḻitalāl'' eṉṟu aṭiyārkkunallār urikkavum nērntatu. kumariyāṟṟik kaṭalkoṇṭatu mūṉṟāṅ kaṭalkōḷ.
maṇimēkali kālattiṟ pukār kaṭaluṭ pukuntatu nālāṅ kaṭalkōḷ. iṟiyaṉā rakapporu ḷuriyiluḷḷa mukkaḻaka varalāṟu, mutaliru kaṭalkōḷkaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. itu nampattakka ceytiyē.
paṉimali kaṭaluṭ patuṅkiyirunta toṉmutu paṇṭik kālattil, nāvalantīviṉ teṉpākam uyarntum vaṭapākam tāḻntum iruntaṉa. paṉimali kiḷarntu kumarimali nāṭu mūḻkiyapiṉ, vaṭapākam uyarntum teṉpākam tāḻntum, vaṭateṉ ticikaḷ muṟiyē uttaram takkaṇam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
o.nō:	kīḻ - kīḻkku - kiḻakku X mēl - mēṟku.
	u (mēl, uyar) + taram = uttaram.
	takku = tāḻvu. takkut toṇṭi = tāḻnta kural toṇṭi.
	takku = takkaṇam (Deccan).
valatti allatu valaticiyik kuṟikkum takṣaṇa (L. dexter-dexterity) eṉṉum vaṭacol takṣ mūlattiṉiṉṟu piṟantatātaliṉ, vēṟu collām. māṉiyar uvilliyamcu camaṟkiruta āṅkila akaramutaliyil uttara (uttara) eṉṉum collip pārkka.
tamiḻ teṟkē tōṉṟiya moḻiyātalāl, teṉmoḻi, teṉkali, teṉṉāṭu, teṉṉilam, teṉpulam, teṉpulattār, teṉpāli, teṉṉakam, teṉpāṇṭi, teṉmaturi, teṉṉaṉ, teṉṉavaṉ mutaliya colvaḻak keḻuntaṉa.
irāmaṉ taṉ kāṉaka vāḻkkiyil akattiyarik kaṇṭapōtu, piṉṉavar muṉṉavaṉi aṉpuṭaṉ varavēṟṟati, kalviyiṟ periyavarum pāvalaruḷ māvalarumāṉa kampar,
``niṉṟavaṉi vantaneṭi yōṉaṭipa ṇintāṉ
aṉṟavaṉu maṉpoṭuta  ḻīiyaḻuta kaṇṇāṉ
naṉṟuvara veṉṟupala nalluripa karntāṉ
eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟamiḻi yampiyici koṇṭāṉ''	(kamparā. akat. 47)
eṉṟu iṉitta taṉittamiḻp pāvāṟ pāṭi, tamiḻiṉ kaṉṉit tūymiyi yum, teṉkumarināṭṭut tōṟṟattiyum, eṉṟu maḻiyāt taṉmiyiyum, akattiyar atik kaṟṟut tērntatiyum muttami ḻilakkaṇam iyaṟṟip pukaḻ peṟṟatiyum, oruṅkē teḷḷat terivittār.
The Tamils Indigenous to South India
``Some writers conduct the ancient Dravidians with the self-confidence of a Cook's guide through the North-western or North-eastern mountin passes of India and drop them with a ready-made foreign culture on the banks of the Kaveri or the Vaigi. The slender evidence on which they rely for this elaborate theorizing is the fact that Brahui, a dialect spoken in the northern corner of India, possesses a few words allied to Tamil words. The only legitimate inference from this is that the Tamil language or a language allied to it previled upto the North-west province in ancient times. This inference is supported by another  fact, viz., that the modern dialects of Northern India now called Sanskritic or Gaudian, have a fundamental grammatical frame work and a scheme of syntax, the same as that of the Dravidian dialects, so much so that sentences from the one set of dialects can be translated into any one of the other set of dialects by the substitution of word for word without cusing any breach of idiom. These facts can only prove that people speaking dialects allied to Tamil once inhabited the whole of India and not that these people must necessarily have come into India from outside the country. No single fact has yet been adduced that compels us to believe that the ancient people of India were not autochthones.''
(The History of the Tamils by P. T. Srinivasa Iyenger, p.2.)
Where This Evolution First Took Place
The five sub-division of the habitable regions occur contiguous to each other and in a small fraction of the earth's surface in India South of the Vindhyas. It is therefore easy to understand how increase of population and alterations in the natural supply of food-stuffs brought about here at different periods the migration of men from region to region and the consequent development of the different stages of human culture, the hunter, the nomad, the pastoral, the coastal and the agricultural, due to the different stimuli provided by the changing milieu; in other words, the geographical control of the growth of human civilization can be worked out and set forth clear as on a map, by a study of man's progress in this restricted portion of the surface of the earth. Outside India, these five natural, regions occur on a vast scale, e.g., the Mulli, the vast steppe land extending from the Carpathians to the foot hills of the Altis, the Kurinji or the great mountin chin from the Pyrenees to the Himalayas and beyond, forming a great girdle round the waist of mother earth the Neydal, the coasts of the Mediterranean sea, and the Indian and the Atlantic oceans, and the Pali, the great desert of Sahara and its continuation in Arabia, Persia and Mongolia''. (Ibid., p.14.)
The myth of the importation of the South Indian languages from outside India.
``Certin investigators assume that everything Indian must have been imported at some remote times from outside India. They hold that the speakers of primitive Tamil and other South Indian languages, to whom they give the name of the Dravidian race, must have settled in India from Central Asia. These theorists drag large bodies of ancient along the map of Asia and Europe as easily as chessmen are shifted on the chess-board, and make them wander aimlessly from country to country, merely to support their baseless theories. It happens that certin words of the Brahui dialect of Baluchistan resemble certin Tamil words. To explin this solitary fact, which is susceptible of various interpretations, they will have it that an ancient Dravidian race, evolved somewhere outside India in the Asiatic highlands, undertook a pilgrimage to India through the North-western Gate, and reached the extreme South through strange and devious ways. Foote constructs an itinerary for the wandering of these peoples, which is a specimen of perverse ingenuity without any historicl evidence to support it. Surely the Neolithians of India were not dumb animals, but spoke languages of their own. The simplest hypothesis under the circumstances is, as Tamil traditional history holds, that the Tamils and other allied peoples were indigenous and their languages were evolved where they are now spoken''
(Stone Age in India by P.T. Srinivasa Iyengar, pp. 46-7)
pērā. vi. ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar āṅkilattil eḻutiya `varalāṟṟu muṉṉit teṉṉintiyā' (Pre-Historic South India), tamiḻar tōṟṟamum paravalum' (Origin and Spread of the Tamils) eṉṉum iru varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ, tamiḻar kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟip pāreṅkum paraviyati naṭuniliyākavum maṟuppiṟ kiṭamiṉṟiyum viḷakkik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
vaṭanūlkaḷuḷ, catapata pirāmaṇam, oru teṉticik kaṭal kōḷukkut tappiya pāṇṭiyaṉit tiraviṭapatiyeṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
moḻiyiyaṟ cāṉṟukaḷ
1. tamiḻum ataṉ tiripāṉa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷum intiyāviṟ kuḷḷēyē vaḻaṅkutalum, avaṟṟuḷ ilakkiyamuḷḷa mutaṉmiyāṉa nāṉmoḻiyum teṉṉintiyāviṟkuḷḷēyē vaḻaṅkutalum, avaṟṟuḷ ḷum talimiyāṉa tamiḻ teṉkōṭiyil vaḻaṅkutalum, ataṉ vaḻakkum teṟkiṟ ciṟantiruttalum, vaṭakkē cellac cella ēṉit tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ ilakkaṇamiṉṟiyum ilakkiya miṉṟiyum paṇpāṭiṉṟiyum colvaḷamiṉṟiyum kocciyākiyum mēṉmēlum ciṟuttum citintum kalaviyākiyum pōvatum, intiyāviṟku veḷiyē citinta tamiḻ allatu tiraviṭac coṟkaḷaṉṟi moḻiyētum vaḻaṅkāmiyum.
2. intiyiṉ aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ citinta teṉ collā yiruttal.
mutal karupporuṭ cāṉṟukaḷ
1. kāla vēṟupāṭum iyaṟki nilimikaḷum uyiriṉa vakikaḷum viḷivup poruḷkaḷum toṉṟutoṭṭu māṟātiruntu varutal.
2. cilappatikārattil collappaṭṭuḷḷa kārōtimam (kāraṉṉam) iṉṟu āttirēliyāvilum tācumēṉiyāvilum maṭṭum iruttalum, teṉṟicip peyarukkuk kāraṇamāṉa teṉṉimaram teṉkiḻakkut tīvukaḷil maṭṭum iyaṟkiyāka vaḷartalum.
maṟuppiṟku maṟuppu
mēṉāṭṭu iṭappeyarīṟukaṭkut tamiḻilēyē poruḷiruttalālum, kumari nāṭṭiliruntu naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkari nāṭṭiṟku mutaṟkaṇ ceṉṟa makkaḷ nīḷmaṇṭiyarā yiruntataṉālum, ūr eṉṉum marutanilak kuṭiyiruppuppeyar kumarināṭṭilēyē tōṉṟiyamiyālum, paruṅkaṟ kaṭṭaṭa amippē ciṟukaṟ kaṭṭaṭa amippiṟku muntuvatu iyaṟkiyāta lālum, ilākōvāri (Lahovary), pālakiruṭṭiṇaṉ nāyar mutaliyōr tamiḻiṉ allatu tamiḻariṉ toṉmiyi maṟuppatu aṟiyāmi paṟṟiyatē yeṉṟu kūṟi viṭukka.
muṭinta muṭipu
tamiḻ, teṉṉāṭṭiṟkum teṟkil teṉmāvāriyil muḻukik kiṭakkum teṉkaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭi nāṭāṉa teṉpāliyil tōṉṟiya teṉmoḻiyē. ataṉāl, atit tōṟṟi vaḷartta tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamum at teṉpulamē.
iv vaṭippaṭiyilēyē, tamiḻiṉ amippum ataṉ tiraviṭat tiripum āriya vēṟupāṭum teḷivāṉa viḷakkam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. iv vuṇmiyi aṟiyātār allatu uṇarātār tamiḻiṉ iyalpi maṭṭumaṉṟi oppiyaṉ moḻinūliyum aṟiyātavarē yāvar.

tamiḻaṉ urimi vēṭki
urimi eṉṟāl eṉṉa?
māntaṉ iv vulakil irukki, kālantaḷḷukiyum vāḻkkiyum vāḻvum eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. varumāṉam, nukarporuḷ, uṭalnalam mutaliyaṉa kuṉṟiyiruttal kālantaḷḷuki; avi iṉṟiyamiyāta aḷaviruttal vāḻkki ; avi aḷaviṟantiruttal vāḻvu. ivaṟṟip pulvāḻkki, aḷavu vāḻkki, nalvāḻkki eṉṟuṅ kūṟalām.
vāḻvum, urimi vāḻvu, aṭimi vāḻvu eṉa iruvakippaṭum.
taṉ illattiṟ kuṭiyiruntu, uṭaluḻippālō maṉavuḻippālō tāṉē tēṭiya poruḷik koṇṭu, iṟivaṉaṭiyār, viruntāḷar, ēḻiyuṟaviṉar, irappōr, pulavar ākiya aivakuppārkku aḷittuṇṭu, taṉ tāymoḻi ilakkiyac cemmoḻiyāyiṉ atil iyaṉṟavari tūymiyākap pēciyeḻuti, kaṭavuḷ nampikkiyiruppiṉ taṉ tāy moḻiyilēyē vaḻipaṭṭu, taṉ muṉṉōr vaḷartta paṇpāṭṭip pōṟṟikkāttu, pakuttaṟivōṭum taṉmāṉattōṭum vāḻvatē, taṉimakaṉ urimi vāḻvu. tam nāṭṭil allatu tamakkeṉṟuriya oru taṉinilap pakutiyil vatintu, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu vāḻntu varuvatē oru nāṭṭiṉattiṉ allatu makkaḷ vakuppiṉ urimi vāḻvu.
tammil iruntu tamatupāt tuṇṭaṟṟāl	(kuṟaḷ. 1107)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟutal kāṇka.
kalvikkum ayalnāṭṭuṟaviṟkum oru moḻiyit terintu koḷvatum, urimiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatē.
mūvaki vāḻkkiyum, urimiyiṉṟēl aṭimi vāḻkkiyē.
tamiḻaṉukku urimiyuṇṭā?
tamiḻaṉ eṉṟatu iṟṟit tamiḻaṉi.
oru vīṭṭiṟku āvaṇam pōl, oru nāṭṭiṟku urimic cāṉṟu uṇmiyāṉa varalāṟu. inta nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattilēyē, maṉōṉmaṇīya āciriyar cuntarampiḷḷi, intiyā cariyāṉapaṭi teṟkiluḷḷateṉṟum, intiya varalāṟṟit teṟkiṉiṉṟē toṭaṅka vēṇṭum eṉṟum, eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭār. intiya varalāṟṟut tantiyeṉat takum viṉceṉṟu cimitum, tam muntukāla intiyā (Early India) eṉṉum varalāṟṟup pottakattil, cuntaram piḷḷiyiṉ kūṟṟip pārāṭṭi, etirkāla intiya varalāṟṟu āciriyarellārum tam varalāṟṟu nūlit teṟkiṉiṉṟē toṭaṅka vēṇṭumeṉṟu valuvuṟac cārnturittār.
ak koḷkiyit taḻuvi, pi.ti.cīnivāciyaṅkār intiyak kaṟkālam (Stone Age In India), āriyarkku muṉṉit tamiḻk kali nākarikam (Pre-Aryan Tamil Culture), tamiḻar varalāṟu (History of the Tamils) mutaliya nūlkaḷiyum; vi.rā. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar tamiḻar tōṟṟamum paravalum (Origin and Spread of the Tamils), varalāṟṟiṟku muṉṉit teṉṉintiyā (Pre-Historic South India) mutaliya nūlkaḷiyum, evarum maṟukka muṭiyātavāṟu tiṟampaṭa eḻutip pōyiṉar.
āyiṉ, avar maṟintapiṉ, pērā. kē. nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyār avar koḷkikkuṅ kūṟṟiṟkum nērmāṟāka, āriya vētattiyē aṭippaṭi yākak koṇṭu intiya varalāṟṟi vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki, uṇmik kum uttikkum ovvāvāṟu pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉip peṟṟāṉ eṉpatupōl, tamiḻar varalāṟṟit talikīḻāka varintuḷḷār.
tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam teṟkil muḻukip pōṉa kumarināṭeṉṉum aṭippaṭiyilēyē, tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyum tamiḻaṉ uyarviyum, uṇmiyāka uṇartaloṇṇum.
varalāṟṟiṟku muṉṉit teṉṉintiyā eṉṉum varalāṟṟup pottakam ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka veḷiyīṭāyiruntum, atip paṭiyāmiyālō, tam iyaṟkiyāṉa aṭimittaṉattālō, viyāpurit taṉmiyālō, talimip patavit tamiḻp pērāciriyarum pērāciriyar nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyāri etirkkat tuṇivatilli. ataṉāl, tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyum, muṉmiyum, tāymiyum, talimiyum tamiḻnāṭṭilum oppukkoḷḷappaṭāmalē yirukkiṉṟaṉa.
oru moḻiyiṉ ciṟappi eṭuttukkāṭṭa, akaramutalipōṟ ciṟantatu vēṟoṉṟumilli. ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali (Lexicon) 23 āṇṭukaḷākap potumakkaḷ paṇattil nālariyilakkam urūpā celaviṭṭut tokukkappaṭṭatu. āyiṉ, mūla (āti) viyā puriyārit tuṇikkoṇṭu tamiḻp pakivarāṉa pirāmaṇat tamiḻp pulavarē atit tokuttataṉāl, tamiḻ oru puṉciṟu putukkalavi moḻiyeṉak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. av vakaramutaliyiṉ kuṟṟaṅkuṟikaḷi yellān teḷḷat teḷivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyum, taṉṉalap piṇṭaṅ kaḷāṉa talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyaruṭ cilarum atip puṟakkaṇiyātu pōṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam, tamiḻ varalāṟu maṟikkappaṭṭum, maṟukkappaṭṭum, tamiḻmoḻi tāḻttappaṭṭum vīḻttappaṭṭum iruppatāl, tamiḻ aṉittintiya āṭci moḻiyākātum, tirukkōvil vaḻipāṭu tamiḻil naṭipeṟātum pōkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl tamiḻ vaḷarcciyum tamiḻaṉ muṉṉēṟṟamum taṭippaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ovvoru nāṭṭilum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟum vaḻi moḻiyē. atu tāymoḻiyākavum irukkalām; ayaṉ moḻiyākavum irukkalām. tāymoḻiyil muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkēṟṟa ilakkiyamillāviṭiṉ, aḵtuḷḷa ayaṉmoḻiyē muṉṉēṟṟa vaḻiyākum. itaṟku, āppirikka ātti rēliya amerikkap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷē taliciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭāvar.
tamiḻil ōraḷavu paṇṭiyilakkiyam iruppiṉum, ikkālak kalvi aṟiviyamum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa nuṇkammiyamumē yātalāl, makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku āṅkilam pōṉṟa oru mēli moḻiyaṟivu iṉṟiyamiyātatākiṉṟatu. ataṉāl, atu ayalārāl valintu pukuttap paṭātu makkaḷālēyē virumpik kaṟkappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu aṭimit taṉamaṉṟu.
āyiṉ, ulakil vēṟeṅkaṇum kāṇamuṭiyāta oru vēṭikkiyum viyappumāṉa moḻiyaṭimittaṉam, in nāvalan tēyattil, ciṟappākat tamiḻnāṭṭil, kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭāka iruntuvarukiṉṟatu. atu vētamoḻiyum camaṟkirutamum tēvamoḻiyeṉpatum, ati ilakkiya vaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkuvōr nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉpatum, atiṉiṉṟu ēṉi moḻikaḷellām kaṭaṉ koḷḷumeṉpatum, atu maṭṭum vēṟem moḻiyiṉiṉṟum kaṭaṉ koḷḷāteṉpatum, atu pirāmaṇaṉālēyē cariyākap palukkappaṭum (uccarikkappaṭum) eṉpatum, ataṉ palukkam immi tiṟampiṉum, peruṅkēṭu viḷiyum eṉpatum, atiṟ ceyyappaṭum vaḻipāṭē iṟivaṉukkup pēriṉpan taruvateṉpatum, pirāmaṇaṉukkut toṇṭu ceyvatē aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar, kittoḻilāḷar ākiya piṟavakuppārellām uyyum vaḻi yeṉpatum, piṟavumām.
iv vulakil toṉṟutoṭṭu vāḻntu varupavar makkaḷē. ātalāl, ulakamoḻikaḷ ellām makkaḷ moḻikaḷē. vēta moḻiyō camaṟ kirutamō tēvamoḻiyāyiṉ, avaṟṟōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuṟṟa kirēkka moḻiyum, ataṟkiṉamāṉa ilattīṉam, cerumāṉiyam mutaliya airōppiya moḻikaḷum tēvamoḻikaḷā yiruttal vēṇṭum. avi aṅṅaṉam illi.
pirāmaṇariṉ muṉṉōr veṇṇiṟattarā yiruntataṉāl tēva reṉap paṭṭāreṉiṉ, iṉṟu avar vaḻiyiṉar. an niṟattarāyilli. iṉṟu airōppiyarum avar vaḻiyiṉarumē nilattēvar eṉappaṭal vēṇṭum.
vētam eṉṉum maṟinūlāl āriyam tēvamoḻiyāyiṟṟeṉiṉ ciṟuteyva vaḻuttut tiraṭṭākiya vētattiṉum vīṭupēṟaḷikkum perun tēva vaḻuttut tiraṭṭum kaṭavuḷ vaḻuttut tiraṭṭumākiya tamiḻmaṟikaḷ, kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa maṭaṅku ciṟantaṉavātal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, vētamoḻi vallōciyum mūccoliyum nirampiyataṉāl tēvamoḻi yeṉappaṭum eṉiṉ, av viraṇṭum paṉmaṭaṅku ciṟanta kāḷavāyk kattalum tēvamoḻik kiṉamātal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, vētamoḻiyum camaṟkirutamum ettakiya moḻikaḷ eṉpatip pārppōm.
vētamoḻi eṉpatu ettakiyatu?
vēta āriyar mikac ciṟupāṉmiyarā yiruntataṉāl, kirēkkattiṟku mika nerukkamāyirunta avar moḻi, akkālattu vaṭanāṭṭu vaṭṭāra moḻiyākiya pirākirutattoṭu iraṇṭaṟak kalantu, ataṉ pāṉmiyatāki ekara okarak kuṟil iḻantu irumoḻik kalaviyāyiṟṟu.
camaṟkirutam ettakiyatu?
tamiḻiluḷḷa paltuṟi yilakkiyatti moḻipeyarttaṟku vēta moḻi pōtiya colvaḷamiṉṟi yiruntataṉāl, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tamiḻc coṟkaḷi vētamoḻiyōṭu kalantu, camaṟkirutam eṉṉum aric ceyaṟki yilakkiya moḻiyi uṇṭākkiṉar.
camaṟkirutam (samskruta) eṉṟālē kalantu ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉpatutāṉ poruḷ.
kummutal = kūṭutal, kalattal. kum - kummal = kumpal, kuviyal.    L. cumulus, heap. itiṉiṉṟē cumulate, accumulate mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
kum - kummi = ki kuvittaṭittup pāṭiyāṭum kūttu.
kum - kumi - kuvi. kumi - kumiḻ - kumiḻi = kuvinta nīr moṭṭu. kuvi - kuviyal, kuvāl, kuvi.
kum - kumpu - kumpal. kumpu - kuppu - kuppal, kuppi, kuppam, koppam.
kumpu - kūmpu - kūppu, kūmputal = kuvital.
ki kūpputal = ki kuvittal.
kumpu - kumpiṭu, kumpiṭutal = ki kuvittal.
kum - kom - kommi. kommi koṭṭutal = ki taṭṭi yaḻittal.
kummi - kommi = kummiyāṭṭam.
kommi - te. koppi (gobbi).
Cum (kum) eṉpatu ilattīṉ moḻiyil oru kūṭṭiṇippuc col (Cumulative Conjunction). atu āṅkilattil com, con, col, cor, co eṉṟu tirintu with, together, altogether, completely eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum muṉṉoṭṭu (prefix) ākum.
atuvē kirēkka moḻiyil sym, syn, syl, sy eṉṟu tirintu together, together with, in union with eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum muṉṉoṭṭākum.
atuvē camaṟkirutattil sam eṉṟu tirintu, with, together with, along with, together, altogether eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum muṉṉoṭṭākum.
samskruta eṉṉum colliṟku, put together, constructed, well or completely formed, perfected eṉṟu māṉiyar uvilliyamcu camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akaramutali poruḷ kūṟutal kāṇka.
karuttal eṉpatu, ceytal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭṭuk kaṭṭal (kaḷavu ceytal), viṉṉal (viṉavutal) eṉpaṉa pōlak kumarināṭṭil vaḻaṅkip piṉṉar vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉa oru tūya teṉcol.
tamiḻakattil toṉṟutoṭṭu veḷḷāḷar, kārāḷar eṉṟu iruvēṟu niṟattār iruntuvantirukkiṉṟaṉar. aḷaviṟantu veyiliṟ kāypavar karuttiruttalum, niḻalil vēli ceypavar veḷuttiruttalum perumpāṉmi yiyalpu. kiyiṟ kāḻppēṟa vēliceyyiṉ karuppar ki karuttum, civappar ki civantum pōkum. ataṉāl, ceytalik kuṟikkak karuttal ceytal, eṉa iru viṉiccoṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ceyta ki cēvēṟum ceyyāta ki nōvēṟum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
karu - karumam - kammam = kammiyat toḻil.
kammañcey mākkaḷ	(nālaṭi. 393)
kammam - kam - kammavar - kammavāru (te.) = mutal toḻilāṉa uḻavic ceyyum teluṅka vakuppār.

kammāḷaṉ - pirā. kammāra, (va.) kārmāra.
kam - kammi = 1. toḻilāḷi, maṭkalañcey kammi (pārata. tiru. 64) 2. kaṟṟaccaṉ.

kammiyaṉ = 1. toḻilāḷi kammiyarum ūrvar kaḷiṟu (cīvaka. 495) 2. kammāḷaṉ 3. kaṟṟaccu.
kammiya nūl = kaṭṭaṭa nūl (ciṟpa cāstiram) ``kammiyanūṟ ṟolvaram pelli kaṇṭu (tiruviḷi. tirunakaraṅ. 38)
kammakkuṭam = kaṉṉāṉ ceyta kuṭam (naṉ. 222, mayili).
kammakārar = 1. kalattaccar 2. kappalōṭṭikaḷ. kammakārar koṇṭāṭumpaṭi naṉṟāka ōṭiṟṟu (cīvaka. 501, uri)
karu eṉṉum mutaṉiliyik kru eṉṟu tirippar vaṭamoḻi yāḷar.
o.nō. : turu (turuvu) - (va.) tru. peru - (va.) pruh.
poṟu - (va.) pru (bhru).

karuttal, ceytal eṉṉum iraṇṭum uṟaviyaṟ coṟkaḷ (Relative terms)
karu - karuvi = karumañ ceyvataṟ kētuvāṉa tuṇipporuḷ. ic col vaṭamoḻiyil illi.
karu + aṇam = karaṇam = 1. ceyki. 2. karuvi. ic colliṉiṉṟu kāraṇa, kārya eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷip piṟappittuḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. ivi mēliyāriya moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟilumilli.
karuttal, ceytal eṉṉum iruviṉikaḷum, veḷuttal (calavi ceytal) eṉpatupōla, taṉviṉiyākavum piṟaviṉiyākavum āḷappeṟṟaṉa.
camaṟkirutam eṉṉum moḻic ciṟappup peyar maṭṭumaṉṟip pāṣi (pāṣā) eṉṉum potuppeyarum teṉcol tiripē.
oruvar vāyiṉiṉṟu coṟkaḷ varuvatu, oru tuḷiyiṉiṉṟu nīr oḻukuvatum vāṉattiṉiṉṟu maḻi peyvatum pōṉṟirukkiṟatu. nīroḻukkuc ciṟuttiruppiṉ peytal eṉṟum, tiraṇṭiruppiṉ poḻital eṉṟum collappaṭum. aṅṅaṉamē, oruvar kaṭalmaṭi tiṟantāṟpōl taṭṭuttaṭiyiṉṟip pēciṉ, kōṭi maḻipōṟ poḻikiṟār eṉṟu colvatu vaḻakkam. atupaṟṟiyē coṟpoḻivu eṉṉum kūṭṭuc collum tōṉṟiṟṟu. ākavē, virintu pēcuvatu poḻivatum viriviṉṟip pēcuvatu peyvatum ākum eṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.
peytal eṉṟa col kocci vaḻakkil pēytal eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum. ataṉāl, pēyntuṅ keṭṭatu, ōyntuṅ keṭṭatu eṉṉum paḻamoḻi eḻuntatu.
pēy - pēyu - pēcu - pēccu.
pēcu - (va.) pāṣ (bhash) - pāṣā
o.nō. : tēy-tēyu-tēcu
(va.) tējas. tēcu - (i.) tēj.
kocci vaḻakkiṉiṉṟum ceñcol allatu ilakkaṇac col piṟappatuṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	koṇṭuvā - koṇṭā - koṇā - koṇar. koṇṭā eṉpatu koccic collākavum, koṇā, koṇar eṉpaṉa ceñcollākavum, koḷḷappaṭutal kāṇka.
iṉi, āriya mutaṉūlāṉatum, iṟivaṉālum iyaṟṟappaṭātu eṉṟumuḷḷatākac collappaṭuvatumāṉa vētattiṉ peyarē, teṉ colliṉ tiripāyiruppatu, ciṟappākak kavaṉikkat takkatum mika makiḻcci taruvatumākum.
viḻittal = 1. kaṇtiṟattal, 2. kavaṉittup pārttal, 3. aṟital,           4. viḷaṅkutal, 5. teḷital.

ceviyuṟutal = kēṭṭal.

o.nō. vāṇikam - vāṇipam. maṇṭapam - (va) maṇṭapa.
muṉ - maṉ = ciṟappākak karutiyaṟiyum māntaṉ, maṉpati = makkaṭ kūṭṭam.
OE. man, OS., OHG, man, Goth. manna,
Skt. maṉu - maṉuṣya - (maṉuṭaṉ, māṉiṭaṉ, maṉitaṉ)
iṅṅaṉam, camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷuḷ ainti lirupakuti tamiḻ. camaṟkirutattilum ēṉiyāriya moḻikaḷilum uḷḷa cuṭṭuc coṟkaṭ kellām mūlam, a,i,u ākiya tamiḻ muccuṭṭukaḷē.
kiṟittuviṟku muṟpaṭṭa kālattilēyē, āriyap pūcāriyar tammi nilattēvareṉṟum tam ilakkiya moḻiyit tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum colli, palvēḷviccāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti, irāca cūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷi, palyāṉic celkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉ pōṉṟa mūvēntari yum ēmāṟṟi, ākamaṅkaḷi eḻuti vittukkoṇṭaṉar. avi aṟivārāycci mikka ikkālattiṟ cellā.
nāṭu tamiḻnāṭu. makkaḷ tamiḻmakkaḷ. matam tamiḻar matam. kōvil tamiḻaracar allatu tiraviṭa aracar allatu tamiḻ nāṭṭaracar kaṭṭiyavi. avaṟṟiluḷḷa teyvap paṭimikaḷ tamiḻak kammiyar cetukkiyavi. ennāṭṭilum tāymoḻiyil vaḻipāṭu ceyvatē, iyalpum muṟimiyum payaṉpaṭuvatum iṟivaṉukku ēṟpumākum. camaṟkirutam ayaṉmoḻiyum, perumpālum tamiḻk kalappā lāṉatum, tamiḻi oḻikka vēṇṭumeṉṟē puṉiyappaṭṭuk karavākap pukuttappaṭṭatum, eṅkēṉum eṉṟēṉum vaḻaṅkātu ilakkiya moḻiyākavē yiruntu varuvatum, tamiḻarkku immiyum poruḷ teriyātatum, āriyap pūcāriyarkkum teḷivāy viḷaṅkātatum, tamiḻar muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku valutta muṭṭukkaṭṭiyāṉatum, tamiḻiṉa oṟṟumiyik kulippatum, avaratu aṭimittaṉatti niliniṟuttuvatumāṉa pōlimoḻi yātalāl, tirukkōvil tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭi vēṭpatum, ataṟkēṟṟa caṭṭam iyaṟṟuvatum, iṟivaṉum etirkkavoṇṇāp piṟappurimiyē. ati maṟuttaṟku oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ (U.N.) cārpāṉa ulakat tīrppu maṉṟattiṟkum atikāramilli.
tamiḻ viṭutaliyē tamiḻaṉ viṭutali. tamiḻaṉ iṉṉum viṭutali peṟavilli.
tamiḻar, tām kumarināṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ vaḻiyiṉar eṉṉum varalāṟṟuṇmiyaṟintu, muḻuvurimiyum virintu peṟuka.
- ``urimi vēṭki'' poṅkal malar 1975
19
urimippēṟu
oruvarkku uriyatu urimi. atu puṟavuṭal paṟṟiyatum akavuṭal paṟṟiyatum āka irutiṟappaṭum. muṉṉatu ellārkkum potu; piṉṉatu paṇpaṭṭa makkaṭkē ciṟappu. māntarkku ovvoru niliyilum ovvoru allatu ciṟcila urimikaḷ tēvi. avi nīrvēṭkiyum uṇavu vēṭkiyum pōṉṟavi. avaṟṟi muyaṉṟō, piṟari vēṇṭiyō, avarōṭu pōrāṭiyō, peṟal vēṇṭum.
irutiṟa vurimikaḷum piṉvarumāṟu aivakippaṭum.
1. vaḷarvurimi
kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyariṉ iṉpak kūṭṭattōṭu piḷḷippēṟu toṭarpupaṭuttappaṭṭiruppatāl, peṟṟōr virumpiṉum virumpā viṭiṉum perumpālum piḷḷikaḷ piṟantuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. nilattil vititta viti muḷittal pōṉṟē piḷḷippēṟum iruppatāl, 
kēḷātē vantu kiḷikaḷā yiṟṟōṉṟi	(30)
eṉṟu nālaṭiyār kūṟuvatu poruntātu. piḷḷikaḷ peṟṟōr viṉiyālēyē peṟappaṭuvatāl, avaṟṟi vaḷarppatu avar kaṭamiyum, avarāl vaḷarkkappaṭuvatu avaṟṟiṉ urimiyum, ākiṉṟatu.
emmik kēḷātu ēṉ piṟantāy?
eṉṟu, peṟṟōr tam piḷḷi vaḷarppit taṭṭik kaḻittuviṭa muṭiyātu.
kuḻavi piṟantavuṭaṉ uṇaviṟku aḻuvatē, ōr urimi vēṭki yum atip peṟum muyaṟciyum ākiṉṟatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil tāy vīṭṭu vēliyil āḻntirukkumpōtu, uriya vēḷiyil taṉ cēykkup pāl koṭātiruppatum uṇṭu. av vēḷiyil kuḻantiyiṉ aḻukiyoli kēṭṭut tāy pāl koṭuppatu vaḻakkam. ataṉāl, aḻutapiḷḷi pāl kuṭikkum eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum eḻuntatu.
kuḻavip paruvattilum kuḻantip paruvattilum piḷḷip paruvattilum vaḷarppatum, piyaṟ paruvattilum iḷamaṭṭap paruvattilum uṇavaḷittuk kāppatōṭu ōr aluvaṟkō toḻilukkō payiṟṟu vippatum, paruvam vantapiṉ maṇañceytu vippatum, peṟṟōriṉ kaṭamiyām. annaṉmikaḷip peṟuvatu piḷḷikaḷiṉ urimiyām.
kittoḻiṟ payiṟciyiṉuṅ kalvip payiṟci ciṟantatātalālum, iruvakip payiṟciyum oruṅkē peṟutaluṅ kūṭumātalālum,
tanti makaṟkāṟṟum naṉṟi aviyattu
munti yiruppac ceyal.	(kuṟaḷ. 67)
īṉṟa poḻutiṟ perituvakkum taṉmakaṉic
cāṉṟōṉ eṉakkēṭṭa tāy	(kuṟaḷ. 69)
eṉṟu tiruvāymalarntār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
2. vāḻvurimi
oruvar iv vulakil vāḻvataṟkuk kalvip payiṟci allatu toḻiṟ payiṟci, vēlippēṟu, pātukāppu - im mūṉṟum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa. oru kuṭumpattiṟ piṟantavaṉukku eṅṅaṉam vaḷarvurimiyuṇṭō, aṅṅaṉamē oru nāṭṭiṟ piṟantavaṉukku vāḻvurimiyuṇṭu. ataṟkuriya mūṉṟiyum aḷikkak kūṭiyatum kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷatum, āḷum aracē.
payiṟciyiṉṟi evvēliyum peṟavum  ceyyavum iyalātāki yāl, nāṭṭiṟ piṟantavarkkellām, avaravar tiṟami, viruppam, uṭaṟkūṟu, maṉappāṉmi, mativaṉmi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkut takkavāṟu, vevvēṟu tuṟiyiṟ payiṟciyaḷittal vēṇṭum. eṇṇum eḻuttum kaṇṇeṉat takum. ātalāl, mutaṟkaṇ, nālakavikku mēṟpaṭṭa ellāp piḷḷi kaṭkum ilavacak kaṭṭāyat tuvakkak kalviyaḷittal vēṇṭum. enta amippum niliyākavum uṟutiyākavum iruttaṟku aṭippaṭi akaṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl, mēṟpaṭi kulintupōm.
viḷiyum payir muḷiyil teriyum.'' ātalāl, tuvakkappaḷḷik kalvip paruvattilēyē, peṟṟōrum āciriyarum piḷḷikaḷiṉ tiṟamiyiyum maṉappāṉmiyiyum kūrntu nōkkiyaṟintu, akakkaraṇa valimi viñciyavari mēṟkalvip payiṟcikkum, puṟakkaraṇa valimi viñciyavarit toḻiṟpayiṟcikkum, aṉupputal vēṇṭum.
mēṟkalvip payiṟciyilum, aṉpum toṇṭu maṉappāṉmiyum uḷḷavari maruttuvak kalvikkum nāvaṉmiyum niṉivāṟṟalum naṭuniliyum uḷḷavaric caṭṭak kalvikkum, ōviya varivāṟṟalum putup puṉivut tiṟaṉum kaṇita matiyum uḷḷavarip poṟiviṉi allatu cūḻcciyaviṉik kalvikkum, iṭiviṭāp paṭippum eṭuttu viḷakkun tiṟaṉum uḷḷavari āciriyap payiṟcik kalvikkum, vaḷarttiyum uṭal valimiyum añcāmiyum tuṇiceyal maṟamum uḷḷavarip paṭit tuṟik kalvikkum, iṉkuralum iciviruppum nīḷviralum uḷḷavari icikkalvikkum, teyvappattiyum tūyavoḻukkamum maṟinūl viruppum uḷḷavari mataviyaṟ kalvikkum, ivvāṟē piṟa tuṟikaṭkum terinteṭuttal vēṇṭum.
kallūrikaḷil iṭamillāviṭiṉ, putukkallūrikaḷit tōṟṟu vittal vēṇṭum. kārik kaṭṭaṭam illāviṭiṉ kūrik kaṭṭaṭatti lēṉum toṭaṅkutal vēṇṭum.
kaṇitam vēṇṭiyavaṉi varalāṟṟu vakuppilum, maruttuvap payiṟci virumpupavaṉic caṭṭak kallūriyilum, iṅṅaṉamē ēṉit tuṟikaḷilum tāṟumāṟākac cērppatu, vaṇṭiyiṟ kaṭṭavēṇṭiya kāḷiyi vīṭṭuk kāvaṟkum, vīṭṭuk kāvaṟkuriya nāyi iṟiviyēṟṟattiṟkum, poti cumakka vēṇṭiya kaḻutiyi ūrvalattiṟkum, ūrvalattiṟkuriya yāṉiyi narakal vaṇṭikkum, vippatum kaṭṭuvatum amarttuvatum pōṉṟatē.
toḻiṟpayiṟciyāyiṉum mēṟkalvip payiṟciyāyiṉum, payiṟci peṟṟa ellārum vēlikkuriyar. palarukku vēliyilli yeṉṟu kivirippatu poṟuppāṭciyaṉṟu. makkaḷtoki mikkiruppiṉ, atip palvēṟu vaḻikaḷil maṭṭuppaṭuttalām. āyiṉ,
irantum uyirvāḻtal vēṇṭiṉ parantu
keṭuka ulakiyaṟṟi yāṉ	(kuṟaḷ. 1062)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar iṟivaṉ mītē vaci pāṭiyiruttali nōkkuka.
makkaḷtoki maṭṭiṟku miñciya ikkālattil, taṉiyuṭimi yāṭcikku iṭamiṉṟēl, aḷavuṭimi yāṭciyum, atuvum pōtā teṉiṉ kūṭṭuṭimi yāṭciyum, iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavām. oru nāṭṭiṟ piṟantu vēlip payiṟci peṟṟa ellārkkum kulamatak kaṭci vēṟu pāṭiṉṟit tiṟamikkēṟṟavāṟu vēliyaḷippatē kūṭṭuṭimiyāṭci yām. atuvē iraciyāvilum cīṉāvilum iṉṟu iruntuvaruvatu. atip potuvuṭimiyāṭciyeṉpatu tavaṟu. potuvuṭamiyāṭci paṇamiṉṟik kuṭumpa vāḻkkipōl naṭappatu. atu oru nāṭṭiṟku ēṟkātu.
pakuttuṇṭu palluyir ōmputal nūlōr
tokuttavaṟṟuḷ ellān tali	(kuṟaḷ. 302)
eṉṟu akkālattilēyē tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
aḷavuṭimiyāṭci yeṉiṉum varampuṭimiyāṭci yeṉiṉum okkum.
iṉi, kuruṭar muṭavar mutaliyōr aiyam (picci) peṟuvatum, vaṟiya nāṭukaḷ celva nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu utavi peṟuvatum vāḻvurimiyiṉpāṟ paṭuvatē,
3. viṭutaliyurimi
taṉippaṭṭa aṭimi taṉ talivaṉiṭattiṉiṉṟum, paṇṇi yāḷkaḷ tam āṇṭiyiṉiṉṟum, oru kulattār iṉṉoru kulattāri ṉiṉṟum, oru nāṭṭār iṉṉoru nāṭṭāriṉiṉṟum, viṭutali peṟuvatu viṭutaliyurimiyām.
āṭavarpōṟ kalvi peṟuvatum tērtalkaḷiṟ kuṭavōli yiṭuvatum, aluvalakaṅkaḷil āṭavaruṭaṉ vēli ceyvatum, kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ kimmi (kimpeṇ) vāḻvu vāḻvatum maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷvatum, peṇṭir peṟa vēṇṭiya urimikaḷām.
kulak kaṭṭuppāṭum peṟṟōr taṭiyum mīṟic ciṟanta muṟiyil ulakam pōṟṟumāṟu kūṭi vāḻvatu, maṇamakkaḷ urimiyām.
iṉi, kaṭcit talivarkkuriyatu pēccurimi; eḻuttāḷarukkum nūlāciriyarukkum uriyatu eḻutturimi; oruvaṉ taṉ tāymoḻiyiṟ pēca iṭamiruppatu moḻiyurimi; oru vakuppār oruvaki uṇaviyuṇṇac caṭṭat taṭiyillātiruppatu uṇavurimi.
iṉi, tamiḻ tiraviṭattiṟkut tāyum āriyattiṟku mūlamumāṉa ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyātalāl aṉittintiya āṭci moḻiyākat tamiḻum amivatu tamiḻariṉ moḻiyurimiyām.
4. potunalavurimi
taṉi vāḻkkiyil oruvaṉ evvaḷavum īṭṭalām; eṅkuñ cella lām. āyiṉ, kūṭṭu vāḻkkiyil, potunalampaṟṟip paṟpala kaṭṭup pāṭukaḷum varampīṭukaḷum vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi taṉivāḻkkiyil urimit taṭiyēṉum, kūṭṭu vāḻkkiyil urimiyēyām.
iru piḷḷikaṭkumēṟ peṟakkūṭāteṉpatu piḷḷi varampīṭu; patiṉintu ceykaṭkumēl nilam vittirukkak kūṭāteṉpatu, celva varampīṭṭil oruvakiyāṉa nilavarampīṭu. iviyellām, oru mānakariṟ pakal vēḷiyil pōkkuvarattu mikka iṭattil, oruvaṉ oru cāliyiṉ kuṟukkē cellak kūṭāteṉpatu pōṉṟaviyē.
5. matavurimi
ulakiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷip paṟṟiyum karuttu vēṟu pāṭirukkumāṟu, makkaḷ maṉappāṉmi paltiṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ākavē, kāṇappaṭāta kaṭavuḷip paṟṟiyum maṟumiyip paṟṟiyum karuttu vēṟu pāṭṭiṟku ettuṇi yiṭamuṇṭeṉpatic collavē vēṇṭuva tilli.
matanampikkiyellām ēraṇa muṟikkum aṟiviyal meyppiṟ kum aṭaṅkātu, appāṟpaṭṭu, ōraḷavu kuruṭṭuttaṉamākavō kuraṅkup piṭiyākavō iruppiṉum, uyirvāḻviyum poruṭpaṭuttāta aḷaviṟku makkaḷ maṉatti iṟukap piṇikkum ceytikaḷuḷ taliciṟantatu matamātalāl, paṇpāṭu mikka ceṅkōlaracellām, tam kuṭikaṭku matavurimi yaḷittēyuḷḷaṉar.
nam intiyak kuṭiyaracu paṉṉāṭṭuk kūṭṭāṭciyātalāl, matavurimi yaḷittiruppatu mikat takkatē.
iṉi, civamatamum tirumāl matamum teṉṉāṭṭut tūya tamiḻ mataṅkaḷātalāl, iṉimēl tamiḻnāṭṭut tirukkōvilkaḷil, payiṟci peṟṟa palvakit tamiḻ vakuppārē, tamiḻil maṭṭum vaḻipāṭāṟṟutal vēṇṭum. itu tamiḻar matavurimiyākum.
pirāmaṇar veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantēṟikaḷiṉ vaḻiyiṉarātalālum, ciṟupāṉmiyarātalālum, tamiḻiyē tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭiruppa tālum, āriyam tiraviṭattiṉ tiripātalālum, āriya mantiraṅkaḷellām tamiḻar aṟiyāta ciṟuteyvak kolivēḷvi vaḻipāṭu paṟṟiyaṉavāta lālum, camaṟkirutam vētamoḻiyum tamiḻuṅ kalanta ilakkiya moḻi yātalālum, āriyamoḻi tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum pirāmaṇar nilattēva reṉṟum ēmāṟṟappaṭṭataṉālēyē, iṭikkālat tamiḻar pirāmaṇar āriya mantiraṅkaḷik koṇṭu tirukkōvil vaḻipāṭu ceyya iṭantantaṉarātalālum, av vēmāṟṟu iṉṉun toṭara immiyun takutiyilli eṉa aṟika.
pirāmaṇar tamiḻukku uṇmiyāyiruntu tamiḻilēyē vaḻipāṭu naṭattiṉ, avar pūcit toḻilit toṭarntu ceyyalām. itukāṟum uyartiṇiyāṉa makkaḷiṉ palvēṟu urimikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉi, vāyillā ēḻi aḵṟiṇi uyiriṉaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ urimiyi nām namatupōṟ pēṇa vēṇṭāvā? atiyum pēṇā viṭiṉ, nām uyartiṇiyeṉṟu tarukkuvatu eṅṅaṉ takum?
māṭukaḷum, kutirikaḷum, ēruḻavum nīriṟikkavum vaṇṭiyiḻukka vum, iravum pakalum vaṟpuṟuttappaṭṭu, pōtiya vuṇaviṉṟiyum muraṭṭut taṉamāka aṭiyuṇṭum kuttuṇṭum, puṇmēṟ puṇpaṭṭum ettuṇit tuṉpappaṭukiṉṟaṉa! avi eṅṅaṉ yāriṭam muṟiyiṭum? eṅṅaṉam mānāṭu kūṭum? eṅṅaṉam kūṭṭaṇi cērum? eṅṅaṉam vēli niṟuttañ ceyyum? uṇṇānōṉpiṉālum vaṉceyalālum tam tollikaḷi yum tuṉpaṅkaḷiyum terivikkavō tīrttuk koḷḷavō immiyum avaṟṟiṟku iṭamilliyē!
makkaḷiṭattu aṉpu ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟum, maṟṟa vuyirkaḷiṭattu aruḷpūṇṭoḻuka vēṇṭumeṉṟum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa paṉuvalkaḷum nammiṭattuḷḷaṉa. iruntum eṉ payaṉ?
amerikkarum mēṉāṭṭārum nampōla naṭikkāviṭiṉum, poṟiviṉip puraṭciyi uṇṭupaṇṇi ellā viṉikaḷiyum cūḻcciyat tuṇikoṇṭē ceytuvaruvatāl, kutiriyiṉamum kaḻutiyiṉamum muṟṟum viṭutali peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. māṭṭiṉam uṇaviṟkaṉṟi vēṟev vakiyiluṅ kollappaṭuvatilli.
nāmum mēliyarpōṟ poṟiviṉip puraṭciyi yuṇṭākki, mēṟkūṟiya vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷiṉ urimiyik kāppōmāka.
- ``urimi vēṭki'' poṅkal malar 1973

-----
moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar 
vāḻkkic cuvaṭukaḷ
ti.pi. 1933 (1902)	:	tirunelvēli māvaṭṭam, caṅkaranayiṉār kōyilil cuṟavam - 26ām nāḷ (7.2.1902) piṟantār. 
tanti	:	ñāṉamuttu
tāy	:	paripūraṇam
ti.pi. 1938 (1907)	:	vaṭārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam, āmpūr micuri
toṭakkakkalvi	:	nallañcal uluttariṉ viṭiyūḻiya naṭunilip paḷḷi.
uyarnilikkalvi:	tirunelvēli māvaṭṭam, pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi, tiruccapi viṭiyūḻiya uyarnilip paḷḷi (9,10,11 - vakuppu)
ti.pi. 1950-52(1919-21)	:	mukavi māvaṭṭam, tiruvilliputtūr vaṭṭam, cīyōṉ maliyiluḷḷa cīyōṉ naṭunilip paḷḷiyilmutaṟpaṭiva āciriyap paṇiyiṟ cērntār.
ti.pi. 1952-53(1921-22)	:	vaṭārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam, āmpūr naṭunilip paḷḷiyil (tām payiṉṟa paḷḷi) utavi āciriyarāyp paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
ti.pi. 1955 (1924)	:	matippu mikkatākak karutappaṭṭa maturit tamiḻc caṅkamnaṭattiya paṇṭitat tērvil veṟṟi peṟṟār.(ï› vāṇṭil pāvāṇarit tavira vēṟoruvarumveṟṟi peṟavilli).
		utavittamiḻāciriyar, ceṉṉi, tiruvallik kēṇik kellaṟṟu uyarnilip paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
ti.pi. 1956 (1925)	:	tamiḻāciriyar, ceṉṉik kiṟittavak kallūri uyarnilip paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
		ciṟuvar pāṭal tiraṭṭu nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1957 (1926)	:	tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiyat tamiḻc caṅkam naṭattiya taṉittamiḻp pulavar tērvil ivar oruvarē veṟṟi peṟṟār eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
ti.pi. 1959 (1928)	:	talimit tamiḻāciriyar, maṉṉārkuṭi piṉlē kallūri uyarnilip paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
		mutal maṉivi ecuttar ammiyār maṟivu.
ti.pi. 1961 (1930)	:	nēcamaṇi ammiyāri maṇantār.
ti.pi. 1963 (1932)			kiṟittavak kīrttaṉam - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1965 (1934)	:	talimit tamiḻāciriyar, picappu īpar uyarnilip paḷḷi, puttūr,  tiruccirāppaḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟi ṉār.
		kīḻikkalit (B.O.L.) tērvil veṟṟi peṟṟār.
ti.pi. 1968 (1937)	:	mutal inti etirppup pōri miyamākak koṇṭu - centamiḻkkāñci nūl veḷi yīṭu. inti etirppuk kiḻami koṇṭāṭiṉār.
		``kaṭṭurik kacaṭaṟi eṉṉum viyāca viḷakkam''nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1971 (1940)	:	oppiyaṉ moḻinūl mutaṟpākam
		iyaṟṟamiḻilakkaṇamākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.
		kaṭṭuri variviyal eṉṉum urinaṭi          ilakkaṇa nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1972 (1941)	:	``tamiḻar carittirac curukkam''  veḷiyīṭu : tamiḻaka iḷiñar maṉṟam, tiruccirāppaḷḷi.
		``tamiḻaṉ eppaṭik keṭṭāṉ'' ākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭal.
ti.pi. 1974 (1943)	:	talimit tamiḻāciriyar, ceṉṉi muttiyālup pēṭṭi uyarnilip paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
	:	cuṭṭu viḷakkam - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
		- paṇṭitamaṇi katirēcaṉār talimiyil naṭi peṟṟa mutalām tamiḻ uṇarcci mānāṭṭil paṅkēṟṟu uriyāṟṟiṉār.
ti.pi. 1975 (1944)	:	- tamiḻttuṟit talivarākac cēlam nakarāṇmik kallūriyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
		- tiraviṭattāy - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1980 (1949)	:	collārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷ - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
		pāvāṇarkkut tavattiru maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār cāṉṟitaḻ vaḻaṅkic ciṟappittār.
		pāvāṇar avarkaḷ cēlam kallūriyil paṇi yāṟṟiyapōtu teṉmoḻi āciriyar peruñcittira ṉār avarkaḷ akkallūriyil payiṉṟār eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
ti.pi. 1981 (1950)	:	uyartarak kaṭṭuri ilakkaṇam (mu.pā.) - nūl veḷiyīṭu.

ti.pi. 1983 (1952)	:	ceṉṉippalkalikkaḻakattil kalimutuvar (M.A.) paṭṭam peṟṟār.
		paḻantamiḻāṭci - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1984 (1953)	:	mutaṟṟāymoḻi allatu tamiḻākka viḷakkam - nūl veḷiyīṭu.

		aṇṇāmali palkalikkaḻakattil virivuri yāḷarākap paṇiyiṟ cērntār.
ti.pi. 1988 (1957)	:	periyār ī.vē.rā. talimiyil cēlam tamiḻp pēravi ivariṉ toṇṭip pārāṭṭit `tirāviṭa moḻinūl ñāyiṟu' eṉum paṭṭam vaḻaṅkic ciṟappittatu.
		ticampar 27, 28, 29-l tilliyil naṭipeṟṟa aṉit tintiyak kīḻikkali mānāṭṭil paṅkēṟṟuc ciṟappittār.
ti.pi.1990 (1959)	:	tamiḻnāṭṭu araciṉ āṭcit tuṟiyil kalic col lākkattokuppil paṅkēṟṟuc ciṟappittamik kākat tamiḻnāṭṭaracu cārpil tamiḻnāṭṭu āḷunarāl  avarukkuc ceppup paṭṭayam vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
		moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar avarkaḷāl peyar cūṭṭappaṭṭa `teṉmoḻi' itaḻ pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār avarkaḷāl toṭaṅkap peṟṟatu. taṉittamiḻiyakka vaḷarccikku iv vitaḻ iṉṟaḷavum perum paṅkāṟṟi varukiṟatu.
ti.pi. 1992 (1961)	:	ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyiṉ cīrkēṭu - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
		- aṇṇāmali palkalikkaḻakattil ēṟpaṭṭa cikkalāl eṉakku vaṟumiyum uṇṭu, maṉiviyum, makkaḷum uṇṭu - atōṭu eṉakku māṉamum uṇṭu - eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭup palkalikkaḻakap paṇiyiliruntu veḷiyēṟiṉār. eṉṉōṭu tamiḻum veḷiyēṟiyatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
ti.pi. 1994 (1963)	:	tuṇiviyār nēcamaṇi ammiyār maṟivu.
ti.pi. 1995 (1964)	:	muṉivar ci.ilakkuvaṉār talimiyilāṉa maturit tamiḻk kāppuk kaḻakam - tamiḻp peruṅ kāvalar eṉṉum paṭṭam vaḻaṅkic ciṟappittatu.
		``eṉ aṇṇāmalinakar vāḻkki'' eṉṉum kaṭṭurit toṭar teṉmoḻiyil veḷivantatu.

		``The Primary Classical Language of the World'' eṉṉum nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1998 (1967)	:	tamiḻ varalāṟu 
		vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu
		“The Language Problem of Tamilnadu and Its Logical Solution” ākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1999 (1968)	:	maturit tamiḻ eḻuttāḷar maṉṟam ivariṉ maṇiviḻāvik koṇṭāṭi moḻinūl mūtaṟiñar eṉum paṭṭam vaḻaṅkic ciṟappittatu.
		06-10-1968-l ivarit talivarākak koṇṭu ulakat tamiḻk kaḻakam tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭatu.
		intiyāl tamiḻ evvāṟu keṭum?
		vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūliṉ vaḻuviyal - 
		``Is Hindi the logical solution of India''
		ākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 2000 (1969)	:	paṟampukkuṭiyil ulakat tamiḻk kaḻaka mutal mānāṭu. im mānāṭṭil tavattiru kuṉṟakkuṭi aṭikaḷār muṉivar ci. ilakkuvaṉār, muṉivar va. cupa. māṇikkaṉār, pulavar kuḻanti uḷḷiṭṭa tamiḻc cāṉṟōr paṅkēṟṟuc ciṟappittami kuṟippiṭat takkatu.
		im mānāṭṭil - tirukkuṟaḷ tamiḻ marapuri
		iciyaraṅku iṉṉicik kōvi
		“jÄœ fl‹bfh©L jiH¡Fkh?'' - ākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 2002 (1971)	:	paṟampumaliyiltavattiru kuṉṟakkuṭi aṭikaḷār talimiyil naṭipeṟṟapāri viḻāvilpāvāṇar centamiḻñāyiṟu eṉṟu pārāṭṭic ciṟappikkap peṟṟār.
		pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷāl teṉ moḻiyil aṟivikkappaṭṭa `centamiḻc coṟ piṟappiyal akaramutalit tiṭṭam' vakukkap peṟṟatu.
ti.pi. 2003 (1972)	:	tañciyil ivar talimiyil ulakat tamiḻkkaḻaka mānāṭu - tamiḻaṉ piṟantakat tīrmāṉippu mānāṭāka naṭantatu.
		- tamiḻar varalāṟu, tamiḻar matam ākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 2004 (1973)	:	 vērccoṟ kaṭṭurikaḷ nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 2005 (1974)	:	8.5.1974-l centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akara mutalit tiṭṭa iyakkunarākat tamiḻnāṭṭaracāl aṉṟiya mutalvar kaliñar mu.karuṇāniti avarkaḷāl amarttappaṭṭār.
		centamiḻc coṟ piṟappiyal pērakara mutaliyiṉ mutal maṭala mutaṟ pakuti avariṉ maṟiviṟkup piṟaku 1985-l veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu.
ti.pi. 2009 (1978)	:	maṇṇil viṇ allatu vaḷḷuvar kūṭṭuṭimi - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
		ceṉṉiyil ulakat tamiḻk kaḻaka nāṉkāvatu mānāṭu pāvāṇar muṉṉiliyil naṭipeṟṟatu. mānāṭṭiṟkup patippāciriyar pulavar a.nakkīraṉār talimi tāṅkiṉār.
ti.pi. 2010 (1979)	:	tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
		vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatiyum tamiḻ vaḷarccikkum, tamiḻ ārāyccikkum otukkiya ip perumakaṉukkut tamiḻaka mutalvar puraṭcit talivar ṭākṭar em.ji.ār avarkaḷāl `centamiḻc celvar' eṉṉum cīriya virutu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
ti.pi. 2011 (1980)	:	`Lemurian Language and Its Ramifications - An Epitome' eṉum āṅkila nūl maturiyil naṭi peṟavirunta aintām ulakattamiḻ mānāṭṭu ayal nāṭṭup pērāḷarkaḷukkāka (52 pakka aḷavil) uruvākkappaṭṭatu.
ti.pi. 2012 (1981)	:	maturiyil naṭipeṟṟa aintām ulakattamiḻ mānāṭṭil paṅkēṟṟu māntaṉ tōṟṟamum, tamiḻar marapum eṉṉum talippil tamiḻartam varalāṟṟup perumiyi nilināṭṭip pēciṉār.
		cuṟavam 2ām nāḷ caṉavari 15-l iravu 12.30-kku ivvulaka vāḻviliruntu maṟintār.
tāymoḻippaṟṟē taliyāyappaṟṟu
tāymoḻip paṟṟillāt taṉṉāṭṭup paṟṟē
	taṉṉiṉaṅ kollavē tāṉkoṇṭa puṟṟē

moḻiyoṉ ṟillāmalē iṉamoṉṟu milli
	iṉamoṉṟil lāmalē nāṭoṉṟu milli!

matiyuṇar viṉṟiyē maṭiveṉun tūkkam
	mayaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟāy maṟutteḻu tamiḻā!

karantum aṇṭivīṭṭuḷ kālvitta liṉṟik
	kaṇmūṭit tūṅkuvāy kaṭiteḻu tamiḻā!

varumāṉaṅ kuṉṟiyē vaṟiyava ṉāṉāy
	vāḻnāḷ vīṇākāmal valleḻu tamiḻā!

arucuvi yuṇṭiyē ākkiṭiṉ uṉki
	arunta maṟukkiṉṟār āynteḻu tamiḻā!

oliyoṭu variyumpiṉ oḻiyavē aṇmi
	uṟumtēva nākari uṇarnteḻu tamiḻā!

pārmutal paṇpāṭu payiṉṟavaṉ tamiḻaṉ
	palariyum uṟaveṉap pakarntavaṉ tamiḻaṉ.

piṟappālē ciṟappilli tamiḻā - itip
	perunāva laṉcoṉṉāṉ tamiḻā!

maṟattālum tiṟattālum tamiḻā - mēṉmi
	matiyaṟi voḻukkattāl tamiḻā!

tamiḻiṉuk kulakiṉil takuvatē talimi
	tamiḻarum aṭiyavē tāḻvilā nilimi
imiḻtaru moḻiyiyal eytuka nalamē
	ematumey varalāṟē eḻukavē valamē.

vēṉiliṟ kāṉmali vemmikoḷ pāliyiṉ
	viḷinila māvatin nāṭu

miṉṉumpal maṇikaḷum mikuvili yāṟpeṟa
	mēlulakum virumpum nāṭu

koṉṟutaṉ makaṉiyē koṭumiyi nimirttucceṅ
	kōṉmuṟi kulaviya nāṭu

nīliyiṉ kaṇavaṟku nikaḻttiya vāymoḻi
	niṟivēṟṟiṉa vēḷāḷar nāṭu

aṟiyātu muracaṇi ayarntiṭum pulavarkkum
	ālavaṭṭam viciṟum nāṭu

cittariṉ maruttuvam ciṟantapoṉ ṉākkamum
	cilampoṭu tikaḻntatin nāṭu
pāvāṇar poṉmoḻikaḷ
māntaṉeṉak kumarimali maruviyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
māṇpuṭiya nākarikam malarntavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
moḻivaḷarcci mutaṉmutalāy muṟṟiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
mōṉiyuṭaṉ ciṟantaceyyuḷ pēciyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
tuṟinakarāl kaṭalvaṇikam tōṟṟiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
piṟanilattu vaṇikariyum pēṇiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
toṉmiyoṭu muṉmi; toṉmiyoṭu naṉmi;
tāymiyoṭu tūymi; taḻuviḷami vaḷami.
pakuttaṟivē māṉamuṭaṉ paṭittavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
pakuttaṟivāl tiṇivakutta paṇpuṭiyāṉ tamiḻaṉē!
paṉimaliyi mutaṉmutaṟkip paṟṟiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
palamuṟimīṉ pulivilatiṟ patittavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
palakaliyum palanūlum payiṟṟiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
palapoṟiyum matilaraṇiṟ patittavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
irutiṇikkum īntuvakkum iṉpamuṟṟāṉ tamiḻaṉē!
ītalici yāviṭattē iṟantavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
kaṭalnaṭuvē kalañceluttik karikaṇṭavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
kalappaṭiyāl kuṇattīvik kāttavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
pakuttaṟivic caṟṟum payaṉpaṭuttār kalvi
mikuttataṉāl uṇṭō payaṉ?
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ.
tamiḻā uṉṟaṉ muṉṉavaṉē
taliyāy vāḻnta teṉṉavaṉē
amiḻtām māri aṉṉavaṉē
aḻakāy mutaṉūl coṉṉavaṉē.
paḵṟuḷi nāṭṭiṟ piṟantavaṉām
pakuttaṟivup paṇpiṟ ciṟantavaṉām
pakiyām maliyi uṟaḻntavaṉām
pālum puliyiṟ kaṟantavaṉām.
aṉpeṉpatu ēcuvum puttarumpōl ellāriṭattum kāṭṭum nēyam.
āṭci oppumi naṭpuṟaviṟkē aṉṟi aṭimittaṉattiṟku ētuvākātu.
ōyvakaviyit tīrmāṉikkum aḷaviyāy irukka vēṇṭiyatu paṇit tiṟamiyēyaṉṟi akavi varampaṉṟu.
tuṟavu tammāliyaṉṟavari potumakkaṭkut toṇṭu ceyvatu ciṟanta tuṟavākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
intiyāvil vilaṅkiṉam irukkum nilimiyi uṇartta avaṟṟiṉ kaḻuttuppuṇṇum vilāvelumput tōṟṟamum pōtum.
kaṭṭuppāṭṭillāviṭiṉ kāvalaṉuṅ kāvāṉātalālum ceṅkōlāṭciyoṭu kūṭiya irukaṭciyaracē kuṭiyaraciṟ kēṟṟatām.
piṟamoḻi pēcum ciṟupāṉmiyar piḷḷikaḷum avvan nāṭṭup perumpāṉmi moḻiyiyē kaṟṟal vēṇṭum.... 
kāl moḻivāri mānilap pirivu eṉpatu poruḷaṟṟatum payaṉaṟṟatumām.
māntaṉ vāḻkki, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam eṉa iruvakittu. maṉiviyōṭu kūṭi illattiliruntu ataṟkuriya aṟañ ceytu vāḻum vāḻkki illaṟam; ulakap paṟṟit tuṟantu ataṟkuriya aṟattōṭu kūṭik kāṭṭil tavañ ceytu vāḻum vāḻkki tuṟavaṟam.
oruvaṉ illattil iruntu maṉiyāḷōṭu kūṭivāḻiṉum aṟañ ceyyātu iruppiṉ avaṉ vāḻkki illaṟamākātu veṟumaṉāṉa ilvāḻkkiyām.
ilaṅkiyil iṭarppaṭum makkaḷ perumpālum tamiḻarāyiruttaliṉ avarkaḷiṉ urimiyip pēṇikkāttaṟku aṅkuḷḷa intiyat tūtāṇmik kuḻuttalivar tamiḻarākavē iruttal vēṇṭum.
karuttuvēṟupāṭṭiṟ kiṭantantu oru cārāri orucārār paḻikkātum pakikkātum iruppatē uṇmiyāṉa pakuttaṟivām.
iv vulakil tamiḻaṉip pōl muṉpu uyarntavaṉu milli; piṉpu tāḻntavaṉum illi.
iṟṟit tamiḻaruṭ perumpālārum tammit tāmē tāḻttuvatilum, iṉattārip pakittup pakivari vāḻavippatilum, pakivar    maṉaṅkuḷirat tam muṉṉōrip paḻippatilum, tammarumit tāymoḻiyip puṟakkaṇittup pakivariṉ aricceyaṟkik kalavi moḻiyip pōṟṟuvatilum oppuyarvaṟṟavarāy uḻalkiṉṟaṉar.
tantiyum aracum
oru kuṭumpattiṟ piṟanta piḷḷikaṭkellām ūṇuṭi yuṟiyuḷ aḷikkat tanti kaṭamippaṭṭiruppatu pōṉṟē, oru nāṭṭiṟ piṟanta kuṭikaṭkellām vēliyum pātukāppum aḷikka aracu kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷatu.
kōvil vaḻipāṭum iruvakic caṭaṅkukaḷum tamiḻil naṭipeṟṟāloḻiya, tamiḻ viṭutaliyaṭintu taṉ paḻamperumiyi mīḷap peṟa muṭiyātu. tamiḻuyarntāltāṉ tamiḻaṉ uyarvāṉ.
antaṇaṉ aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ mutaṉmutal tamiḻakat tuṟaviyariyē kuṟittatu pōṉṟu pārppāṉ eṉṉum peyarum mutaṉmutal tamiḻap pūcāriyiyē kuṟittatu.
tamiḻaṉ eṉṉum iṉam tamiḻpaṟṟiyatē yātalāl. tamiḻ tōṉṟiya iṭamē tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamām. atu teṉvāriyil mūḻkip pōṉa kumarināṭē.
2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta mukamatiyar ciṟupāṉmiyarēṉum āṅkilar nīṅkiyavuṭaṉ tamakkeṉak kōṉmi (Soveriegnity) koṇṭa taṉināṭu peṟṟuviṭṭaṉarē. nūṟāyiram āṇṭiṟku muṉṉamē tōṉṟi orukāl nāvalantēyam muḻutum āṇṭa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa tamiḻar ēṉ tam nāṭṭiyum peṟavilli?..... itaṟkuk karaṇiyam, mukamatiyarkkuḷḷa ōriṉa vuṇarcciyum oṟṟumiyum maṟamum māṉamum iṟṟit tamiḻaruṭ perumpālārkku eḷḷaḷavum iṉmiyēyām.
ellāk kēṭukaḷum pāṭukaḷum tuṉpaṅkaḷum tollikaḷum nīṅki ellārum iṉpamāka vāḻavēṇṭu meṉṉum iṉṉaruḷ nōkkam koṇṭē teḷḷiya maṉamum oḷḷiya aṟivum tiṇṇiya neñcum nuṇṇiya matiyum koṇṭa tiruvaḷḷuvar tirukkuṟaḷi iyaṟṟiṉār.
paṇanāṭṭamiṉṟi, ellārum tattamakkiyaṉṟa paṇiyic ceytu, ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum otta urimiyōṭu payaṉpaṭutti oṟṟumiyāka oruṅkē vāḻum kuṭumpa allatu kūṭṭuk kuṭumpa vāḻkkiyē potuvuṭimi yākum.
oru kuṭumpattiṟ piṟanta ellārkkum eṅṅaṉam tiṟamikkut takka paṇiyum tēvikkut takka nukarcciyum uṇṭō, aṅṅaṉamē oru nāṭṭiṟ piṟanta ellārkkum iruttal vēṇṭum. ituvē pāttuṇṭal eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar kūṭṭuṭimi.
